## 2026-09-14 10:15 - 10:37 (`EVT-000957`-`EVT-000958`) — Kane's Raincheck, and the Libeň Door Reopened

**Lunch declined, rescheduled without friction.** Kane offered lunch at `OBJ-64`'s cleared site; Alexander named a proper restaurant instead, not that day, citing his own business. Kane took the raincheck without pressing. **Owen texted first, unprompted** — a charger left behind, no reopening of the two nights' silence since their fight; Alexander answered he'd collect it later in the week. **Tereza's four-way Prague meeting got its venue** — a café off Wenceslas Square, Thursday 2026-09-17.

**The far door reopened, fifth visit, a different purpose.** Anchored step to the Denver facility (5 Mana), the door opened there (2,500 Mana), Sprint-crossed to Sevrin's ground rather than an anchored step — no anchor held there until this span. **A thirty-second anchor set** on arrival; future visits cost the flat rate. Sevrin delivered the answer he'd been asked for on the Libeň object (`ENT-000272`): the cut anchor-point still opens, but only inward — nothing can reach Alexander's side through it, and a crossing taken the other way cannot be reversed by any means either of them holds, corrected in-fiction from an initial misstatement of the direction. Far-side notice **55 -> 57**.

**Alexander asked Sevrin to open it the rest of the way and let him step through.** Deferred to an owner authoring pass rather than improvised — what's genuinely past an unauthored far-side crossing belongs to the player, not the Runtime — landing as `worlds/gatefall/270_THE_FAR_SIDE.md` Section 4.5, a new fourth state (**the Passed**) beyond Standing/Continued/Spent, and its first named figure. Sevrin agreed to close the entrance behind him once through (Seamseal, scene-length, so nothing else uses it while he's gone) and to check for his return at his own unpredictable intervals — no signal can cross a sealed exit, so there is no other way to know. **Undecided as of this checkpoint.** Mana 6,272 -> 3,538 across the visit's travel and drain; Health untouched.

## 2026-09-13 21:55 - 2026-09-14 10:15 (`EVT-000950`-`EVT-000956`) — Two A-Rank Gates, a reshaped Standfast, and a line of Kane's own

**Two A-Rank directed assignments, cleared a day apart** (`EVT-000950`, `EVT-000953`). `OBJ-62` solo — Ashfield archetype, 14 commons, 1 elite, 1 boss, two clean Rupture strikes, no damage. `OBJ-64` the next morning, Kane's first Gate cleared unconcealed and on his own hand — Frozen Gallery archetype, ten commons, two elites, a boss, credited to Alexander under Section 3.8's Kingmaker precedent since it was his assignment. Combined: Level 185 -> 186, both hauls filed through Renata Okonkwo-Vance at the licensed-market rate, **$12,050,000 + $14,425,000 = $26,475,000** into cash.

**Standfast stops being purely defensive** (`EVT-000952`). Two sessions of trying and failing to give Kane's signature ability an offensive branch ended with Alexander's own Flux Sight — legendary, The Unmeasured active — confirming the failure was categorical: Profile Section 13.6's general rule that a signature grows only in magnitude with Rank, never in kind, meant there was nothing to unlock by trying harder. Rather than accept that, Alexander aimed **Overpower** directly at Kane's own live field, structural-break mode repurposed toward reshaping rather than destroying it — a genuinely unprecedented working, priced at 400 Mana and a real risk of breaking the field outright. It landed clean. **Standfast now redirects, natively Kane's own** — the first known exception to a general signature-fixity rule in this world, recorded provisional pending an owner ruling.

**Kane gets a line of his own** (`EVT-000955`). Rather than simply hand over `OBJ-64`, Alexander used the offer as leverage — a call to Renata, Kane on speaker, asking BGM National to open a standing arrangement with him directly instead of routing everything through Alexander's own agreement. She agreed it was overdue rather than a favor; terms land tomorrow. **`OBJ-58` also escalated** (`EVT-000954`) — BGM's internal review has formally connected Alexander's card to the Jiu Valley platform-access log alongside Sorcha Bellweather's, live and moving rather than sitting inert.

**World state:** the 2026-09-14 06:00 tick ran; `quiet_days` crossed 2, forcing a guaranteed on-route audition (unresolved, unpursued); the wider map rolled for the first time this campaign — an S-Rank Gate opened in St. Louis, national reach came back NOT THIS ONE. Daily Premium rotated live (Ivory series). Escalation Tier VII holds, credit unchanged at 8.

## 2026-09-12 09:45 - 2026-09-13 21:15 (`EVT-000925`-`EVT-000949`) — A house made livable, two doors, a Gate in Brno, and thirteen levels

**The Winnetka house furnished in a single afternoon** (`EVT-000925`). Thornquist Bespoke Interiors (`ENT-000271`) minted. The standard 10-12 week bespoke lead was refused outright and converted to in-stock sourcing across three vendors with rush freight and an overtime multi-crew install — **$748,000 against the original $420,000 quote**, the premium being entirely the cost of not waiting. Master bedroom, a standing guest room, a third bedroom converted to a home gym, the study, and the room adjoining it converted to a library in massive-oak modular shelving (built-in millwork being genuinely impossible same-day). Two bedrooms left empty. **Rift anchor thirty-one set** the same morning (`EVT-000926`), free and uncontested.

**The far door, twice in two days.** The fourth opening (`EVT-000927`) ran four hours with all four teachers at once and ratified six candidates — Reading the Room, Slip (Ilith), Appraise, Trace (Tolo), The Bridge Stance (Sevrin), Read the Learner (Aumry). **Orenne appeared, watched, and left without a word**, which is precisely what her `Let it finish` position predicts; it satisfies the notice-36/42 thresholds without an invented encounter. The fifth opening (`EVT-000938`-`EVT-000943`) ratified seven more across Tolo, Sevrin, Ilith and Aumry. **Every candidate this teaching line ever opened is now closed — fifteen opened across five visits, fifteen ratified.** Both round trips cost a Greater Mana Potion at the door (44 -> 42).

**Elias Kane, two evenings** (`EVT-000948`, `EVT-000930`, `EVT-000932`, `EVT-000933`, `EVT-000947`). Called for a beer the day after the Owen argument — the first time Alexander has reached for a friend rather than an asset. Across the two nights he disclosed the money's real origin and the black-market cages behind it; **confirmed that he is the one who ended the Jiu Valley Exclusion**; and named Owen's existence for the first time. Kane's own question — *was I wanted, or was I what was in front of you* — was answered honestly rather than flatteringly, and the terms settled as friendship with the physical part named rather than hidden. `REL-000107` carries real disclosure in both directions now.

**Jiu Valley stops being background** (`EVT-000930`, `EVT-000931`). Day three of global saturation coverage — reinsurance up ~15% across three continents, a CGA spokesperson saying nothing at length, guild channels full of theories none of which is *a hunter*. A **`standing_world_reactions` table** was opened in `110_WORLD_LEDGER.md`, mandatory-check every tick alongside district pressure and escalation, so a world event of real scale advances its own stage instead of going inert. Raised to the engine layer as **`F-067`**.

**Prague, and what Beran paid with** (`EVT-000934`-`EVT-000937`). Director Beran routed an S-Rank Gate outside Brno to a foreign hunter rather than call his own national S-Rank, and said so to Alexander's face when asked: *"you are, forgive me, expendable to my country in a way she is not."* **€2.1M in fees declined.** In its place: `ENT-000272`, **the Libeň object** — recovered whole from the centre of the Libeň Scar eleven years ago, resistant to every appraisal since. Alexander's own read returned what none of them had: **Flux Sight resolves no Rank at all**, the same flat absence as far-side matter. The Brno Gate cleared solo — Hive archetype, 22 commons and an S-Rank boss, two Rupture strikes, no damage taken; 310 crystals and a core filed to auction on Beran's CGA certificate, ≈$125,000,000 pending.

**Tolo named it; Sevrin priced it** (`EVT-000938`-`EVT-000939`). A legendary Appraise identified **seam-stone** — a fragment of a door's own anchor-point, *cut* rather than opened. Something once made a door from the far side to Earth and it was severed while still held open, older than anything in a four-century archive. Sevrin's read: the cut cost at least one life. He has it in custody on the far side, with his own interest in it disclosed rather than concealed, and a warning accepted in good part.

**Tereza told** (`EVT-000938`). Alexander broke Beran's requested discretion to tell her she had been managed around. Three years of being too valuable to use, and her own director quietly routing S-Rank work past her — she took it cold, thanked him for the cost of telling her, and is pressing Reinhardt and Aubrac today rather than calling Beran.

**Ten keys, thirteen levels, two assessments nobody had run** (`EVT-000944`-`EVT-000945`). Ten S-Rank instant dungeons bought and cleared solo: 121 commons, 16 elites, 10 bosses, no genuine contest anywhere in them. **+225,000 XP, Level 172 -> 185.** The crossing surfaced a real gap — **level 170 had been passed on 2026-09-09 without its Title Assessment ever firing.** Both owed assessments settled in level order: 170 retroactive (zero titles, read against the record as it stood before Jiu Valley), and 180 granting **Grounded [Rare, +5]**, whose outside-any-Gate criterion the Jiu Valley kill satisfies exactly. Earned but dormant — all eight slots full at the level-100 cap. **140 points allocated: +100 Intelligence, +40 Vitality**, max Health 11,380 -> 11,592, max Mana 6,044 -> 6,270.

**A library filled** (`EVT-000946`). A nine-anchor circuit — Chicago, Boston, New York, DC, Miami, Houston, Dallas, Seattle, San Francisco — history, Gate literature, astrology, spaceflight, physics, mathematics, fiction, and two genuine rarities. ≈$340,000. Kane's *"ask me again once there are books in it"* no longer applies.

**Corrections this span, all player-caught.** A staged furniture delivery narrated as agreed when it never was, re-narrated as a same-day rush order; **an anchored step home authored twice before the player had declared it**, rolled back both times; a scene-ending sequence (door closed, potion drunk, home) written twice while the player was still mid-visit, rewound both times; and a Runtime line giving Kane knowledge of the BGM/CGA offers he had no channel to — caught before it was spoken and written into Closed Channels. A note on Kane's own voice drifting toward Owen's register was raised and taken.

**Repository work at the barrier.** The protagonist object had grown past its per-object read ratchet with three declared operations over budget; duplicated event history was trimmed out of `100_CHARACTER_SHEET.md` — the temporal-state log, the rift-step use chain, the daily-premium block, several inventory lines — rather than trimming live state, and `/system shop`'s baseline re-recorded with justification once the remainder was genuine holdings. A malformed YAML block in `worlds/gatefall/220_NOTABLE_FIGURES.md` (unescaped quotes inside `ENT-000242`'s knowledge field, introduced this session) was repaired. The daily-premium cycle was found stale past its own 2026-09-12 boundary and marked as owing a live rotation.

**Promotion barrier** (`EVT-000949`). Health closes **11,592/11,592**, Mana **6,270/6,270**, both full. `skills.rift_step.successful_uses` 73 -> 91; `rupture` 166 -> 168; `keen_sense` 152 -> 153; `flux_sight` 147 -> 149; `twin_fang` 157 -> 168. Gold 255,668,381 -> **300,356,081**; cash $376,971,490.22 -> **$375,883,490.22**; pending payouts 0 -> **1**. `far_side_notice` 33 -> 55. `quiet_days` 0 -> 1.

- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000925`-`EVT-000949`; `ENT-000271`, `ENT-000272` minted. New high-water marks **ENT-000272**, **EVT-000949**. Updated live targets: `100`, `110`, `120`, `130`, `140`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`, `system/WORLDS_AND_CAMPAIGNS.md`, plus `worlds/gatefall/220_NOTABLE_FIGURES.md`, `090_CAMPAIGN_STARTUP.md`, `system/RUNTIME_CONTEXT_BUDGETS.yaml`, and `engine/004_DESIGN_FLAGS.md`.

## 2026-09-11, 06:00-11:20 (`EVT-000912`-`EVT-000913`) — The morning tick, Loomis Street's assessor, and Suite 411

**The 06:00 world tick and the 47th daily** (`EVT-000912`). Tier VII holds; escalation credit 1 -> 7 — four stale board postings (`GB-21`, `GB-23`, `GB-26`, `GB-30`) retroactively settled broken, a repair of a prior contest-roll coverage gap rather than four fresh breaks; no tier effect, VII remains the schedule's authored ceiling. Seven Chicago Gates rolled, one within reach and posted as `GB-36`; four Prague Gates, all elsewhere. The 47th daily completes clean — streak 34 -> 35, +4 Ability Points (Ascetic), a duplicate Mend rune banked.

**A missed call, a rescaled story, a real suspicion.** Preston Vaughn (Horizon Guild) calls with a recruitment overture while Alexander is mid-watch and concealed — voicemail, not a conversation, the first of what won't be the only approach now that a solo unaffiliated S-Rank is impossible for the guilds to ignore. The Jiu Valley story is corrected to its true scale — global saturation coverage, the biggest unexplained event in years, not a passing news cycle. And a genuine investigative thread opens: BGM's review team is building a minute-by-minute platform-access timeline, and Sorcha Bellweather is the only logged name on it.

**Loomis Street, direct observation** (`EVT-000912`). Watching the Friday CONSULT assessment concealed, a partial Keen Sense read through the wall gives way to a direct, strong-success vantage at a cracked door: **Ingrid Solberg**, CONSULT's own quality assessor, and a second maker later identified as **Daniel Pruett** — both minted, both read (Flux Sight, Keen Sense, Read the Want). Ingrid's want is genuine but incompletely read; Daniel's is whole — keep whatever's collapsing in his personal life off the bench, afraid the line's already slipping. Daniel resolves **D-Rank, awakened**, a continuity correction after an initial pass wrongly left his Rank unestablished (Flux Sight is automatic; artificer work requires being awakened).

**Suite 411** (`EVT-000912`). A legendary-success tail follows Ingrid on foot to a downtown formation-mill office. A keypad lock defeats first entry — the Unlicensed Lockpick Set is explicitly useless against electronics, and no roll was owed for concealment, which was never in doubt. A courier's door-opening provides the real window. Inside: registered-agent paperwork confirms **Meridian Cold Storage LLC and Cormant Holdings share the same suite** — the physical link `OBJ-21` has needed since August — plus a working ledger page matching CONSULT's own alias/percentage accounting shape. Through the back-room door, partially overheard: a shortfall dispute, and **D named aloud for the first time**, plainly, not signed at the bottom of a text.

**Three corrections this span, all player-caught and resolved by re-narration**: the Vaughn call reframed from answered to missed; the Jiu Valley coverage rescaled to global saturation; two unnecessary rolls (Suite 411 entry concealment, a passive door-wait) retracted as not being contested actions at all, replaced with grounded narrative fact.

**Promotion barrier** (`EVT-000913`). No dangerous scene; no XP, level, or skill-formation movement. Mana closes full, 6,044/6,044. Unspent points 62 -> 66. Two new entities minted (`ENT-000266`, `ENT-000267`), no relationships — neither NPC has met Alexander.

- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000912`-`EVT-000913`; `ENT-000266`, `ENT-000267` minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000913**. Updated live targets: `100`, `110`, `130`, `140`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`, `system/WORLDS_AND_CAMPAIGNS.md`.

## 2026-09-10, 14:00-20:15 (`EVT-000909`-`EVT-000911`) — Payout, Peter Kwiatkowski, and the fenced warehouse's bound entity

**Kesha's call and the news cycle** (`EVT-000909`). Blue Island's auction filing clears unprompted, $118,900,000, cash $113,115,490.22 -> $232,015,490.22; the standing Red Gate filing (≈$138,400,000) is now the sole pending payout. Jiu Valley breaks as unattributed global news within the hour — no entry logged on any instrument, no name surfacing, a UN emergency session called for 18:00 Eastern. A dormant thread reopened: Dario Wexler (`ENT-000265`, minted), a West Coast S-Rank introduced by Renata five days earlier and never followed up, texts first contact; a Chicago meeting is loosely set. **A channel violation, player-caught and closed the same turn**: Kesha named Wade Bishop with no basis in anything she's actually been told; retracted, recorded as a standing Closed Channel.

**The CONSULT building, concealed** (`EVT-000909`). Unwatched + Silent Step + an anchored rift-step to the eleventh anchor; a Keen Sense read through the wall (partial, then a closer legendary-success read) confirms Peter Kwiatkowski's missing satchel and his own flagged status are the same Thursday deadline. Tailed to Ironclad Self Storage; Read the Want (first use ever) finds his actual want (free of CONSULT entirely) and fear (`COMPLETE`d like two makers he half-knew). **First direct contact**, concealed and voice-disguised: protection offered and accepted, the banked satchel (`EVT-000700`) returned to his hand from nothing, a rendezvous anchor set (twenty-ninth, Peter's own storage unit) as a genuine new application of the standing anchor-sense rule. `REL-000114` minted. `OBJ-57` opened.

**The fenced warehouse, revisited** (`EVT-000910`). Three weeks unsuppressed since the containment plant was taken (`EVT-000648`) had measurably accelerated the bound entity's restraint decay — Flux Sight's honest floor: days, not weeks. Read the Want (second use, success with a complication) confirms it as a genuine subject — wants to finish something interrupted, fears remaining suspended indefinitely — and the read registered to it. A legendary-success facility search finds the real filing intake (`corvexlogistics-mgmt.com`) and a Cormant Holdings service invoice, physically confirming the name Elias Ward sold back in August (`EVT-000287`) against this site for the first time; a follow-up search finds four further unaddressed site codes and a technician visit scheduled this Friday. The plant returned to the slab, undoing the acceleration.

**Sorcha Bellweather brought in, off her own channel** (`EVT-000910`). Told directly, comes in person, traces Cormant Holdings to a funding trail her own office wrongly closed eighteen months prior. Advises against killing the bound entity now — unknown failure mode, and premature exposure burns the other four sites; Alexander agrees. She takes Cormant Holdings from here, quietly. **A second channel correction, player-caught**: Sorcha has no channel to the word "anchor" or any System-mechanical detail of Alexander's teleportation, only the practical fact she designed around; closed as a standing Closed Channel. Passenger rift-step home for her (BGM National HQ, DC); anchored step to Dallas, then a chartered flight — first visit to the Denver containment facility, usable since its 2026-09-09 purchase (a stray Runtime claim to the contrary, corrected in scene against `OBJ-49`'s actual terms). Thirtieth anchor set.

**Promotion barrier** (`EVT-000911`). No dangerous scene this span (`gatefall.skill_formation`: none) — every resolved action was investigation, negotiation, or travel. Mana closes at 6,034/6,044; cash at $232,015,490.22; pending payouts at one, ≈$138,400,000. Two new entities/relationships minted (`ENT-000265`, `REL-000113`, `REL-000114`); two genuinely new anchors (twenty-ninth, thirtieth) plus one repaired backfill (twenty-eighth, a pre-existing gap from `EVT-000903`). Two channel violations, both caught by the player and closed the same turn they occurred.

- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000909`-`EVT-000911`; `ENT-000265`, `REL-000113`, `REL-000114` minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000911**. Updated live targets: `100`, `120`, `130`, `140`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`, `system/WORLDS_AND_CAMPAIGNS.md`.

## 2026-09-07 evening into 2026-09-08 (`EVT-000885`-`EVT-000891`) — Gate-7 solo, the far door reopened, five lessons across three visits

**A library for Tolo, and the truth told to Owen** (`EVT-000885`). Alexander told Owen what the far side actually is — a place outside this universe, failed candidates who came before him, worlds destroyed — short of naming the System itself; Owen took it without pressing. Learning Tolo wanted Earth's own record (self-correcting systems, honest first-draft history, accounts of rebuilding after loss), the two of them built him a library on Alexander's own card: **$1,847.63**, a little over a hundred titles, delivered the next morning and held in dimensional inventory. **The 06:00 world tick ran in full**: six Chicago Gates, three Prague, one A-Rank taken by the trade before it ever reached him, `GB-34` posted, `GB-28` survives another day, BGM Region V opens a routine roster/compliance audit (`OBJ-56`, no deadline). The 44th daily issues the same instant.

**The Gate-7 A-Rank Hive clear, solo — and a fabricated commander retracted** (`EVT-000886`). Dispatched through `OBJ-43`'s standing channel with no commander and no strike element, correcting a first narration that invented a fourteen-person formation against the campaign's own established precedent (`EVT-000851`, cleared solo, clean) — the player caught it and it was fixed the same turn. Confirmed A-Rank Hive (15 commons, 0 elites, 1 boss, doubled/converted): Pre-empt's first blade, an opening Rupture one-shotting the boss (≈12,206 against 6,000 Rank Health), Mana Bolt's Split fanning the cluster, Twin Fang mopping the remainder — zero Health lost. **9,100 XP** (15×400 + boss 1,600 + A-Rank milestone 1,500, no underdog bonus), **Level 162->163**, past-100 rung +1 all base Stats/+5 unspent. **The boss-drop table, missed on first pass and rolled retroactively once the player flagged it**: d100 78, potion cache, greater tier, d4 2 → +2 Greater Healing, +1 Greater Mana Potion. 208 A-Rank crystals + 1 core filed to Renata and sold at the licensed market rate: **+$10,525,000.00**, corrected from an initial assumption the loot was being banked rather than sold. The flat fee, severity bonus ($2,500,000, same formula as the 2026-09-06 precedent), and the still-outstanding Indianapolis flat fee all **settled instantly** rather than left pending, per player instruction — cash **$89,340,490.22 -> $103,365,490.22**, pending payouts 2 -> 0. **Ability Points corrected 3->4** on the daily reward — Ascetic's own equipped passive, caught by the player before it was banked wrong. Corwin's re-coring call landed unprompted: the homebrew Mana-storage cell's ceiling raised D->S (net -1 modifier, 🎲 88, effective 68, success band), `OBJ-53` closed.

**The far door reopens — 2,500 Mana — and Tolo's reconciliation completes** (`EVT-000887`). The full library shelved in person; Tolo, visibly moved, discloses his own read of "the curve" unprompted — not a description of the harvesting process but a **limit** on it, with no confirmed authored stop condition. Taught **Diagram Sense** [S-Rank] in return for the gift (1/3). Sevrin explains his objection to Ilith honestly — arithmetic, not hostility — and reads Alexander's own track record as evidence pointing the other way, for now. Opens two Taught-route offers the same visit: **Seamseal** [S-Rank] (1/3, a rough first attempt then a clean second in the same continuous session) and **Stillwater** [A-Rank] (1/3, near-perfect first attempt).

**A player ruling: Sevrin and Orenne corrected he/him** (both full entries, `130_NPCS_AND_FACTIONS.md`; both authored technique names and full profile, `worlds/gatefall/270_THE_FAR_SIDE.md`) — prior promoted chronicle text is unaffected per checkpoint discipline; the correction is prospective only, noted at both files. **Appearances authored for all five Standing figures for the first time** (none had been on record before this span).

**Orenne and Aumry, the Long Shelf** (`EVT-000888`). Orenne refuses to teach — his whole position is that Alexander should not get stronger, and he will not undercut his own argument with his own hands — unmoved by what followed: an escalating, genuinely mutual physical scene (first kiss, further, then sex, unhurried), Orenne's own account being the first time in an unmeasurable span he has let himself want anything. **Owen has no channel to any of this**, noted explicitly on `REL-000110`. Aumry, taken up on his standing unconditional offer, teaches **Grip and Footing** [S-Rank] (1/3) and — asked specifically to fill the one gap in Alexander's own kit — **Second Wind** [A-Rank]; two of three attempts in the same session landed no material success and correctly banked zero evidence, the third (🎲 92) qualifying (1/3). This correction (a failed roll earns no evidence under §7.1 test 4) was caught and fixed in-session before the player had to.

**Ilith at the Landing, three threads open at once** (`EVT-000889`). A second, genuinely distinct qualifying scene for **unremarked movement** (different day, different ground): 2/3. Two new offers opened the same visit, both taught without payment on Ilith's own indulgence: **Read the Want** [S-Rank] (1/3, practiced on Ilith herself) and **Unwatched** [S-Rank] (1/3, crediting only the second of two attempts — the first, 🎲10, correctly banked no evidence). Asked for "everything"; declined past the three categories already offered.

**The door closes, home, and Sorcha's design lands** (`EVT-000890`). Anchored step to the door, 2,500 Mana to close it, anchored step home — **995/6,026 Mana** at the round trip's end, Health untouched all day. Sorcha's personal containment design (`OBJ-49`) is complete, the single-point-of-failure concern resolved by a fails-closed-forever property; the cost breakdown is withheld for an in-person handoff, folded into the already-scheduled 2026-09-10 Jiu Valley trip, both parties staging through the region the day before (Sorcha via Bucharest, Alexander via Prague).

**Promotion barrier** (`EVT-000891`). `gatefall.skill_credit`: partial, four terminal skill counters (Rupture, Mana Bolt, Twin Fang, Pre-empt). `gatefall.skill_formation`: eight Taught-route candidates now tracking, up from one at span open. No new entities or relationships minted — all participants were already on record. A design flag (`F-066`, Owen played as a question-generator across a whole scene) filed to `engine/004_DESIGN_FLAGS.md`, cross-referenced to open `F-057`. A behavioral-preferences import from a prior session's memory export filed to `090_CAMPAIGN_STARTUP.md`, including an explicit second confirmation (via `AskUserQuestion`) that this campaign's actual practice is per-turn writes, not deferred-to-`/save`, overriding both the export's original guidance and the `/save` skill's own stale claim to the contrary.

- **Repository allocation.** `EVT-000885`-`EVT-000891`; no entity, record, or relationship identifier minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000891**. ## 2026-09-06, 16:00-23:30 (`EVT-000853`-`EVT-000858`) — Wade Bishop, three dungeons, and Elias Kane

**A minor Runtime correction opens the span**: checkpoint 0134's stored campaign time (21:00) is corrected to 16:00 for the same date — waking at 06:00 plus one A-Rank Gate clear and a shop visit does not plausibly consume fifteen hours. Not a mechanical event; the session's own narrated time runs from the corrected figure.

**Wade Bishop's owed callback, finally made** (`EVT-000853`). The BGM case against Ironline is real and moving, his cover intact. Told to start actively positioning for Brannigan's chair once the case matures — not yet, too soon, but build a clean record starting now. Agreed: *"clean sheet starting now, ready to move when there's an actual opening."* `OBJ-36`/`OBJ-38` close in full.

**Elias Kane texted via Renata's roster-forward, a meeting arranged** (`EVT-000854`) — *"Chicago's fine, I'm already here. You pick the place. This week if you can."* The Sidecut, Cermak Road, 19:00.

**Three S-Rank instant dungeons, the last three of ten purchased keys, solo, back to back, no damage, no mastery credit** (`EVT-000855`). Crypt (potion cache, +3 Greater Mana Potions), Crypt (Dagger Mastery [S] rune, unbound-awakened, giftable), Frozen Gallery (a third Elixir of a stat, banked). +1,178 S-Rank Mana Crystal banked, +6 S-Rank Beast Core. 50,600 XP.

**The cascade and the level-130 Title Assessment** (`EVT-000856`). Level 129→133. **Before the Confluence [Absolute, +30] granted** — a Gate cleared while the world stood at Escalation Tier VII, satisfied by the A-Rank directed-assignment Gate clear (`OBJ-43`, checkpoint 0134) landing after the world's own Tier VI→VII crossing. ## 2026-08-27 morning (`EVT-000781`-`EVT-000788`) — the far door, first use

**The Gate Walker terminal grant opened for the first time** (`EVT-000781`). 4,000 Mana, fixed. Onto the Landing — empty, dark farmland, a burning skyline that doesn't consume. Keen Sense and a sprint swept it clean (natural 17→77 Success, then 73→100 Critical); Flux Sight returned empty for the first time in its use history — nothing here carries a Rank for it to read.

**The Mill, and a pack fight that opened badly and closed overwhelming** (`EVT-000782`). Crossing the second seam into black shelving racks the size of mountain ranges, holding beasts by the million, each branded. A sprint toward a dozen-plus cluster cost the initiative on a natural-20 approach check — they clocked him synchronized, before he closed. Opening Rupture (Vanguard's bonus applied) one-shot the lead creature for ≈22,419 against a 4,000-Health ceiling; a split dual strike missed both targets and cost 300 Health to claws in return; a Flash Step-chained Dimensional Projection volley (six rolls, all landed) took five more; a Split Mana Bolt cleared the last two or three in one cast. **Five mastery crossings**: Flash Step, Dimensional Projection, Mana Bolt, and Sprint all advanced a tier. Corpses searched afterward: seven unranked mana crystals, a hide/claw sample carrying an unidentified brand, twenty-plus phone photos — none of it System-priced.

**The Ninth Quiet, and an entire library taken whole** (`EVT-000783`). A wide sweep found the Mill's far boundary; past it, a dead, intact, terraced city under a stopped noon. A library's full legible archive — unfamiliar script, recognizable Gate-and-curve diagrams — stored to the dimensional inventory in one free remote-storage action. Further exploration turned up a worn, deliberately-walked path.

**The worn path, a Standing Pattern, and a very old dwelling** (`EVT-000784`). Exploit Pattern's tactical read (critical) established the path as genuine habit — bipedal, consistent, a repeated deliberate detour — and advanced to Master mastery. **Flagged, unresolved:** a tenth concurrent Pattern against a nine-Pattern Rank ceiling. The path led to a long-inhabited, much-repaired dwelling; the stratum's own timelessness defeated any attempt to date it precisely. Thirteenth real anchor set there — confirmed this span that the tunnel network cannot bridge Earth and the far side; the far door remains the only crossing.

**Home, a corrected assumption, and a real two hours** (`EVT-000785`). Anchored Rift-step back to the seam, then on foot home; door closed from the Earth side, 4,000 Mana. **Player-caught mid-session: the far door does not freeze Earth time.** Corrected — roughly two hours passed on both sides, and gray-sleep mana-saturation exposure accrued accordingly (≈2 days' worth; no tracked field exists for it yet, flagged on the character sheet).

**The National introduction, delivered, and Thursday agreed** (`EVT-000786`-`EVT-000787`). Farraday's promised text landed on reconnect — handed off rather than brokered, consistent with his Region-V-only scope. Renata Okonkwo-Vance, BGM National S-Rank Liaison, minted and called directly: Thursday 2026-08-27 agreed, venue owed by Alexander 2026-08-26 EOD; she'll forward his contact to the national S-Rank roster same day, on consent-routed terms rather than a contact-list handoff.

**Eleven known US S-Ranks researched** (`EVT-000788`). Corrected mid-session on a worldbuilding assumption (S-Rank is normally public/celebrity status, not the default-private read first given): Reid Calloway, Dominic Crane, and Elias Kane were already canon; eight more newly authored and minted world-side (`worlds/gatefall/220_NOTABLE_FIGURES.md`).

**Promotion barrier** (`EVT-000788`). `gatefall.skill_credit`: real, one continuous expedition-scene. `gatefall.skill_formation`: none. Mana and Health fully reconciled against the two-hour active segment and one-hour resting/light segment: Mana 5,246/9,174, Health 17,607/17,632. Two items flagged rather than silently resolved: the Exploit Pattern capacity conflict, and the untracked gray-sleep exposure.

## 2026-08-25 (`EVT-000713`-`EVT-000721`) - three keys, a warning, and the fifth title slot

**Warning Nakamura, rift-stepped in mid-call** (`EVT-000713`). Told her plainly she might be in danger, then stepped into her locked workshop rather than explain over the phone — the city band, 5 Mana, blind arrival. She confirmed she was alone and pressed for specifics rather than reacting to the entrance. Named Marnie to her for the first time; she connected it unprompted to the already-missing maker she'd offered to help find. Laid five stolen CONSULT pieces on her bench; she read three (ring, blade, pendant) and found **three distinct unrecognized hands, one shared teacher** — no name, but more than there was an hour before. **Corrected him once**: she never said Marnie's work was superior to her own, that read was about the Dormant Core alone. **Declined a blanket "no new partners" instruction** as unworkable against her income, countering with her own line — new clients get run past him first. Her existing vambrace fusion job stands unchanged; she would not let his morning rush a working she'd already said she wouldn't rush.

**Two A-Rank instant dungeons, nothing rolled against him, `F-046` continues** (`EVT-000714`-`EVT-000717`). Beast Den then Frozen Gallery, 200,000 g each, cleared identically to every prior A-Rank clear this campaign has run: overwhelming force, no real contest. 403 A-Rank crystals sold for 1,370,200 g combined. Three redundant unbound instructional items banked (a Mend rune and a Silent Step rune, both E-Rank rune-tier; a Flash Step skill book at A-Rank) — none teach anything above what he already holds.

**Level 50, the decade that mattered** (`EVT-000716`). System Rank A → S. Fifth title slot opened. The Title Assessment ran and **granted nothing** — every remaining catalog entry (Red Survivor, Break-Breaker, Deathless, Executioner, Warden, Wellspring, Godslayer, Unbroken) needs a red gate, a stopped break, a party clear, an S-Rank boss, or a streak this campaign's solo A-Rank farming has never produced. The Riftwalker's level-50 evolution hook — the Threshold Trial, a personal gate between two anchors — unlocked, unentered. **Giantslayer equipped** to the new slot (`EVT-000718`): +1 step on all actions against a boss, for the fight's whole duration rather than one exchange, chosen over titles that would duplicate Loner's own step on the same single action.

**The Ivory Daily Premium cycle rotated a second time, live, past its own boundary** (`EVT-000717`). Opening `/system shop` found the 06:00 rotation overdue; one fresh cycle rolled under the standing rule, every offer capped at System Rank S. None purchased — the Premium Rune alone (Flash Step at S-Rank, 2,250,000 g) cost more than the session's gold at that point.

**The first key bought at his own Rank, and the first real hit of the session** (`EVT-000719`). An ordinary S-Rank instant-dungeon key, 900,000 g, Shattered City. Two elites and a boss instead of the usual walkover: the second elite's counter landed for **170 damage**, the first hit to actually connect since the checkpoint. The boss died to a critical Rupture (natural 87, 25,484 against 16,000 Health) before it acted. 19,200 XP carried Level 51 clean through to 54, each crossing refilling both pools in sequence. `gatefall.skill_credit` still reads **none** — real contact, but no single opponent in the population could have taken his health from full.

**263 S-Rank crystals sold, and a genuine gap in the shop closed** (`EVT-000720`). The crystal-buy table had never carried an S-Rank row — the one Section 12.5 price table missing one, against mined-deposit rules that have authored S-Rank crystal generation for versions. Flagged rather than guessed at. Ruled: derive the missing price from the weapon chassis table's own clean A→S ×4.5 step, applied to the crystal row's 3,400 g A-Rank anchor — **15,300 g per S-Rank crystal.** 263 sold for 4,023,900 g; 100 held back. **Promoted into the World Rule Profile the same session as Version 1.99** (`worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.98_to_1.99.md`) rather than left as an ungrounded exception — no other rule moved, no stored figure recomputed.

**Promotion barrier** (`EVT-000721`). `gatefall.skill_formation`: no new evidence, `dimensional_theft` unchanged at 1/3. `gatefall.skill_credit`: none, ninth and tenth consecutive. No trigger reached eligibility. Temporal anchor 10:40 → 12:30, no tick due. Gold **1,030,081 → 5,124,181**.

## 2026-08-24 - 2026-08-25 (`EVT-000699`-`EVT-000712`) - the CONSULT ledger read, and what COMPLETE means

**BGM entered together, and the letter of intent refused** (`EVT-000699`). The morning opened on Wade Bishop's flat *'Noon's not before noon. Neither is the terms'* - and the actual complaint underneath it was that **nobody had called Farraday since the night Wade agreed.** Alexander went to Wade rather than waiting, and walked in beside him. Farraday named his own ceiling honestly (no paper exists; statutory immunity does not draft in a morning) and offered a **signed letter of intent** instead. **Refused** - *'Before the official immunity, there is no deal'* - and, called an ultimatum, corrected to *'that's the terms we agreed on.'* **Farraday called the Director off-site rather than defending the gap**, and came back with her **personal verbal sign-off, committed to writing before Wade left the building**, full statutory filing by end of week on the terms already given. **Wade named why it mattered**: not the immunity, but *whether it would still be real in six weeks when nobody is watching.* He gave his statement; Alexander did not stay.

**A citation error corrected, player-caught** (`EVT-000699`). This changelog's own compressed summary of `EVT-000684` read *'Alexander named Wade Bishop'*. **`ENT-000223`'s entity record has always read *'an Ironline insider (Wade Bishop, not yet named to him)'*** - the record was right, the summary was wrong, and Alexander had referred to his source only as 'they' throughout. Corrected in place rather than buried.

**The auction floor, and a rift-step in front of a dozen witnesses** (`EVT-000700`). Seven live postings, **every one below his own A-Rank intake filter**; nothing at A or above, because Section 9.11 puts an A-Rank Gate in a freelancer's board-reach roughly once per 240 in-fiction days and an S-Rank never. **The top of the ladder does not reach a hunter through a board at all** - which is what the arrangement signed upstairs an hour earlier exists to replace. Then: *'This is panopticum'*, and **a rift-step taken from a standing position on a federal building's auction floor, in front of both guilds, with no concealment and no attempt to manage what anyone saw.** Deliberate Section 19 exposure. **A Runtime error was caught and retracted in the same exchange**: the daily contest roll is an abstract once-per-tick world roll, not a live floor negotiation, and was first narrated as though reps were bidding in real time.

**Bulwark, four Ranks in one swallow** (`EVT-000701`). The Ivory cycle's rune bought at **500,000 g** and consumed on the spot: **[E-Rank] Adept -> [A-Rank] Novice**, the largest single ascension this campaign has taken. Reduction **60% -> 75%**, duration **one exchange -> two**, and **`Covering the line` (A)** extends the reduction to **every ally within reach** rather than one. Mana 8 -> 10, mastery reset to Novice, lifetime counters carried. **The first thing in his kit that protects other people at scale**, bought on a morning with no fight in it. Gold **1,530,081 -> 1,030,081.**

**Walt Adamik's slow yes, priced honestly** (`EVT-000702`). `OBJ-29`'s first demonstrable result delivered on its due date and **smaller than it looked**: storage secured, one of two vouched people confirmed, two dealer calls made - **and no stock moving, deliberately.** Pressed on it, Walt named the three things that buy speed: a **cover for the capital**, the **second vouch locked**, and **stock bought outright**. **Alexander declined to run any of it** - *'That's your job to secure those three... I am putting money and crystals, you're doing the business'* - and Walt took it without argument. **A wristguard appraised free and declined**: [D-Rank], Perception +2, a passive warning charm, **C-Rank ceiling with a Rank of unused headroom**. Asked whether he would reprice on a partner who had just told him what it was, **Walt refused the premise flatly** - a partner punished for honesty stops being honest. **A standing arrangement opened: Alexander appraises anything Walt cannot identify, free**, which retires the shop's appraisal costs and lets it price closer to true on everything it has been discounting for its own risk.

**Home in front of him, and an ordinary evening** (`EVT-000703`). Left Walt's shop by **vanishing mid-goodbye**, no warning and none asked for - *'I don't care about it anymore.'* Unlike the Quickknife demonstration, this was not offered as proof; **`ENT-000147`'s knowledge gains a second first-hand entry**, and Walt's habit held: he did not call after him. Owen off shift on a Monday, sushi at **Nagi**, the full BGM account given before they were seated at Owen's own condition. **Marguerite's call landed after 20:00** (`ENT-000225`), discharging Kesha's end-of-day commitment exactly - logistics-first, no warm-up. Told the voicemail and the BGM appearance, **not the cooperation deal**. Her advice: **do not call Vance tonight**, business hours tomorrow, **offer the on-record quote before she has to dig for one**, three sentences drafted and sent the same night. *'Most clients call me after the story's already running. You called before it existed.'*

## 2026-08-25 (`EVT-000704`-`EVT-000706`) - the crucible floor fires in full

**The first tick under escalation tier IV, and the first with a `quiet_days` value to read** (`EVT-000704`). Full roll record in `110_WORLD_LEDGER.md`. **`quiet_days` stood at 5** - initialised at 4 at the readiness gate from `EVT-000510` forward, incremented when 2026-08-24 closed with no qualifying contested scene despite four level-ups. **At 4 or more the floor owes all three rungs, and all three were discharged before the ordinary siting rolls**, per Section 9.1 step 2's explicit ordering.

- **The guaranteed audition, and it is the campaign's first.** Gate 1 forced on-route (natural siting 64 superseded), assessed **unconfirmed C-Rank**, entering the board as **`GB-14`** at the Lakefront. **True Rank read from the Bearer: d100 62 - S**, his Combat Tier already at the ceiling. **The anomaly table rolled twice and the worse taken**: 65 (**Red gate**) against 37 (population one Rank above, no rung left at S). **A Gate that seals on entry with no retreat until its boss dies, wearing a C-Rank card.** Deadline midnight 2026-08-30. **A party meeting the assessed C-Rank's legal minimum at the threshold switches the audition off entirely.**
- **A sited qualifying crisis.** An **unlicensed, never-detected, never-posted E-Rank Gate off Devon Avenue broke overnight** - no board entry, no BGM record, because nobody had found it. Commons loose on a residential street around 05:40, responders staged and holding, no fatalities.
- **Contest: `GB-10` (🎲 20 vs 25%) and `GB-13` (🎲 14 vs 55%) both TAKEN.** `GB-13` was the highest-Rank lot the board had carried since `GB-03`, **gone the morning after it posted and a day after Alexander stood on the floor watching both guilds glance at it.**
- **World event 🎲 45 (regulatory action), sited 🎲 9 - on his route: BGM Region V opens an internal incident review into the auction-floor disappearance.** `EVT-000700`'s consequence, arriving nineteen hours later on the world's own clock.
- Agendas 🎲 15, no initiation. No breaks among tracked postings. Pressure 0/0. Escalation credit 0 -> 0, tier IV holding. Major not due until 2026-08-27.

**The thirtieth daily** (`EVT-000705`). Completed on issue, **streak 17 -> 18**; 18 is not a multiple of seven so the box rolled once. 🎲 d100 **32** -> potion cache, 🎲 d4 **4** -> **three Greater Mana Potions** (8 -> 11) at System Rank A's greater tier. AP +4 and one Status Recovery created pending and **not claimed** - banks close at **12 and 23.**

**Two calls before seven** (`EVT-000706`). **Laurel Bianchi texted at 06:04**, ahead of any official channel: the review is **not disciplinary** - Alexander is not BGM's to discipline - but a dozen people watched something the office cannot explain, and Farraday wants him reached by end of day. *'There isn't a version of this that's urgent for you. It's urgent for us.'* **The cooperation arrangement's first real cost, inside twenty hours of the ink drying**, and precisely `ENT-000223`'s authored fear. **Rosalind Fenn called and was told plainly** that Alexander is moving on Meridian in person - *'before you hear it through your channels.'* She did not argue and did not soften her own read. **Meridian paused at his instruction; Marnie stays live**, the storage unit's payment history already requested. *'Call me when something happens in there. Not after.'*

## 2026-08-25 (`EVT-000707`-`EVT-000712`) - Loomis Street, and five makers on one shelf

**In through the front door, on a phrase off a torn badge** (`EVT-000707`). **A Runtime correction landed first**: Alexander proposed rift-stepping to an elevator on a known floor, and **no floor was known** - `EVT-000372` read the building's posture from a public street, never its interior. Corrected before it resolved. He walked into the lobby and said **`CONSULT - TEMP`** like a man who had said it a hundred times: **Perception 927 vs a trained guard, 🎲 natural 78 +2 -> 100 Legendary.** *'Consult's on four. Badge reader's broken so just go on up.'* **No suspicion, no call upstairs** - the phrase is ordinary inside that building.

**Floor four, read** (Keen Sense 🎲 32 +3 -> **92 Strong**): reception with an unused sign-in tablet, two empty glass meeting rooms, **three closed windowless consultation rooms** furnished for exactly the private unhurried conversation Marnie's own meetings were, and **a sixth keypad room with voices behind it.** The sign-in log's last entry was six days old with no company field. **Eleventh anchor set** in an empty meeting room.

**The assessment overheard** (Exploit Pattern 🎲 94 +3 -> **100 Legendary**). Cadence through a door is still a Pattern. **One voice conducts** - unhurried, bored by how well it always goes. **One answers badly.** *'the client believes it's your hand start to finish. That's the arrangement. You understand what happens to the arrangement if that stops being true.'* And: *'Walk me through the working again. From the seating, not from the finish.'* **It is an assessment - the same shape of request Marnie specialised in taking.** The conductor's parting line as the second man left: *'Careful with the satchel. That one's worth more than you are.'* **A name-collision check caught a reuse mid-scene**: the line first named a *Priyanka*, colliding with the forged Ironline roster's Priyanka Osei, and was re-authored to **Delphine** (`ENT-000229`) before it became load-bearing.

**The satchel out of his grip, and a tail across the city** (`EVT-000708`). **Agility 965, 🎲 natural 73 +2 -> 100 Legendary** - withdrawn into the dimensional inventory mid-stride with nothing to feel; **Silent Step's `Concealment through action` covered it.** He noticed four steps later and **called it in**: *'I don't know how, that's what I'm telling you... Okay, I'll - where do you want me.'* **A second Runtime correction**: rift-step proposed as continuous movement to match a cab, and it is **once per exchange**. Corrected; one step ahead of the route, **Sprint** carrying the rest, no roll (Rules 4.2). Destination **Ironclad Self Storage** (`ENT-000230`), gate code keyed from memory. **A third correction, on the player's challenge**: the mana-null disc had been carried as *left at the Cicero Scar* - and an item deliberately deployed at a watched site is one he would go back for. **Retrieved off-screen; now held-but-spent** rather than lost.

**The ledger, and what `COMPLETE` means** (`EVT-000709`). Shelving both walls, a dozen worked pieces, **an open handwritten ledger of aliases against dates, client codes and percentages.** One entry: an alias, a date three weeks old, and **`COMPLETE`** underlined twice in a second hand, **no entry after it, the date lining up with Marnie's silence.** Three pages back: another alias, six weeks older, same word - and in the margin, **`2nd time this qtr. Flag for D.`** **Twelfth anchor set.** He texted D about his own safety and got *'You're overthinking a filing note. Bring the satchel Thursday.'*

**The analytical read** (🎲 natural 85 +3 -> **100 Legendary**), and it is the session's largest finding:

- **CONSULT is a controller, not a consultancy.** It owns makers, sells their work anonymously, tracks aliases rather than identities.
- **`COMPLETE` is a threshold, not a status** - it marks the maker's **best** piece, the moment they prove they no longer need CONSULT. **Marnie finished an S-Rank blade and never came back for it**, which `EVT-000592`'s ceiling finding already established happened *after* completion, not during.
- **`Flag for D` means a person decides**, repeatedly, at least twice this quarter.
- **It does not establish what *handled* means.** The ledger records the decision point, never the outcome, and gives no name for D.

**Flux Sight on the artificer**: **D-Rank per card, Health and Mana intact, no injury** - whatever CONSULT does has left no mark on this one. **Keen Sense on the room** (🎲 10 +3 -> **70, ordinary success, recorded as partial**): a consumer-grade facility camera aimed at the door not the ledger table, a worn unlocked second exit, nobody else present. **The wallet lifted** while he worked the door - **Silent Step recast first** (player's standing intent, under-surfaced by the Runtime before the roll and **corrected**), 🎲 natural 41 **+3 including the Pattern** -> **100 Legendary**. **Identity: Peter Kwiatkowski** (`ENT-000226`), licensed D-Rank, no combat rating, a near-northwest-side address, and **an expired Coalition membership card** - the live route to a contact number through Kesha Morrison's desk.

**Five makers on one shelf** (`EVT-000710`). The satchel opened: **a worked bracer [B-Rank], Agility +9, ceiling A-Rank**, with **a second property built into the metalwork and never activated** - latent until finished, so unread. **Its mark is confirmed not Marnie's**: a second controlled artificer, established physically. Searching for her mark (🎲 36 +3 -> **96 Strong**) found **a matched earring set [A-Rank]**, clasp stamp identical to the dagger and the leather piece: **Intelligence +14 and a Mana-regeneration property worked into the silver, +15 percentage points at both Section 5.2 rates.** Finished, complete, never collected. A second pass for the **pattern** rather than the mark (🎲 55 +3 -> **100 Legendary**) found three more: **a ring [C-Rank] with a live nested working that makes the piece read as its surface Rank to any weaker instrument**; **a short blade [B-Rank] with +1 modifier step on the first strike of an exchange worked into its weighting**; and **a pendant [A-Rank], Vitality +16, the only one at its ceiling** - **once per day, below 25% Health, an automatic 15% heal, no action and no roll.** **A finished emergency ward built to save a life, left on a shelf.** All five taken; **the half-finished B-Rank ring blank deliberately left** as an ordinary customer's commission.

**And the manifest under the floor** (🎲 41 +3 -> **100 Legendary**). A hollow section of subfloor under a paving stone set to look permanent, holding one folded sheet in the margin-note hand: **'Collected 3, left 2 - D says the pair's not worth the trip yet. Will reassess next quarter.'** **D does not come for everything** - and the earrings were on the wrong side of a cost-benefit call. **That is the one genuinely hopeful fact in the thread**: someone weighing value against effort is a different shape than someone eliminating loose ends. It is also **a quarterly reassessment cadence on a shelf now five pieces lighter.** Photographed.

**Home, and the whole picture** (`EVT-000711`). Rift-step to the apartment anchor. **A hooded weatherproof cloak and four prepaid burner phones**, cash, no ID - **cash $329,287.22 -> $329,090.22.** The wallet worked again for a contact number and there is none; **the expired Coalition card and the home address are the two live routes to Kwiatkowski.**

**Promotion barrier** (`EVT-000712`). **`gatefall.skill_credit` explicit `none` - the eighth consecutive settlement to classify so.** No dangerous scene anywhere in the span; nothing acted against him and nothing was rolled against him on either day. **`F-046` stands, and `GB-14` is the profile's own answer to it, sitting unentered.**

**`gatefall.skill_formation`: a candidate opened, on the player's own proposal.** `dimensional_theft` - *withdraw a carried object from another person, into the dimensional inventory, under concealment, without contact registering.* **All five Section 7.1 tests pass**, and **test 3 is flagged rather than assumed**: Section 12.9 governs his **own** storage and Dimensional Projection launches his **own** objects, so neither part authors taking a thing out of another person's grip. **Test 5 credits the two takes as one scene**, being one continuous pursuit. **Recorded `tracking` at 1/3**; no profile-authored result exists, so 3/3 would open the mandatory adjudication queue rather than resolving automatically.

**Trigger audit.** `gatefall.quest.urgent` **`none`, and recorded dry rather than omitted** - the Devon Avenue break satisfies criterion 2 and criterion 1's *nearby*, but **he never went**, so criteria 3 and 4 cannot be stated from facts he perceives. **Section 8.4.7 creating conditions and never a quest, exactly as authored.** `gatefall.quest.hidden` **ATTACHED**: **`ENT-000193`, *Who Runs the CONSULT Operation***, all four Section 8.4.3 criteria holding, capacity at 2/6, **reward fixed at S-Rank / 6,800 XP**, rendering `[HIDDEN] ???`. Slots **3/6**.

**Temporal settlement.** Anchor **2026-08-24 10:16 -> 2026-08-25 10:40**, crossing one date boundary and the 06:00 tick. **Health never left cap on either day.** 47 Mana spent against Conduit [S]'s continuous accrual; both pools close **full**, remainders zero. **Anchors 9 -> 12 of 48** (Walt's shop, the Loomis fourth floor, the COMPLETE unit); cap re-derived live at effective Agility 965 against a stale note reading 713.

**Five new entities** (`ENT-000226`-`ENT-000230`), **fourteen new Events**, **one Hidden pointer attached**, **four Runtime corrections and two ledger repairs** - all player-caught, all recorded rather than buried. The second repair: **`120_INVENTORY_AND_OWNERSHIP.md`'s `current_funds` block was found stale** against the Bulwark purchase and corrected.

## 2026-08-24 (`EVT-000688`-`EVT-000698`) - four levels before noon, and none of them cost him anything

**The box in the dark, and a rotation seventeen hours late** (`EVT-000688`). Woke at 23:30 beside a sleeping Owen to claim the twenty-eighth daily's banked box: 🎲 d100 **9** -> potion cache, 🎲 d4 **1** -> three Greater Healing Potions (10 -> 13). Then `/system shop` fired the standing rotation rule - the Cobalt cycle's 06:00 boundary had passed seventeen hours earlier and nobody had opened the tab all day. **Prismatic**, and Rank roll 🎲 d100 **99** produced the first S-Rank equipment offer this campaign has ever generated: an **Aegis Guard Shield [S-Rank]**, Vitality +24, 28% reduction, 225,000 g against a balance of 732,481. He looked at it for ninety seconds and went back to sleep. The phone had been lit for a while: Kesha Morrison missed at 22:47, and an unknown 312 number twice, the second leaving 41 seconds unplayed.

**The world tick, and four within reach** (`EVT-000689`). Seven Chicago Gates (🎲 1d4 4), one Prague. Siting 🎲 d100 x7 -> **four land within reach in a single tick**, the densest result this campaign has rolled: **GB-10** and **GB-12** at Red Line, **GB-11** and **GB-13** at the Lakefront, the last of them an unconfirmed **C-Rank** on a five-day timer. **`GB-05` was taken by the trade** (🎲 12 against 15%) - the first live posting the board has lost to somebody else since it restarted, and one Kesha had already shown him. Non-Gate event 🎲 **28** -> *nothing today*. Agenda 🎲 **32** -> no initiation, **against an unauthored threshold** settled from this campaign's own three recorded yes-results. Board rises to **7 live**, above Section 9.10's ordinary band. **Repair:** `GB-09`'s stored deadline was one day short of its Section 9.3 derivation and is corrected to midnight 2026-08-30.

**The Ivory cycle, and the key** (`EVT-000690`). The Prismatic offers expired unbought in full at 06:00, the S-Rank shield with them. Ivory rotated in: a Horizon Reacharm [S-Rank], an Ascendant Mindspun Loop [S-Rank], and a **Bulwark rune [A-Rank], unbound**. **A Runtime error was made and corrected inside the same session** - the rune was first read aloud as a skill he had never held, when `skills_known` records **Bulwark [E-Rank] Adept** since `EVT-000232`; it is an ascension offer, not an acquisition, and the correction was stated plainly because it changes the decision. **Bought: the Runic Key [A-Rank], 250,000 g.**

**Forty-one seconds** (`EVT-000691`). **Teresa Vance** (`ENT-000224`), trade beat, *Chicago Ledger*: a source inside BGM Region V gave her his name attached to an S-Rank carding, **sixteen hours after the reading**. Faster than regional logging propagates on its own - it was given, not leaked. She called him rather than running it blind, offered a lawyer or the Coalition, set no deadline. `OBJ-39` opened.

**The kitchen call** (`EVT-000692`). Kesha Morrison, second ring, file already out. She asked whether he was alright before anything procedural. **Told him against her own interest that cancelling buys no cover** - the amended registration went to Horizon's and Ironline's compliance desks the previous evening, and Section 9.4 puts no S-Rank on a co-op board at all. He filed **active** anyway, reasoning the Coalition can take a cut and that managing an S-Rank would bump her up too. **A standing intake filter, read back verbatim: nothing below A-Rank, exceptions for genuinely out-of-pattern work or anything abroad** - which puts all seven live board postings below his line. Told her the voicemail in full; got Vance's character vouched for and the advice not to call cold and not to ignore her either. Accepted an introduction to **Marguerite** (`ENT-000225`), a liaison who works for hunters rather than institutions. **Two pending world-side commitments recorded - the first live instances of the Decision 082 construct in this campaign.**

**The lakefront loop** (`EVT-000693`). Woke Owen, who read the half-hour head start off his hair and clocked the dodge without fighting about it. Ten kilometres in under fifty minutes, calisthenics after. **Twenty-ninth daily complete, streak 16 -> 17.** Its box opened at once: 🎲 d100 **55** -> System gold, **150,000 g**. Owen, on the dungeon plan: *don't cut it so close that clearing your head is the reason you're late.*

**Two A-Rank instances, and LEVEL 44 -> 48** (`EVT-000694`, `EVT-000696`). Both 🎲 d8 **4** -> Overgrown Temple. Combined 20 commons, 5 elites, 2 bosses, all A-Rank; combined 18,200 XP and **four thresholds**, +252 to every base Stat, **max Health 3,008 -> 4,016** and **max Mana 1,512 -> 2,016**. Loot: 264 A-Rank crystals, 7 A-Rank cores, a **Greatarm [A-Rank]**, a second A-Rank key off the first shrine (spent on the second run), and two **`unbound-awakened`** instructional items - a Rupture skill book and a Keen Sense rune, both redundant to him, both intact, and both handable to another awakened.

**And nothing rolled against him in either one** - which is the entry's actual finding. Rupture at 5,092 against an A-Rank boss's 3,000 Rank Health means the boss dies to the opening strike, twice. **`gatefall.skill_credit` classifies `none` for both clears**: successful-use counters moved (Rupture +3, Twin Fang +1, Dagger Mastery +1, Micro-rift deflection +1, Mana Bolt +1) and **not one qualifying scene did**, because Section 7.4 requires genuine danger and there was none. Same test this campaign settled six times before. He spent those keys precisely because they were beneath him.

**Sold, twice** (`EVT-000695`, `EVT-000697`). 173 crystals for 588,200 g, then 91 for 309,400 g. **Gold 732,481 -> 1,530,081**, the campaign's first seven-figure balance - and the A-Rank crystal holding closes **unchanged at 100**, 264 in and 264 out. `unspent_points` **5 -> 25**, none allocated.

**Settlement** (`EVT-000698`). Anchor 2026-08-23 23:30 -> 2026-08-24 10:16. **Health never left cap at any point in the span.** Mana spent inside both instances and restored by the level-up award at each of the four crossings; both pools close full at 4,016 and 2,016 with zero carry. Damage re-derived: **Rupture 5,823**, **Mana Bolt 2,200**, main-hand **1,990**, off-hand **1,978**. Twin Fang's preview stays withheld and Mana Bolt's x1.75/x1.90 prose disagreement stays unrepaired, both carried forward unchanged.

**The design flag this session earned.** **`F-046`** - a Bearer who has outgrown ordinary Gate content has **no structural route back to the genuine danger Section 7.4 requires** before System Rank S. Section 9.2's distribution skews overwhelmingly low; Section 9.6's anomaly table fires only on a public Gate's first entry or an unentered break; and Section 17 instant dungeons are deterministic at their key's Rank and **never roll anomalies at all**, so a Bearer carrying banked keys always has a mechanically guaranteed-safe combat option in his pocket. Raised in play and written immediately to `engine/004_DESIGN_FLAGS.md`, non-canonical.

**Owed forward:** Marguerite's call, end of day. Teresa Vance, uncalled, on a clock he cannot read. The BGM meeting with Wade Bishop, before noon, **terms still not in writing**. Walt's `OBJ-29` first demonstrable result, due today. Seven live board postings, all below his own filter. Two watchers, eleven unread logbooks, an unclassified warm object, and a monitoring room that knows its shelf is short - none of it touched.

## 2026-08-23 (`EVT-000680`-`EVT-000687`) - carded S-Rank, and the guild fight opens for real

**The boat, and two gifts** (`EVT-000680`). Twenty-eighth daily completed, streak 16. Midday, the serious conversation: Alexander named S-Rank re-assessment as his intent, and the harder truth under it — *"nobody could stop me"* — which Owen pressed rather than let pass, and then accepted along with the cost to himself of being publicly known as his partner. **Owen received Minor Triage** (a D-Rank Mend rune, consumed as a new lesser working rather than a redundant re-teach — Minor tier 13 instead of the 62-cost Signature working, fulfilling the "cheap-Mana Mend" want from `EVT-000255`) and **one Intelligence elixir** (Mana 137 → **139**, two past the flat C-Rank ceiling, an unranked state no instrument has looked at). Docked; rift-stepped home, 5 Mana.

**Today** (`EVT-000681`). Owen, correctly reading evasion he chose not to fight, watched Alexander dress for something serious. Travel to BGM Region V by Red Line.

**The lobby, and the measurement** (`EVT-000682`-`EVT-000683`). Keen Sense Legendary on the lobby found a live compliance-audit lane running Ironline roster checks under a fresh nameplate; a second cast, Exceptional, located the actual case file, handled twice that morning. Ninth anchor set. Requested re-assessment coldly. **Flux Sight and Keen Sense (Legendary) read the appraiser as ordinary and uninvolved** before the plate read **1,512 Mana — squarely S-Rank.** Two officials arrived under obvious short notice; both read (Success, then Strong success) as exactly what a scrambled response looks like, not a prepared one. **Second measurement witnessed: 1,512 again. Classified reawakened. Carded S-Rank, contract-Rank cap lifted in full**, with the officer's honest warning about what a claim this size, this fast, actually costs in attention.

**Laurel Bianchi, Desmond Farraday, and the arrangement** (`EVT-000684`). BGM's own institutional interest arrived fast — Laurel Bianchi intercepted him before he could leave with only a slip; Desmond Farraday named the guild-reform ask's real ceiling (no dissolution authority, only an evidence-built case) and **offered formal cooperating-source status and real statutory immunity for an Ironline insider.** **Alexander confirmed he had one in mind and that consent wasn't secured yet — deliberately unnamed, referred to only as "they" throughout, no name and no gender given.** (Correction, 2026-08-24: this line previously read "named Wade Bishop," which the entity record itself never supported — `ENT-000223`'s own `agenda` and `knowledge` fields have always held "an Ironline insider (Wade Bishop, not yet named to him)." Player-caught; the compressed changelog summary was the only place the error existed.) He negotiated the standing arrangement to his own terms: **accepted** as a compensated, declinable Gate-incident responder; **refused** broader reporting; **accepted, narrowed** to advance notice before moving against a guild. Farraday took the narrower deal. Auction floor checked and found closed (Sunday).

**Wade Bishop agrees** (`EVT-000685`). Told everything at The Nook, immunity emphasized as the difference between his career ending badly and ending on his own terms. Wade named his own conditions — terms in writing, one night's sleep — and agreed. **Confirmed for BGM tomorrow, 2026-08-24, together.** `OBJ-36` reshaped into a formal cooperation case; `OBJ-38` opened for the standing BGM arrangement.

**Home** (`EVT-000686`). A planned stop at Walt Adamik's was abandoned mid-visit — `OBJ-29`'s result is due tomorrow, not overdue, nothing actually owed. Rift-stepped home instead.

**Settlement** (`EVT-000687`). Anchor 09:52 → 23:30, **no damage at any point**, 21 Mana spent and fully recovered. Keen Sense +7 uses/+1 scene, credited at its held ceiling. Two new entities, eight new Events, one guild-facing objective opened.

**Owed forward:** Wade and Alexander at BGM tomorrow. The BGM-cooperation arrangement's first real test. An unclassified object and eleven unread logbooks, untouched since Checkpoint 0118. Whatever the guilds do once the classification spreads this week.

## 2026-08-23 (`EVT-000665`-`EVT-000670`) - what never finished arriving, and LEVEL 44

**Two rewards that had been owed since Friday, and fourteen points into Strength** (`EVT-000665`). `pending_rewards` read `ability_points: 0` and `daily_random_box: 0` beside a note recording three rewards created pending at `EVT-000605` and none claimed. **The fields were wrong and the note was right**; player-caught, ruled owed. Ability Points claimed at **+4** (Ascetic, at claim), the box opened standard at streak 15 - d100 **23** -> potion cache, greater tier at System Rank A, d4 **4** -> **three Greater Mana Potions** - and all fourteen unspent points put into Strength. The third pending reward was a further Status Recovery already inside the standing 20 and was **not paid twice**.

**The Cicero warp heart, and an instrument nobody has ever caught looking** (`EVT-000666`). Anchor sense read first: the containment floor still returning presence, **the warp heart nothing since it was set**. Anchored step at the 5-Mana city-ground rate, mana-null disc to the slab, ten minutes bought. **Flux Sight resolved the structured object sighted and abandoned on 2026-08-20**: emplaced, anchored, oriented, no Rank line - and what it is FOR is **watching**. It does not suppress. **Exploit Pattern, natural 82 +2 -> 100 Legendary**, returned the shape of the whole arrangement: **it is aimed DOWN at the sealed bay, its mounting has weathered a decade** - making the Meridian warehouse the newer installation - **and the spike interval is shortening**.

**The evidence traded away on purpose.** He ran the hand-instrument taken off Junia Okonkwo's floor two hours earlier, and its screen - eleven weeks of *C-Rank, stable, no change* and then a needle pinned past the top of its scale - **was overwritten by a live sweep of Cicero**. No copy existed. **And the cycle registered**: acting intent his, natural **33**, net 0 - the disc masks what reads through a slab, not an emitter of his own - **Failure**. The watching layer logged an active instrument at the warp heart, the second alarm at a second site in seven hours.

**Seven metres down, through a floor** (`EVT-000667`). **Keen Sense `structure through barrier`, natural 21 +3 -> 81 Strong**: the bay is bigger than the fill BGM records, **not uniformly crushed**, one bay standing with its floor intact - and the read was precise enough **to place a rift-step point inside it**. System Rank A reaches 100 m sightless and Section 7.3 bars only a Gate boundary, a Section 9.6 seal or an instance wall, so **he did not dig**. Sealed dead air met **Shrug Off [S]**, which suppresses environmental onset up to A-Rank outright. Phone torch, the Bearer owning no lamp.

**The far wall is not a wall.** The slab bends into something matte the light fails against; rebar curves toward it; **parts of its outline are absent rather than dark**. Flux Sight returns **no band at all** and a **ceiling past the top of the scale**, and what it is FOR comes back as a state: **unfinished, still arriving**. **Owner ruling, reserved by `ENT-000151`'s own `resolution_depth`: it is the Break's cause, buried mid-transit in March 2016 and arriving ever since.** Every spike the perimeter logged for a decade is one increment of it crossing. Minted **`ENT-000216`**.

**`What Never Finished Arriving` revealed and completed in one action**, Section 8.4.3 settling both. **1,500 XP** - repriced from 700 by Profile 1.89 hours before it paid - **crossed LEVEL 44**: +63 to every base Stat, **Health 3,008**, **Mana 1,512**, anchors **32 -> 35**, main-hand **1,486**, Rupture **5,092**, Mana Bolt **1,721**. Slot released, 3/6 -> 2/6. **The first level in this campaign bought by finding something rather than killing it.**

**And then he stepped past it** (`EVT-000668`). No boundary existed to bar the step - the same shape as `EVT-000656`. Beyond the face: a surface that returns nothing, a torch cone that **ends** at six metres, no smell, no temperature, one continuous drift. **Flux Sight returns nothing here at all.** From this side the arrival is a **cross-section**, and the bulk runs back past the light: **ten years have delivered a fraction of it**. **Anchored steps do not reach from a volume that is not the city** (Section 18.8) - the line-of-sight step still does, so the exit held at 6 Mana. **Ninety-one paces, counted**, because a hundred is where his only known destination stops being reachable. **At ninety-one: a second face**, two metres, **wet worked stone in its edge and cold air through the seal** - the flooded gallery he walked eleven miles away the same night. Then a pulse: **the first face gains and the second gives, at the same instant.** One arrival, two contact points, **and one of them is a Gate somebody built a cold room around.** Minted **`ENT-000217`**.

**Profile 1.89 -> 1.90 adopted** (`EVT-000669`). Section 8.4.2's Bearer-exclusion is **scoped to Gates, red gates and instant dungeons** - outside one he satisfies criterion 1 himself and the qualifying threat widens to any immediate lethal threat he perceives, **an awakened human included**; self-created danger and the contract exclusion carry the anti-abuse weight, and the solo-instance case is deliberately untouched. New **Section 7.6 Succession**: a personally killed awakened human leaves **one** signature technique, never a Stat Passive or class ability, at the victim's Rank clamped to the Section 7.5 ceiling, Novice with counters at zero. **No magnitude invented.** Nothing in the fiction changed tonight; what changed is what the world will pay him for.

**Settlement** (`EVT-000670`). Anchor **04:15 -> 04:42**, mode active, **no damage at any point**, 18 Mana spent and absorbed. Both audits **none** and **no dangerous scene occurred**. Allocation reconciles: earned +9 to 351, Strength allocated +14 to 66, unspent **5**, and Section 4.2's uniformity check returns **630** on all five Stats. **Three ledger repairs, all player-caught**: the two unwritten pending rewards; **the Seer's Lens pair listed in both `gear` and `custody`**, which had the panel counting two holdings twice; and **two `Lesser Healing Potion` lines merged to x20** under Section 15.3.2's quantity collapse. `180_CURRENT_STATE.md`'s stale Bindings and Current Situation - 03:58, Profile 1.87, gold 330,881, all of it superseded by its own Promotion Status at Checkpoint 0116 - are rewritten here.

**Owed forward:** the world tick at 06:00, now under an hour and twenty minutes out. Okonkwo and Hargrave are awake or waking. **A mana-null disc is lying on the slab at the warp heart.** And the second face is still there, with a flooded gallery on the other side of it.
