## 2026-08-23 (`EVT-000659`-`EVT-000664`) - the first pulled strike, and six things restored to the ledger

**A rule authored an in-fiction hour earlier got used for exactly the case that authored it** (`EVT-000659`). Two people stood in the containment room; Alexander wanted them down and not dead; under Profile 1.86 there had been no way to say so. He declared the strike **pulled** and fired Dimensional Projection **from inside the Gate**, the boundary ruled to cross because Section 7.3 bars only *a sealed barrier* and the launched object is *a real physical object after withdrawal*.

**He rejected his own opening plan on the arithmetic, which is the part worth keeping.** Crystals price at weapon power 0 and the improvised chassis x0.5 - **81 pulled**, enough for a man holding 40 and nowhere near a B-Rank holding 600, with all five projectiles reaching 405. He switched to blades on the finding that **Dagger Mastery's `Thrown` (C) covers a blade launched by Dimensional Projection**. Rolls 49/49 -> strong, **666** and 660 pulled; 82 -> critical, **322** pulled. **The first projectile alone cleared her 600.**

**Both reached 0 by pulled damage alone, so neither died** - incapacitated, stabilising on their own, each carrying a Section 6.3 injury. **Pulled overkill is still incapacitation:** the crystal delivered eight times the man's entire pool and Section 6.2.1 does not care, which its own migration record names in terms. Neither acted. Neither perceived him. `Recall and launch` (D) brought every projectile home in the same action, so nothing was left on that floor - **the player's stated worry about leaving an ordinary weapon behind was met by the recall rung rather than by the weapon choice**, and the real trade was named: a mana attack leaves no object and is loud on the only anomaly instrument in the building, a recalled blade leaves nothing and is quiet on it.

**`ENT-000213` Junia Okonkwo and `ENT-000214` Emmett Hargrave minted**, both above `disposition_baseline` and both authored under Decision 092 in the response that created them. **Several fields are recorded `unestablished` rather than invented** - Hargrave never spoke audibly and never turned around, and twenty seconds of a man's back does not entail a voice or a secret. That is the `F-041` fabrication class declined on purpose.

**Then the instrument** (`EVT-000660`), and it is the largest single fact this thread has produced. Okonkwo had it pointed at the Gate when she was dropped and the cycle had finished. Eleven weeks of **C-Rank, stable, no change** - and with no plant on the manifold, **the reading has gone past the top of its own scale and pinned there.** Which reframes the plant entirely: **it was never only cooling, it was holding the signature down**, and every reading that operation ever trusted was the lid talking rather than the thing beneath it.

**A Runtime authoring error retracted in the same Event.** Owen was narrated awake at 04:00 with a kettle going, and had earlier been handed a **06:00** boat he "would not move" - **both invented, neither supported.** `EVT-000243` has him carried from a couch to a bed **without waking**; `EVT-000386` puts him asleep at **06:00 on a Saturday in this flat**. Canon holds *tomorrow morning, the boat, with Owen*, and no hour. **The 06:00 is struck and the two-hour clock cited across several exchanges never existed.** Same class as the phantom twenty cores at `EVT-000638`. Player-caught.

**Four hundred thousand gold, and a desk deliberately not walked up to** (`EVT-000661`). 10 D-Rank and 118 A-Rank crystals to the System shop: **330,881 -> 732,481 g.** Section 12.1 posts $50,000 an A-Rank crystal, so roughly **$5.9M of USD was left on the table** - against Section 12.2's implication that the licensed market keeps a sale record and runs a Rank audit, on a man **carded E-Rank** holding crystal out of an unregistered A-Rank Gate whose staff were unconscious on its floor at the time. **A standing player instruction is recorded:** *sell* means the System shop.

**Profile 1.88 adopted** (`EVT-000662`), and **six holdings restored** (`EVT-000663`). Section 12.9's opening sentence was exhaustive by construction - stored or carried - so an item owned but in someone else's hands had no legal position and `EVT-000636` **deleted two of them**. New custody clause, new `IN CUSTODY` panel group, and the load-bearing part is the **exclusion from remote storage** despite its bare wording being satisfied: he consented to it being there, so recovery is an act in the fiction rather than a thought. **The Dormant Core [S-Rank], the heartwood, the frost-fused plating, three C-Rank crystals and the Seer's Lens pair** are back on his ledger with holder, since, and end condition - the most valuable object in the campaign sitting on a bench he could otherwise have emptied silently.

**Settlement** (`EVT-000664`). Anchor **03:58 -> 04:15**, mode active, **no damage**, 13 Mana spent and absorbed by Conduit [S]. Both audits **none**, and **no dangerous scene occurred at all** - two people were put down before either acted. Dimensional Projection +1 use / +1 scene / mastery **0/3 -> 1/3** at Expert.

**Three ledger repairs, and two of them were mine from one checkpoint ago.** Dimensional Projection's stored line rendered **Practiced** values (4 projectiles / 13 m / Mana 5) against an **Expert** counter, never re-derived when it crossed at `EVT-000651`; live values are **5 / 21 m / 4**. **Seven A-Rank beast-drop crystals and one Beast Core [A-Rank] were owed under Section 11.1 and never awarded** - Checkpoint 0114 promoted the mined deposit and stopped, and the player caught it. And the **A-Rank crystal holding and the containment plant both existed only in `120_INVENTORY_AND_OWNERSHIP.md` prose** with no structured `inventory` line, the exact shape `EVT-000636` recorded against the plating.

**Owed forward:** the world tick at 06:00, under two hours out. Okonkwo and Hargrave wake up. And the first A-Rank core this campaign has held is exactly what Section 12.10 wants for the Warded Vambrace pair Nakamura has never agreed to touch.

## 2026-08-23 (`EVT-000654`-`EVT-000658`) - Profile 1.87, and the Gate that does not close the ordinary way

**The engine gained a way to not kill someone, because the fiction asked and the profile had no answer.** A player question - *can I knock these two out instead* - had no honest yes under 1.86. Section 6.2 resolved every landed hit into damage and removed it from Health; Section 6.5 made death final; **nothing anywhere authored an attacker doing less than his magnitude on purpose**, and a grep for `subdue`, `non-lethal`, `unconscious` and `knock out` returned nothing at all. Sections 4.0 and 4.1 had always promised that intent precedes method and the die resolves a *declared* action, so *I am pulling this* was legitimate under sections already in force and evaporated between the declaration and the resolution. **The hole was in the resolution surface, not the permission.**

At effective Strength 644 the consequence was absurd in the gentlest available direction: the least harmful thing Alexander could throw, a mana crystal at the improvised chassis, still computed **322 a projectile** against a man with no Rank Health whatsoever. The protagonist is by background a krav maga instructor.

**Section 6.2.1, the pulled strike** (`EVT-000654`, `F-042`). Declared before the roll, free, no action and no Mana, on any attack the attacker controls, never after a result is known. Damage resolves at **x0.25** through the ordinary formula with every multiplier, reduction and injury test applying to the reduced figure. **A target reduced to 0 Health by pulled damage alone is incapacitated rather than killed** and stabilises on its own. The bright line attaches to the source of the damage and never to intent - **any** unpulled contribution to reaching 0 makes it an ordinary death under Section 6.5, which is untouched. No accuracy, no defence, no modifier step, nothing against a monster. Pinned by `tools/test_gatefall_pulled_strike_contract.ps1`, which fails on eleven legs when the section is excised.

**Then four hours of a physicist alone in a dead gallery, and two Legendary reads** (`EVT-000655`). **Provenance:** people have been in here, months back, **repeatedly**, walking one line from the mouth to the boss and stopping - and **never once mining a seam**, on a site somebody poured real capital into. They were checking on it. **Structure:** the strata carry the binding's own slow pulse, so **the binding is not attached to the boss; the boss is attached to the binding**, and the instance hangs off it. Loner was applied to both rolls and had been omitted from the prior span's - corrected forward, not retroactively.

**The ruling that follows was stopped for rather than narrated** (`EVT-000656`). `ENT-000167`'s `resolution_depth` had reserved *what is bound, why, and by whom*, and *what the boss kill does* is that same question in different clothing - answering it inside a combat round would have authored the campaign's payoff by accident. **Elected: the tether cuts.** The boss dies, the instance does **not** collapse, and what the binding held stops being held. **Alexander's inference is now correct and he still has no proof of it** - and **the ordinary correct action is the catastrophic one**, which is a thing Wade Bishop's entire profession does for money.

**And somebody came** (`EVT-000657`). Seven hours after a non-routine alert reached an inbox three shells deep - rolled at 41+, **natural 94** - two figures stopped in the lit room beyond the mouth. **A seventh anchor**, the first ever set inside a Gate, its bounded reach priced before it was taken. Eleven metres of standing water on a **Legendary** approach. Then the read, and **a Runtime error corrected in the same breath**: Flux Sight had twice been asserted not to cross a Gate boundary and was then withheld until asked for. Both wrong. Section 4.4 attaches a boundary clause to Keen Sense, Mana Bolt and Flash Step and **none to Flux Sight**; the section is emphatic that it reads on sight with no roll, no activation and no declaration, and that **rendering is not optional** - withholding it pending a declaration is `F-017` item four by name.

**The read is lopsided and that is the finding.** The woman: **[B-Rank], 600/600, 288/300**, Resonance Survey and a Damping Field, twelve Mana already spent on the manifold - **and neither ability turned on the aperture eight metres away.** The man: **nothing at all.** Unawakened, ordinary, back to the thing. **Neither has looked at the Gate once.**

**Settlement** (`EVT-000658`). Anchor **2026-08-22 23:55 -> 2026-08-23 03:58**, crossing midnight, mode active throughout. **No danger occurred this span at all** - four resolved rolls, three Legendary, none contested by anything alive - so `gatefall.skill_credit` is **none** on its own terms rather than by omission; `gatefall.skill_formation` **none**. Five Mana spent against Conduit [S]'s continuous accrual; both pools close at cap. Anchors **6 -> 7 of 32**.

**Three ledger repairs, none of them play.** Dagger Mastery's chassis carried **Adept's +1.20 / x1.95 against a stored `mastery_level` of 4**, which had two gates disagreeing about the same weapon - Expert authors +1.25, so **x2.00**, and the previews re-derive to **1,332** and **1,320**. `progression.ability_points_earned` had not advanced with the level-43 grant (337 against allocated 332 plus unspent 10), so the +5 is declared on `EVT-000653` and the counter reads **342**. And a telemetry field name cited in prose inside `system_state` tripped Section 8.4.6; reworded.

**Owed forward:** minting the two figures with Decision 092 coverage the moment either is spoken to; the world tick at 06:00, two hours out, with every anchor unreachable from inside; and a boat Owen said he would not move.

## 2026-08-22 (`EVT-000646`-`EVT-000653`) — the containment site entered, its purpose answered, and LEVEL 43

**Fifteen weeks of paperwork on `OBJ-21` produced a shell name and a burned investigator's licence. One night of walking in produced half the answer outright.**

**The approach was correct and it cost the right things** (`EVT-000646`). A rift-step to the fence-line anchor, a Suppression Emitter against the undisclosed anomaly layer, Silent Step, a mana-null disc pressed to the side door, and a B-Rank lockpick set against an unranked mortise deadbolt — which resolved **automatically** under Rules 4.2, because a specialist tool that reads a mechanism and reshapes to fit, in the hands of effective Perception 549 against a domain rating of 10 with nobody opposing, is not a contest. **A detection roll made against that arrival was retracted the same session and is recorded here rather than buried:** `EVT-000612` had already rift-stepped to that exact anchor eight hours earlier and run *two* active skill reads on the fence line with its anchor sense reporting clean, and `EVT-000577` before it. Canon had answered the question twice. The Runtime asserted a resistance from plausibility instead of reading settled state — **the exact drift the Resident Core names, and it runs against the player, who is the only party holding the correction.** Player-caught.

**The site is a cage, and the ruling that made it one was the player's** (`EVT-000652`). `ENT-000167`'s `resolution_depth` had reserved the warehouse's true purpose as an owner ruling and explicitly declined to author it. That reservation came due the instant the Bearer's own Flux Sight would have resolved it automatically, so the ruling was taken at the table from four options with their costs laid side by side. **Elected: the Gate is not being farmed, it is being kept.** Everything the investigation had gathered reorganised around it — the capital, the climate plant, the shells, the anomaly layer, the compartmented staff. The plant is **suppression apparatus disguised as building services**; the poured floor is a cooling manifold; and the boss carries an **old, load-bearing binding** degrading slowly. What remains deliberately unauthored: what is bound, why, and by whom.

**Two Ranks of institutional error, and one person who noticed.** Flux Sight converted the site's own unconfirmed reading to **confirmed A-Rank by capability** (§9.5, bypassing the true-Rank roll and its anomaly band) against the **C-Rank** every instrument on site reports. **Hollis Rennard** (`ENT-000212`, `REL-000099`) is the D-Rank containment monitor who had been sitting alone in that room with the lights killed for three hours because her instruments and her eyes stopped agreeing a month ago. Her evidence is an **eleven-week plant duty-cycle log** — a field nobody reads because it is plant, not the asset — non-linear for four weeks, plotted against a threshold line she drew hoping never to see crossed. It crossed that evening. She traded all of it for the one fact Alexander gave her unprompted, on her direct question, knowing what it would cost her: *'A Rank.'*

**She is wrong about him and the record keeps her wrong.** Her instrument returned nothing on a man standing three metres away, and she concluded he might be an emanation of the Gate. He never corrected it. The inference is honest, her evidence supports it, and Decision 091 requires the mistake be played rather than repaired — **and the instrument's real limitation is that it reads anomaly signature, not people, and would have returned nothing on anyone.** A promised beat where the Emitter's expiry would 'start her instrument seeing him' was **retracted before delivery** rather than shipped: nothing in canon says a handheld signature reader detects a Bearer, that is squarely the live §19 exposure question, and it is not the Runtime's to answer with a prop.

**The first Urgent quest in twenty-nine dry days, created by the player's own hand** (`EVT-000648`-`EVT-000649`). Alexander's math and physics read the plant as a heat balance and returned the honest answer — *the plant is not the heat, the plant is the only thing removing the heat* — and he took it anyway, into the dimensional inventory under §12.9, and the eighty-minute ceiling became immediate. **'Uncontained'** fired on the instant, because §8.4.2 excludes the Bearer himself and anyone under his own contract, and Hollis is neither. Accepted, slot 4/6, reward fixed at **1,600 XP** on System Rank A. Completed **two minutes later** by a passenger rift-step at ten times cost (50 Mana), and the slot released.

**The Gate, and a fight that was never one** (`EVT-000650`-`EVT-000651`). Archetype **Flooded Mine** (d8 3): standing water, −1 step on Strength and Agility, drowning hazard — so the answer was Intelligence-based ranged work, which the flood does not tax. Population came up **thin**: 6 commons, 1 elite, 1 boss on 3d6/1d2, bottom of the range for an A-Rank. Keen Sense mapped the whole gallery on a natural 81 that clamped to **effective 100, Legendary**; Exploit Pattern fumbled the same exchange on a **natural 3**, the critical tail no margin removes. Six Mana Bolts, Pre-empt (S) resolving first throughout, cleared six A-Rank commons at 1,500 Health each — four criticals, one strong, one natural-97 tail. Four Dimensional Projection strikes ended a 3,000-Health elite before it acted. **Bulwark was activated and never tested**, 8 Mana on an exchange that produced no incoming hit — the cost was named before activation rather than after.

**Three mastery crossings, and a full strip.** **Mana Bolt ADEPT → EXPERT**, **Dimensional Projection PRACTICED → ADEPT**, both on third qualifying scenes; Resonance Extraction to 2/3 toward Expert on one scene covering four worked seams. **211 A-Rank crystals** — 203 of 208 mined, plus **Prospector's +2 per personally extracted deposit across four deposits**, which the Runtime had omitted from the running total and the player caught.

**XP was dropped entirely and the player caught that too.** Six A-Rank commons and one elite went unpaid through the whole engagement while the narration moved on to consequence. Recovered at the barrier: `6 × 400 + 800 = 3,200`, no underdog multiplier (both at his own System Rank). **2,417 → 4,017 → 7,217 against threshold(42) = 4,200 → LEVEL 43, XP 3,017/4,300.** The System Rank A rung paid **+63 to every base Stat**; **maximum Health 2,504 → 2,756**, **maximum Mana 1,260 → 1,386**, both refilled, **+5 unspent points** (5 → 10). Not a decade level, so no Title Assessment.

**And two long-standing thresholds closed in the same roll.** The Combat Tier S health figure `180_CURRENT_STATE.md` has carried for three checkpoints (2,700) is cleared at **2,756** — noted, not stored, since Combat Tier derives only at its own consumer instant. The §19.2 S-Rank licence band was already cleared and now stands at **1,386 against 1,225**. Nothing has been asked of any instrument and nothing is disclosed.

**Two stale stored values repaired, both the `F-037` shape, both player-caught.** `anchor_sense.rung` read **B-Rank** while its own note authors `kind` at **A-Rank and above** — it had not moved since the System Rank A crossing at `EVT-000589`; the authored rule governs and the string is repaired to A. And **Conduit was quoted at +30 points in play against the sheet's rendered +25**, corrected mid-session; the outcome did not move, because at either rate the S-grant's continuous in-scene accrual absorbs every cost, but **the number stated was wrong and is recorded as wrong.** A third disagreement is **flagged and deliberately not repaired here**: Mana Bolt's line carries `×1.75` in its prose and computes `×1.90` in its own worked figure. It predates this span, no Event this session touched it, and it is outside this checkpoint's derived target set.

**Settlement** (`EVT-000653`). Anchor **20:40 → 23:55**, one continuous span, mode `active` throughout — no rest was narrated and none is invented. **No damage was taken at any point**, so Health never left cap. 133 Mana spent across twelve activations and fully recovered under Conduit [S]. Both pools close at the new maxima, remainders zero. Both audits **none**; `gatefall.skill_credit` discharged per-Event at `EVT-000650` and `EVT-000651`.

**Repository allocation.** `ENT-000212` (Hollis Rennard), `REL-000099`, `EVT-000646`-`EVT-000653`. New high-water marks **ENT-000212, REL-000099, EVT-000653**.

**Carried forward, unsettled.** Wade Bishop's first daily report arrived mid-scene at 21:03 and was never opened. The boss's binding is a live thread with **no quest pointer attached** — §8.4.5's time-separation rule bars a record authored and attached in the exchange that produced it. Hollis Rennard is standing outside a fence with an intact personal log, a dead tablet, an employer who does not know she has spoken to anyone, and no chosen next move. And the containment plant is now a holding rather than a fixture, shown to nobody.

## 2026-08-22 (`EVT-000644`-`EVT-000645`) — recovered play: Fenn's delivery, found by reading the session back

**This span is not new play. It is play that happened and was never written down.** The 2026-08-20 session's own transcript was read against the ledgers at this checkpoint, and its closing beat — Rosalind Fenn's Ironline delivery — existed nowhere in canon while `180_CURRENT_STATE.md` still carried *Fenn on Ironline's real ownership* as an obligation owed **tonight**. The fiction had discharged it hours earlier. **From the ledger's side, a promise silently satisfied in prose is indistinguishable from one never answered** — which is precisely why the Resident Core refuses to let a commitment be narrated without being recorded.

**The finding itself** (`EVT-000644`). Fenn texted rather than called, consistent with her own rule about what she will put in writing. **The Region V office is not owned by anyone with their name on a door: three shells deep, the same architecture as the shell behind Marnie's storage unit, different registered agent.** She cannot get past the third layer, and she is exact about the reason — whoever built it **knew how far a licensed investigator's tools reach and stopped one shell short of it on purpose.**

**She volunteered the cross-thread reading unasked:** *'That's twice now. Two different threads, same architecture. I don't think that's a coincidence and I'm not going to pretend I do.'* It is the first time she has connected two of Alexander's briefs rather than reporting each in isolation. **`OBJ-27` and `OBJ-36` stop being adjacent threads and become one architecture seen from two ends** — the fenced warehouse, Marnie's storage unit, and now Region V. Nothing confirms a single operation behind all three; the inference is available and deliberately unmade.

**And she refused payment.** *'Not billing you for tonight. This one I wanted to know myself.'* **Characterization break recorded under Decision 076**, and a substantive one: her `want` on record is to be paid on results and keep the Walt Adamik referral channel clean, because a results-billing PI lives on the next recommendation. A night worked unbilled out of her own curiosity is the first time she has done anything of the kind.

**Settlement** (`EVT-000645`). Anchor **20:35 → 20:40**, five minutes walking. Both audits **none** — reading a text is neither a method nor a dangerous scene. Pools, gold, cash and XP all unchanged; the five Section 9.1 supply sources advanced to the new anchor with empty results.

**Repository allocation.** `EVT-000644`-`EVT-000645`; no entity, record or relationship identifier. New high-water mark **EVT-000645**.

A point moves a Stat by one; Section 3.2's rung moves it by sixty-three.

Frozen Gallery, Crypt, Flooded Mine. **The dice came back to life at two Ranks down**: a **natural-2 fumble** that Decision 052 says no modifier can lift, a boss answering at natural 86 for 42 damage, and a Flooded Mine boss that finished on a **second graze by fifty-two points**. Broken Rhythm's standing passive step decided the first fight outright — unmodified, that roll is a graze at 526 against 1,000 Health.

**Owner ruling: a qualifying scene is a live resolution, not a dangerous one** (`EVT-000625`). Settles the Section 7.4 question open since `EVT-000574`. **A scene credits where the resolution could actually fail**; where Rules 4.2 disposes of every foe automatically, nothing is credited. D-Rank at three steps below Combat Tier credits nothing — `EVT-000616`'s zero now rests on a rule rather than a judgment — and **C-Rank at two steps below credits**. The evidence for the losing reading was stated and remains true: 42 damage of a 2,252 pool is 1.9% and is not danger by any account. The owner ruled that a skill exercised against an outcome that can go wrong is what a mastery track measures.

**LEVEL 41 → 42**, crossing inside the third instance (`EVT-000626`). +63 to all five base Stats, +5 points, **maximum Health 2,504, maximum Mana 1,260**.

**AND THE S-RANK CAPACITY LINE WITH IT.** Section 19.2 measures maximum Mana alone and puts **S-Rank at 1,225+**. He holds **1,260**. A BGM instrument applied today returns **S** on a licence that reads **E**. **Re-assessment is voluntary and must be requested**, compulsory only by court order after a public incident — so nothing is issued, nothing is filed, and nobody knows. The concealment this campaign has run on for a month is now a **choice** rather than a fact.

**Five mastery advances — and the Runtime first narrated eight** (`EVT-000626`). **Dagger Mastery [A] → EXPERT** (chassis ×1.95 → **×2.00**), **Mana Bolt [B] → EXPERT** (×1.75 → ×1.90), **Silent Step [D] → MASTER**, **Dimensional Projection [C] → ADEPT**, **Broken Rhythm [C] → PRACTICED**. **Twin Fang, Flash Step and Sprint stand at 2/3 and did not advance:** the first instance was fought with plain dagger strikes, before the decision to drill everything, so skills used only in instances two and three took **two** qualifying scenes rather than three. Corrected at the barrier, before promotion.

**What would not train.** **Mend took one use and zero qualifying scenes — he cannot train healing because nothing can hurt him.** **Field Command is untrainable solo.** **Keen Sense and Exploit Pattern spent two scenes each on a level that returns nothing**, both being at their §7.5 ceiling with dividend complete and Mana at minimum — the `F-012` shape Section 7.4 already names.

**189 crystals sold for 32,130 g; gold 317,531 → 287,531 → 319,661** (`EVT-000627`). **C-Rank pays for itself where D-Rank did not**, and threw in a 10,000 g resale rune and a free [C-Rank] key besides. The 🎲 d20 **20** player-choice drop taken as a second **Quickknife [C-Rank]**, its imprint fixed from the boss under 11.2.1 rather than rolled: **Serpentine** body family (+1 further Agility), **Control** signature Echo (+1 step, once per scene, to establish or break a hold), Weathered finish.

**Two Runtime errors corrected in play, both by reading the authoritative line rather than by any gate.** **`Prospector`'s +2 crystals per deposit was dropped from all five D-Rank instances** of the prior span — a standing equipped-title bonus with no counter to disagree with its absence, the exact `F-017` shape; D-Rank stock corrected 50 → 60. And **the claim that §12.10 fusion requires identical pieces is false**: its six checks want the **same chassis** and **within one Rank**, nothing about identity. That error was restrictive, so it under-sold the options — and the looser truth makes the banked **Quickknife [B-Rank]** a legal fusion frame one Rank above the donor.

**Repository allocation.** `EVT-000623`-`EVT-000628`; no entity, record or relationship identifier minted. New high-water mark **EVT-000628**. `110_WORLD_LEDGER.md` opened and correctly unchanged — three sealed §17 instances are invisible to the world, no tick fell in the span, and `EVT-000620`'s discipline held: none was opened where he could be seen.

**The §17 bystander gap is ruled** (`EVT-000620`). **Opening an instant dungeon is physically visible — he is seen to vanish, and seen to return.** Section 14.4 conceals the System, not the vacancy. **And he never opens one where he can be seen**, which is the protagonist's own standing discipline and the reason ninety days of play have never charged him for it. This span's five openings are clarified as out of sight; nothing is created and nothing removed. **What it leaves live is the cost of being forced** — an emergency, a camera, a companion at his shoulder. ## 2026-08-22 (`EVT-000614`-`EVT-000619`) — a drink cancelled, five keys burned, and level 41

**The owed beer went from scheduled to owed-again** (`EVT-000614`, `EVT-000615`). Alexander called Dale Pruitt (`ENT-000135`) at ~14:00 to bring it forward to that afternoon — 🎲 14 at net 0, **major failure**: four rings to voicemail from a man who has never not answered, then *'can't. 1900 still good'*. At 14:26 Dale corrected his own tone, sent the address he had promised that morning and forgotten (**The Sidecut**, 2211 W Cermak, `ENT-000211`), and volunteered — for the first time in a month of texts, unjoked — that he had been on the phone since eight with nothing to show. Alexander then cancelled 19:00 outright, giving *'i did not check the calendar'* and **offering no replacement time**: 🎲 56 at net -1, **failure**. *'No problem at all'* after fifteen minutes, *'I'll be there anyway. Booked the night for it'* — and no alternative night named, by a man who produced a night, a window, a street and a reason for the street inside a day when first asked. `OBJ-33` **open, scheduled → open, unscheduled**; characterization first recorded on `ENT-000135` under Decision 076.

**Dale's disposition authored under Decision 092** (`EVT-000614`), his record predating the campaign's `disposition_baseline: ENT-000210`. `want`: eight combat-rated bodies before the client stops waiting. `secret`: the bigger client has let him understand the eight-body requirement is about **the filing rather than the site**, and he has not said yes or no for six days — which is why the beer was booked at a bar nobody in the trade uses, and why *what are you actually doing with yourself* was the question waiting under it. **He has no idea he is describing the class of filing Alexander holds unfiled** (`OBJ-27`).

**Five [D-Rank] instant dungeons, 14:45-16:00** (`EVT-000616`). Keys 11,250 g. Archetypes 🎲 8 Frozen Gallery / 6 Shattered City / 7 Ashfield / 3 Flooded Mine / 8 Frozen Gallery. **Sixty D-Rank bodies, every one resolved automatically under Rules Section 4.2** — 930 main-hand against 100-Health commons — **no damage taken and no Mana spent** across the whole run. Take: **186 D-Rank crystals, 13 cores**, and five boss drops of which three are instruction he cannot use and one is a Perception accessory for a man whose Perception is dead. **The Ashfield's hourly burn check never fired**, the instance closing inside fifteen minutes.

**LEVEL 40 → 41**, crossing inside instance 4 (`EVT-000616`). Section 3.2's System Rank A rung: **+63 to all five base Stats**, +5 unspent points, pools restored to new maxima. **Max Health 2,000 → 2,252; max Mana 990 → 1,116.** XP 797/4,100. **The Section 19.2 S-Rank licence gap falls 243 → 109 Mana — one further level.** Combat line re-derived from Section 6.2 rather than carried (`F-037`): main-hand **1,053**, off-hand **1,041**, Rupture **4,518**, Mana Bolt **1,239**; the Twin Fang package and projected Quickknife are composites that could not be confirmed against the loaded formula and were **deliberately not asserted**.

**Section 7.4 settled against the span, and it is the span's one contestable call** (`EVT-000616`, `EVT-000619`). `successful_uses` credited in full — Dagger Mastery 240 → **300**, Resonance Extraction 35 → **40**. **`qualifying_scenes_total` credited ZERO for both**, and no mastery progress moved: Section 7.4 requires genuine dangerous-scene contribution, holds that *trivial repetition does not count*, and requires the scenes be *materially distinct*. Sixty automatic kills three Ranks down, taking nothing, across five near-identical instances, is the case the rule describes. **First live application since `EVT-000574` closed that question's retroactivity forward-only.**

**Owner ruling: sub-Rank clear time runs on a halving ladder** (`EVT-000617`) — **raised because the Runtime invented a duration Section 17 does not author**, narrating an hour and five minutes per instance plus an unauthored walk home. Clear-and-loot time halves per Rank below the Bearer: 1 below **60 min**, 2 below **30**, 3 below **15**, no floor; at Rank and above keeps Section 17's full two-hour window; the differential reads from **Combat Tier (6.7)**. **A second gap was named and deliberately left unruled** — Section 17 does not author what an onlooker sees when an instance opens in public, and this span opened five on a Chicago street in daylight. No consequence applied, none invented.

**The shop, and a correction the player made to the Runtime** (`EVT-000618`). `/system shop` rendered under the campaign's pre-render gate. The Runtime had twice asserted the shop buys crystals only and priced the run as a **2,510 g loss** on that basis. **Section 12.5's Resale paragraph** — skipped by a bounded read that stopped short of it — repurchases catalogue equipment at **25%** and unused consumables, runes and keys at **50%**, refusing only named artifacts, commissioned gear, broken gear, mundane possessions, **cores**, and story objects. The campaign's own record already carried it (`EVT-000332`; the ledger's core lines). Sold: 136 D-Rank crystals **+5,440 g** and the banked Keen Sense rune [C-Rank] **+10,000 g**. **Gold 313,341 → 302,091 → 317,531; net +4,190 g on the afternoon.** What leaves with that rune is the option of putting Keen Sense at [C-Rank] into a sensor's hands, the day after Iris Halvorsen joined the crew.

**Two Runtime authoring faults corrected in play, both by the player.** An **unauthored destination** — the narrated walk home and the kitchen, withdrawn; Alexander never left the route from Walt Adamik's, and Section 17 needs no location or privacy to open an instance. And the **resale error** above. Both are recorded rather than quietly repaired, because the failure in each case was reading a bounded range and treating what was read as complete.

**Ledger repairs.** `daily_premium.offers.armor` read `unpurchased` against its own `purchased: 1/6` field for a full checkpoint — `F-037` class, repaired. The sold [C-Rank] Keen Sense rune's line carried *[S-Rank] Adept* for Keen Sense against a live [A-Rank] Master; it left the ledger with the sale.

**Repository allocation.** `EVT-000614`-`EVT-000619` and `ENT-000211`. ## 2026-08-21 (`EVT-000602`-`EVT-000604`) — the profile learned a trade, and so did he

**Three profile edges adopted at one anchor** (`EVT-000602`), none moving a stored value: **1.84** made every render template show its own geometry; **1.85** authored **fusion** (Section 12.10) — two finished pieces become one, an effect moves and a magnitude never does, the donor is consumed at every outcome including every failure, one fusion per piece; **1.86** authored **Seamwork** (Section 7.3) and narrowed Section 12.8.

**The evening, 17:30 to 21:20** (`EVT-000603`). A book off his own shelf at the apartment — **his personal library is established canon from this Event**, which a mathematics and physics graduate obviously owns and nothing had needed to say. A Flux Sight sweep of four boss materials that **returned nothing new and is recorded as an explicit nil**, the Ceiling Register already holding all four. Then Nakamura’s bench at 19:20, ten minutes early.

**He asked for an operation the profile did not author**, and the scene stopped rather than inventing one. She named her own uncertainty instead of pricing around it. **He bought two E-Rank Hunter’s Bands at 80 g each to practise on and she refused them** — a catalogue accessory carries no named effect, so it can never be a fusion donor at any Rank. **Gold 367,501 -> 367,341.**

**She taught the half that kills gear**: fusion is not joining, it is unmaking first. **Roll d100 49, net +1, effective 69 — success.** A finished working opened intact on the fourth attempt. *‘That took me eleven months.’* **Wednesday booked on her initiative**, to read a working that has reached its ceiling before one of his is in pieces.

**SEAMWORK RATIFIED** (`EVT-000604`), automatically and with no adjudication queue, Profile 1.86 authoring the complete result. **[C-Rank] Novice, Mana 3**, on three qualifying scenes — `EVT-000485`, `EVT-000550`, `EVT-000603`. **Native C rather than E** under Section 18.3.1: the evidence already showed him reading a maker’s mid-process corrections and opening a working, so an E-Rank entry would have authored two Ranks of nothing. **Counters at zero**, Section 7.1 barring backfill — the Rank/mastery asymmetry is raised as `F-040`, not resolved.

**This candidate had been closed once, as `none`, by a Runtime-authored audit citing no owner ruling** (`EVT-000485`) which also instructed later sessions not to revisit it. The owner reopened it on Section 13.6’s own text. `EVT-000485` stands; the procedural defect is `F-039`.

**Also settled:** Health 1,980 -> **2,000** at rest across three hours fifty, remainder zeroed at cap; **Status Recovery 18 -> 19**, an owner ruling correcting a field its own `latest_settlement` line had contradicted since `EVT-000553`; and the **Daily Premium rotated at System Rank A for the first time** — Verdant series, offering **[S-Rank] Adaptive Arcanist Feet** and an **[S-Rank] Alchemist Key**, the first S-Rank equipment this Bearer has ever been offered.


## Changes

**2026-08-21 17:26 – 17:30 — the withheld offers reopen under an authored ladder (`EVT-000599`-`EVT-000600`). MIGRATION.**

- **A migration span, not a played one.** Four minutes of fictional time, no die rolled, nothing fought, no Mana spent. State catching up to a profile that moved underneath it.
- **`EVT-000590` withheld two Section 7.5 breakthroughs as unauthored** — Keen Sense and Exploit Pattern both at [A] Master beneath a ceiling System Rank A had moved to S, with §7.3's ladders authoring those skills through A and stopping. The Runtime had improvised the categories rather than read them; the validation gate caught it at the barrier and the state was restored.
- **Profile 1.81 paid that amendment**, giving all sixteen mastery-tracked skills an S column on the terms every rung below carries — a category and nothing else — under the ruling that *a learned technique reaches S without becoming a signature*. **The withheld notes had been stale since**, asserting *no S-Rank rung exists* about rungs that now do: the `F-037` shape again.
- **KEEN SENSE [A] Master → [S] Adept.** Mana 1 → 2. Scope: S Adept computes 8 subjects, **`scope_floor` ratchets 8 → 9 and binds**, holding 9. Category **`Standing watch`** — *the read no longer ends with the scene*: it persists on a place or subject already detected, and a material change there reaches him after he has left it.
- **EXPLOIT PATTERN [A] Master → [S] Adept.** Passive. Scope held at **9 Patterns** by the same ratchet. Category **`Standing Pattern`** — a Pattern on **a person or an organisation** persists indefinitely while its conditions hold, reaching negotiation, planning and institutional behaviour rather than one exchange.
- **One improvisation adopted, one refused, and the refusal is the interesting half.** 1.81 kept `Standing Pattern` in name and shape. It **explicitly rejected** the rung invented for Keen Sense — *no S category crosses a Gate boundary, a §9.6 seal, or a §17 instance wall* — which the profile names as the natural top of four ladders and refuses on purpose. **`Across the seal` does not exist and never did.** `Standing watch` is the smaller and better grant: the fenced warehouse, the Cicero Scar sublevel and the Dormant Core all stay behind their seals.
- **Three skills at [S] again, and this time all three are authored.** All at the System Rank A + 1 ceiling and finished until System Rank S at level 50.
- **No derived figure moves.** Neither skill carries a magnitude; both scope counts are held by their floors at values they already had; Keen Sense costs one more Mana against a pool of 982.
- **Campaign time 17:26 → 17:30.** Pools unchanged. Status Recovery +1 still unclaimed, now across five checkpoints.

**2026-08-21 17:26 — the Ascetic sentence struck, and the ability-point history closes (`EVT-000598`).**

- **Owner ruling. One sentence struck; no stored value moves and the profile is untouched.** Sections 8.1 and 16.2 already state the rule correctly: the daily Ability Points reward is **+3 by default, +4 while Ascetic is equipped**, read **at claim**.
- **The sentence that closed `skill_rulings.ascetic_claim_timing_evt_000357` was the problem.** It claimed the +4 applied *from the twenty-second daily onward* — a blanket statement about every later daily, **contradicted by the equip record**: Ascetic sat in the `EVT-000357` loadout, was swapped out when it became Cartographer + Untouchable (uncarried until the `EVT-000444` correction), and was only restored at `EVT-000535`. There was no continuous run from the twenty-second.
- **The arithmetic settles which reading is right.** The pool is fixed by hard state at **323**; level-ups are exact at **195** and title payments at **40** (gated at `EVT-000597`). So the daily line is **88** — twenty-eight dailies at +3 plus **exactly four** at +4. **The struck reading produced 91 and could not close**, requiring three points earned and never allocated that do not exist.
- **Corroborated by the record**: `EVT-000487` names *the +8 from two claimed AP entries* — two +4 dailies while Ascetic sat in the early loadout — and the twenty-seventh paid +4 after `EVT-000535`.
- **The exact four are deliberately not enumerated.** Reconstructing per-entry history from prose is what Profile 1.82's migration declines to do, and for the same reason. The total is what is proved and what the counter holds.
- **The ability-point ledger is closed.** `EVT-000594` found the undeclared points, `EVT-000597` gated the axis forward, and this answers the one question the counters could not ask about the past. **Nothing further is owed on it.**

**2026-08-21 17:26 — Profile 1.82: the last ungated growth axis comes under Decision 079 (`EVT-000597`).**

- **Three ledger corrections in a row were the same defect.** `EVT-000594` (twenty-five points allocated, fifteen recorded), `EVT-000595` (two baselines derived instead of read) and `EVT-000596` (a capacity that never moved when its source did) were all **numbers kept as prose**, and all three were found by hand rather than by a gate. **Ability points were the last growth axis without one** — XP and every skill counter have had one since 1.19 and 1.26.
- **Six `tracked_counters` paths.** `progression.ability_points_earned` at **323**, and `stats.<stat>_allocated` at **52 / 27 / 113 / 29 / 102**, each derived as `base − creation − automatic(40)` from the creation array 11/12/8/11/8 and Section 3.2's ladder (`automatic(40) = 378`).
- **`unspent_points` and the five base Stats stop being stored** and become renderings — `earned − Σ allocated`, and `creation + automatic(level) + allocated`.
- **The whole check is one line.** Section 3.2 raises all five Stats by the same rung, so `base − creation − allocated` must be **the same integer for all five**. It is **378**, five times. **Had `EVT-000594`'s ten Intelligence points gone undeclared, Intelligence would read 368 against four Stats reading 378** — a failing gate at the settlement instead of an audit three days later.
- **Earned is taken from the pool identity, not re-totalled from its sources**, and the reason is recorded: the daily Ability Point line lands **within one point** of closing on the unresolved Ascetic claim-timing question. Allocated and unspent are hard state, so 323 holds whichever way that ruling goes. **Gating does not settle the attribution and does not pretend to.**
- **No stored value moves. Baselines are prospective** — no historical Event gains deltas, no past allocation is revisited. From here forward a settlement that moves a base Stat without a matching delta fails validation.
- **Not closed:** the Ascetic question, and `titles.points_granted` disagreeing with its own `earned_names` grade payments — a title-ledger field these counters do not reach.

**2026-08-21 17:26 — a quest slot the System Rank opened and nobody counted (`EVT-000596`).**

- **`non_daily_quests.capacity_total` read 5 against a System Rank of A.** Section 8.4.1 derives it from System Rank alone — **2 / 3 / 4 / 5 / 6 / 7 at E / D / C / B / A / S** — and *no skill, allocation, item or title raises it*. **5 → 6.**
- **The B → A crossing at `EVT-000589` settled six consequences and re-derived five.** Both ceilings to S, the growth rung +23 → +63, a fourth title slot, five Stat Passives re-pinned — and quest capacity, read off the identical value, did not move.
- **No quest was affected.** Three attached against a ceiling the Bearer has never reached; nothing was refused, deferred or lost.
- **The gate found it, not a reading.** `test_gatefall_quest_contract.ps1` derives the figure from the profile ladder and the sheet's own `system_rank`, and had been red since the crossing.
- **Same shape as `EVT-000595`, one field over**: a value the profile derives from another value, stored as a rendered number, with the derivation done by hand at the moment the source moved.
- **Fixing it uncovered four more, because a failing suite stops at its first failure.** The capacity leg had stood in front of everything after it since `EVT-000589`. **All five Stat Passive rows** broke §15.3.1's row contract — `EVT-000591` wrote each S-Rank grant *between* the Stat value and the threshold marker, crossing the bold markers — and all five still named a clamp that no longer binds (*[S-Rank] held: System Rank ceiling*; at System Rank A the ceiling **is** S, so the terminal is a plain **`S-Rank`**). **Flux Sight's tail was two Ranks stale** on a line already reading [S-Rank]. **Rupture's and Twin Fang's magnitude clauses** had been reworded out of the shape their contract reads. Every one a rendering, none a wrong value.
- **The suite now runs to completion: 38 of 38 green**, the first fully passing run since the level-40 crossing.

**2026-08-21 17:26 — two baselines derived instead of read, and three surfaces that never heard the correction (`EVT-000595`).**

- **Five rendering defects**, found by reconciling every stored skill value against the profile it derives from. **No roll re-run, no past outcome altered**, and no counter, Rank, mastery level or floor moves.
- **Rupture's S-Rank baseline read 938. §7.2 authors 1,000.** 938 is `375 × 2.5` — how the table was *built*, not what it *says*. **DMG 3,715 → 3,877.** `magnitude_floor` 260 still binds. The [S] rung was taken at `EVT-000590` after the span's last fight, so **3,715 was never used against anything.**
- **Mana Bolt's B-Rank baseline read 155. §7.2 authors 150** — same cause, one row down (`62 × 2.5`). **The line was also a whole span stale**, carrying effective Intelligence 412 and DMG 907 while the Record beside it read 491 and 1,034. **DMG → 1,026** at effective Intelligence 491. Mana Bolt has resolved at the 155 baseline since `EVT-000586`, worth 8 damage on a strike near 1,000 — **those rolls stand.**
- **Combat Tier unaffected — holds at A.** `offense_tier` was already S and cannot rise; `defense_tier` holds A; §6.7 takes the lower axis.
- **Keen Sense read 10 subjects against a computed 9.** The line computed `5 + 4 = 9` and rendered it as *himself plus 9 allies*, counting the Bearer twice. **Himself plus 8, 9 subjects.** Right at [B], drifted on the A-Rank Master crossing at Checkpoint 0100. Counters untouched.
- **Resonance Extraction rendered `scope_floor` 4 against a stored counter of 5**, which has read 5 since `EVT-000588`. **Four further miners → five.** The rung still bought the category rather than the count — at one higher number.
- **This changelog, `180_CURRENT_STATE.md` and `system/ID_REGISTRY.md` each recorded `EVT-000590` as three breakthroughs into [S]**, naming `Across the seal` and `Standing Pattern` as granted. The Event **withdrew both and restored the skills to [A-Rank] Master**; the character sheet and chronicle were correct throughout, and three rolling views carried the withdrawn version for five checkpoints. **2026-08-19 — Profile 1.74, and four flags closed cheaply (`EVT-000533`-`EVT-000534`).**

**`F-004` — a Gate that breaks unentered now rolls its true Rank.** §9.5 resolved true Rank *on first entry* and named no other trigger, so an unentered break had no Rank at all. **The roll is discovery, not creation**: a Gate's true Rank exists from the moment it opens. What the world committed to was the **assessed** Rank — the board priced it, the cordon was staffed to it — and the gap is the danger. **This supersedes the per-instance ruling at `EVT-000270`**, whose case held only while breaks never reached him, which 1.73's tick has just stopped being true. The flag had been *deferred to a play session* since 2026-07-31, and the play session never came **because breaks never reached him**.

**§18 swept, and it is clean.** All eight classes checked for dead grants and every context-derived cost expression checked for totality. **No third dead grant** — Echo Knight's slots are Mana-limited rather than structurally limited, Reliquary's relics each cover a different origin, Seam's read seams pay whenever their archetype recurs. **Every cost expression is total**; Riftwalker's anchored step was the only incomplete one and 1.72 closed it. §18.3.1 now carries **the class-grant analogue of §7.2's eligibility rule** — a grant returning nothing the Bearer already lacks is repaired before the offer renders, and it matters more here because **a class cannot be re-chosen**.

**`F-019` — one ledger owns each protagonist figure.** `180` had disagreed with itself three times about his own numbers and every disagreement passed every gate, because nothing said which copy was authoritative. The sheet owns them; everything else cites. **Citing goes stale visibly; restating goes stale silently.** Pools gated, mutation-tested.

**`F-031` — the growth ratchet gains a 400-token absolute floor.** A proportional allowance meant small surfaces tripped on editing noise while large ones absorbed real bloat.

**And the tooling written yesterday caught a mistake today.** The `F-019` rule was first placed *inside* the Save Algorithm heading, which the save plan loads whole, and the surface failed. **The categoriser reported it 100% residue with nothing trimmable and said not to cut but to report the budget as the blocker** — correctly. The fault was placement, not size. Reseated as its own section, the surface returned to its exact prior size.

**2026-08-19 — F-017 reduced from nine open items to five (`EVT-000531`-`EVT-000532`).**

`F-017` recorded ten mechanical errors across one long combat session, **all ten player-caught and none self-caught in the moment**. Item 4 closed at Profile 1.61; four more close here.

**Item 3 — XP untracked for an entire instant dungeon — is closed by making XP reconcilable.** The cause was that XP was a *rendered string*, so nothing could disagree with it. It is now the tracked counter `progression.xp_total`, under Decision 079's `baseline + Σ deltas = current_value`, which the repository gate already enforces for every other counter. **An unawarded kill now fails a save instead of vanishing.** Baselined at **1,945**, reconciling forward rather than back-filled across 530 Events.

**Items 1 and 2 — a modifier step treated as +1 instead of +20, and a dropped standing title bonus — are answered by making the arithmetic visible.** Every resolved roll must now render its own arithmetic and **itemise each contributing step with its source**. Wrong math fails on its face instead of needing the player to recompute it, and **a step whose source cannot be named is not a step** — the specific defence against standing bonuses nobody has to declare that exchange.

**Item 8's mislabelling half is now gated.** The mastery *word* was already asserted against `mastery_level`; the **star block was not**, and now is.

**Items 9 and 10 verified closed rather than assumed.** Decision 085's participation-audit gate is exactly item 9, and it fired three times this session. `new_checkpoint.ps1` owning the restore pointer is item 10, and it forced a live pointer back to `0088` earlier for precisely that reason.

**And a gate that never fired was caught before it shipped.** The star assertion crashed the validator under StrictMode — `Where-Object` returns a scalar on a single match, so `.Count` threw on Stone Skin's one filled star and the validator died before reporting anything. **It read as a clean pass for two runs.** Found only by mutation-testing: **a gate that silently never fires is worse than no gate, because it is also a false assurance.**

**Items 5, 6, 7 and the reconciliation half of 8 remain open** — per-exchange execution rather than stored state, and not claimed closed.

**2026-08-19 — Three design flags closed in tooling rather than in prose (`EVT-000529`-`EVT-000530`).**

**The owner declined further flags and asked for the fixes.** No fictional time, no die, no scene.

**`F-030` — a template hard-coded a value canonical state may change.** §8.1's Reward row read a bare `Ability Points +3` while the Bearer held **Ascetic**, whose entire authored effect is *grants +4 instead of +3* — **a default dressed as layout**, with no angle brackets marking it state-derived, in a profile whose own §15.1 tells a Runtime to *fill the slots and change nothing else*. **All three render sites now carry `<+n>`**, and the two prose statements say where the number comes from and that it is read **at claim** rather than at issue.

**The generalising half is the §15.1 change.** That section had already adjudicated one template-versus-prose conflict in the templates' favour — *the templates are what a Runtime actually copies, so the templates win* — which is **right for frame geometry and actively harmful for values**. The two are now separated outright: **geometry is the template's, values are canonical state's, and a template stating a value as a literal is a defect rather than a default.**

**`F-031` question 3 — the context measurement reports its categories.** Every over-budget surface now splits into **duplicated-event-history / superseded-state / residue**, with the byte count `trim_policy.order` clears before anything in the residue is touched. *Owen is 92 KB, 45 KB of it duplicated chronicle* is a fix anyone can make correctly; *Owen is 92 KB* is a judgement call against a deadline, which is how a session came to cut character prose to buy seven bytes. **The split is a mechanical proxy and says so**, and it is taken **per line** — a first cut splitting per paragraph reported 66% of `skills_known` as superseded and would have told an operator to cut the skill definitions themselves. **It was used in the session it was written**: it found the history in `180_CURRENT_STATE.md` that cleared `/system log`, and it justified re-recording two baselines with attribution rather than cutting further.

**`F-015` / `EVT-000523` — the rendered-multiplier drift is now a gate.** A magnitude skill's multiplier is `max(native + 0.15 × (mastery_level − 1), magnitude_floor)`, **both terms already stored beside it**, and nothing recomputed it when mastery advanced — which left **three of four skill lines stale against their own counters**. `native_multiplier` is minted as a tracked counter (Rupture 200, Mend 100, Mana Bolt 100) and `tools/validate_repository.ps1` asserts the relation. **The ratchet is part of the relation, not an exception**, and the gate's first run proved it by flagging Rupture's floor-held ×2.60 until §7.2's `max()` was included. Mutation-tested. **Twin Fang and Dagger Mastery are deliberately excluded** — both carry their own authored formulas, and forcing them into this one would be inventing a rule to satisfy a check.

**Two context baselines re-recorded with attribution**, not waived: `/system skills` and `/system shop` both read `skills_known`, which gained **two genuinely new skill entries** and four ascension rewrites against a baseline set when the list held 21. Everything duplicated was cut first, taking `gatefall_skills_known` from 1,246 bytes an entry to under 1,100 — **below where the span started**.

**2026-08-19 — Profile 1.73: the world given a clock, and a dead class grant replaced (`EVT-000527`-`EVT-000528`).**

**The owner's complaint, and the count that settled it.** *Hidden quests are too narrow, progression takes very detailed conditions, some not even finished, and the Runtime always says the owner should author it — which is wrong, because he should not have to author what he wants to be surprised by.* Against the ledgers: **two non-daily quests in ninety in-fiction days**, both hidden, both `revealed: false`, **both attached to something he had already touched**; **two of four slots never used**; **four Gate breaks, none reaching him**; **zero red gates ever rolled**; **a tracked board at zero postings**. Three catalogue titles unearned for reasons about the world rather than about him.

**And §9.1 authorised it in its own words** — *the Runtime distributes them into the fiction as needed and does not roll a fixed calendar*. Thirty-five Gates a week in Chicago and a sentence permitting none of them to be rolled at him.

**Five edges.** A **daily world tick** at each 06:00 boundary rolling Gates, Ranks, **rarity every time**, breaks, posting regeneration and agenda initiations, independently of the Bearer, results binding. **Siting rolled `d100`** — 01-10 on his route, 11-30 within reach, 31-00 elsewhere — so **proximity is an input rather than a veto**. A **push channel** on both quest triggers, closing the mirror-only behaviour. **Cost expressions required to be total** (`F-032` q2). And §18.8's **pocket cache replaced by a standing anchor sense** (`F-033`), because the cache duplicated two containers he had carried for twenty levels and anchors three through five were otherwise inert — 1.67's Soulforged repair, one class over.

**Enforced, not promised.** `tools/validate_repository.ps1` now fails a campaign whose `world_clock.last_ticked` sits behind the most recent day boundary at or before its `campaign_time`. Mutation-tested: a clock set back three days reports four unrolled ticks. **First tick falls 2026-08-20 06:00.** Nothing is back-dated for the ninety days played before the tick existed.

**2026-08-19 — Profile 1.72, four rulings, three stale multipliers, four ascensions and seventeen points (`EVT-000521`-`EVT-000526`).**

**No fictional time passes anywhere in this span.** It is a profile adoption, a set of owner rulings, a rendering repair, four Section 7.5 breakthroughs and one stat allocation, all resolved at the same instant, all System bookkeeping. He is still standing inside the cleared Class Quest instance.

**Profile 1.71 → 1.72, three additive edges** (`EVT-000521`). **(1)** Section 18.8 priced a Riftwalker's anchored step off *the instance's Rank* while the same paragraph advertised anchors in a *contiguous stretch of the city* — and its own worked example anchors two of three points outside any instance. Raised as **`F-032`** and ruled: **the city case reads the Rank ladder at E-Rank, 5 Mana**, because ordinary city ground is the bottom of that ladder. The flat 6 was explicitly rejected — §18.8 names it as what an anchored step costs *instead of*. **(2)** Releasing an anchor was never authored; it is now **free, instant and at will**, so the cap is a working set rather than a set of permanent commitments — a correction the Runtime had itself mis-stated as a slot being *burned*. **(3)** **Every holding carries a Rank**, defaulting to its source's, and the System returning no category is a statement about *identification* rather than about Rank.

**Four rulings settled** (`EVT-000522`). The **Mana Reservoir 1:1 draw** and the **seven Phase 1 commons removed rather than killed** are both **confirmed** as authored in the trial. **`ascetic_claim_timing_evt_000357`, open since 2026-08-14, is ruled AT CLAIM** — which awards no retroactive point, because that entry's declared order was claim-then-equip, but changes everything after it: the equipped state at collection is what pays, so the title slot stops being a 06:00 deadline. **The frost-fused plating is ruled [B-Rank] × 5** under the new §11.1 rule, unidentified since `EVT-000502`.

**A player audit of one stale figure found a defect class** (`EVT-000523`). The Runtime had held Mana Bolt's Expert magnitude rung *unresolved* because its line asserts ×1.15 while also saying *Adept (+0.15) will be the first real gain past the floor*. The player's read — that the larger value is right and the contradiction is a refactor artefact — is correct, and it is not confined to Mana Bolt. **Every magnitude-multiplier skill resolves at `native + 0.15 × (mastery_level - 1)`**, and **Mend is the control**: native ×1.0, stored level 4, renders ×1.45, and restored exactly 15 a cast all trial. **Three of four rendered multipliers were stale against their own stored counters** — Rupture ×2.45 against ×2.60, Mana Bolt ×1.15 against ×1.45, Twin Fang ×1.80 against ×2.00. **The rendered multiplier is a derivation of two values the sheet already stores, and nothing recomputes it when mastery advances**; every gate passes because a stale number has nothing mechanical to disagree with. `F-015`'s family, but **deterministically checkable**, which most of that family is not. **No counter moved and no resolved outcome changed** — Rupture ran at 645 rather than the narrated 605 through the back half of the trial and one-shot the same commons.

**All four Section 7.5 offers taken** (`EVT-000524`), and only after the player asked the question that mattered: *is the next Rank authored, and is it actually better than mastery on the lower one?* Both checked before anything moved. **Rupture [B] Master → [A] Adept**, baseline 150 → 375, **`magnitude_floor` holding the multiplier at ×2.60** against A-Adept's authored ×2.30 — **DMG 689 → 1,274**, gaining **Propagating**. **Exploit Pattern** and **Keen Sense** both to **[A] Adept**, both computing *down* on scope to 7 and **held at 8 by the ratchet** — they bought **Anticipatory** and **Provenance**, categories rather than numbers, and the player was told so before approving. **Sprint [E] Master → [D] Adept**, gaining **Broken ground** and a **whole-scene window by Rank**, which retires its unauthored Master duration rung rather than answering it. **Section 7.2's *above Intelligence 105 the road inverts* warning was checked and belongs to the E→D rung** — `(25+I)×2.30 = (10+I)×2.60` solves at exactly 105; on B→A it inverts above 1,575.

**Seventeen points to Intelligence** (`EVT-000525`): base 95 → 112, effective 98 → 115, **maximum Mana 196 → 230**. **Current Mana holds at 144 and is not filled**, on the reading that the campaign's three prior allocations filled *because* both pools were at cap. **Combat Tier re-checked and unmoved at B** — `defense_tier` is pinned by `520 / 217.5 = 2.39`, so offence is free in tier terms and nothing imposed gets harder. Chosen over Vitality because **deflection converts Mana into avoided damage at roughly 52 Health a point against a critical**, versus Vitality's flat 4. **The sixth rift anchor was considered and declined** at +13 Agility for no combat value.

**2026-08-19 — The Class Quest entered, cleared, and a permanent class taken (`EVT-000511`-`EVT-000520`).**

**Twenty banked points were spent before the seal, and the reason is the whole shape of the trial.** §6.7 snapshots Combat Tier at **entry**, so anything allocated afterward cannot change what the trial is scaled to. Intelligence +10 and Vitality +10 took maximum Health 476 → **516** and maximum Mana 174 → **194** — and the tier was re-checked after, because it must be: Rupture at 605 pushed `offense_tier` to A, but `defense_tier` held at B on `516 ÷ 217.5 = 2.37` against a threshold of 5. **`max(C, min(A, B))` = B either way, so the allocation was pure gain**, and the player was shown that before spending rather than after.

**Terrain rolled Shattered City (d8 6): broken verticality, falls dealing Rank damage, ground usable as a weapon.** Five authored phases and a final foe, **§18.2.2 suspending potions, Status Recovery, pending rewards and all elapsed-time recovery** — one budget, no refill, no retreat.

**The trial resolved as a single scene, and that fact governs the whole settlement.** It was established at `EVT-000512` by Keen Sense's scene-long cast covering the entire trial, and confirmed by threshold absorption being spendable exactly once across all six phases. **Every mastery-tracked skill therefore takes at most one qualifying scene from it**, however many phases it ran through.

**Phase by phase.** **1 — The Converging:** eleven C-Rank commons on two bearings; Rupture dropped a canted floor slab on seven of them, and **they were ruled *removed from the phase* rather than killed** — Shattered City's C-Rank fall figure is 62 against 250 Health — which is why the phase paid 240 XP and not 660. A withdrawal into a dead-end corridor turned two bearings into one; Dimensional Projection put a longsword and a quickknife into two commons at ×4 each. **The only hit that landed all phase was a natural 100 through a one-metre gap, 144.** **2 — The Grind:** two B-Rank elites, 1,200 each, **killed in one action apiece by Twin Fang at 2,228**, for ten Mana and no Health. **3 — The Refusal:** the phase reads the damage record and halves whatever tops it — **it halved Twin Fang, at 5,148 against everything else combined, and he never swung it**, opening with a Mana Bolt and a Rupture because the geometry asked for it. **4 — The Ground:** the corridor inverted as the floor began to fail; **Overpower at A-Rank took the slab out from under him deliberately** and he rode it down. **5 — The Clock:** six B-Rank commons under a hard six-exchange limit, **cleared in exactly six Ruptures with no overrun**, the final foe handed nothing.

**Level 29 → 30 crossed mid-trial (`EVT-000514`), and it refilled both pools inside a trial that had suspended every other form of recovery.** §18.2.2's suspension list is **closed and specific** — potion, Status Recovery, pending reward, elapsed-time recovery — and a §3 level-up restoration is none of the four. **372 → 520/520 and 148 → 196/196**, which paid for the entire Endurance phase.

**System Rank C → B fired three things at once (`EVT-000515`).** The **skill ceiling rose B → A**, making Rupture, Exploit Pattern and Keen Sense breakthrough-eligible. **All five Stat Passives advanced B → A.** And **Level 30 is a Title Assessment**, granting **Giantslayer** (a boss above his System Rank, killed alone, sealed — `EVT-000501` that morning) and **The Unmeasured** (a card three Ranks below System Rank, never re-assessed — E against B), **+12 points, titles 10 → 12, slots 2 → 3**. Giantslayer was equipped and then swapped for **Centurion** once the trade was shown, because Phase 5 was about to field six commons and Centurion's +1 Mana each is the only refill the trial permits. **Executioner was recounted on player instruction and denied at roughly 20-25 of 50**, correcting a stored `~30`; the count is decidable because §3.3's underdog multiplier is applied *only* above System Rank.

**The final foe died to one Rupture.** Before it: the Mana Reservoir drawn 1:1 for 108, **a mechanic `EVT-000479` had left undefined and which is authored here and owed the player's confirmation**; a fumbled boss search bought eight Mends at fifteen Health a cast, 172 → **292**, which is what put him above its 348 critical; then **natural 61 → 100, ×4, 2,432 against 2,400**. **Eleven commons, five elites, six commons and a boss, on one budget, out at 292/520 and 144/196.**

**Signals: kill efficiency 8, battlefield control 6, weapon breadth 10, endurance 8, positioning 12.** Riftwalker offered on positioning, Soulforged Armory on weapon breadth — and **Herald revealed uncatalogued**, its §18.10 condition being at least one point in **all five** signals. Reliquary and Seam were both checked against the record and neither fired. **RIFTWALKER TAKEN, permanently** (`EVT-000519`): rift-step, 60% micro-rift deflection, a pocket cache, and **0 of 5 rift anchors** — none set inside a trial instance that collapses on exit.

**Four §7.5 Rank ascension offers stand open and unanswered** — Rupture, Exploit Pattern and Keen Sense on the ceiling lift, Sprint on reaching Master in the trial. **Two rulings owe confirmation** (the reservoir draw; the seven commons removed rather than killed). **Two Runtime errors are recorded rather than buried:** Shrug Off's A-Rank immunity was stated as covering everything B-Rank and below when the rung reads *more than one Rank below* — corrected in-scene, and it is why the Phase 5 critical landed — and **`maximum_mana` was narrated as 194 for six exchanges against a correct 196, with Rupture likewise left at 605 against 608**. Neither reached disk; **no resolved outcome, roll, kill or player decision turns on either**.

**Economy: nothing moved.** A §18 trial is entered by declaration and pays a class rather than drops. Gold **171,861**, cash **$281,155.22**, both unchanged. **XP +3,840. 17 unspent stat points stand unallocated.**

**2026-08-19 — Three Crypts run for training, and three breakthroughs on the earned road (`EVT-000506`-`EVT-000510`).**

**The ruling that shaped the whole span, and it was made before any key was spent.** Three **E-Rank** keys were declared and then revised. §7.4 states that a trivial or consequence-free test **adds a use but no qualifying scene**, and mastery advances on scenes, not uses. An E-Rank instance carries no genuine threat at Level 27 — 40-Health commons against a 167 main-hand, a ~6-damage strike against 468 Health — so three E-Rank clears would have moved `successful_uses` and **advanced nothing at all**. C-Rank is content at his own System Rank, with campaign precedent at `EVT-000469`-`EVT-000476`. **30,000 g of C-Rank keys bought eleven mastery advances and three breakthroughs; 1,500 g of E-Rank keys would have bought loot.**

**148 B-Rank crystals sold for 111,000 g** to fund it (`EVT-000506`), holding 50 back.

**Three instances, and all three rolled archetype 1 — Crypt** (`EVT-000507`). A 1-in-512 run on the d8. Darkness throughout, meaning a Perception check against ambush entering every combat: **nine checks, no failures**, with **Cartographer finally earning its slot** — its second clause pays on actions that exploit or evade the archetype's twist, and the Crypt's twist is exactly that.

**Sixty-six attacks came back and two landed** — naturals **99** and **96**, in separate instances, 144 apiece through 42% reduction, **each absorbed by Shrug Off's threshold absorption as damage-only**. Two Status Recoveries restored both pools between clears (18 → 16). Nothing else reached a partial.

**Extraction was resolved automatically rather than rolled, and the Event records that it was.** §4.3 resolves a declared method automatically where capability clearly guarantees it, and a Bearer who took a 144-crystal B-Rank vein on a legendary read in fifty minutes is not uncertain against 44 C-Rank crystals in a two-hour window. **181 C-Rank crystals and 9 C-Rank cores.**

**XP +4,800 and deliberately no underdog bonus** — C-Rank content at System Rank C is *at* his Rank, so §3.3's multiplier is ×1 rather than the ×2 the B-Rank instance paid. **Level 27 → 29**, pools re-derived and restored twice to **476/476** and **174/174**, unspent points **10 → 20**. **Level 30 is System Rank B and is 1,895 XP away.**

A collapsed net −1 would have hidden both, and reaching Master is what created the eligibility.

Multiplier +0.95, chassis **×1.70**. **This was the free and strictly better version of the 112,500 g Premium rune still sitting on the tab** — §7.5 returns a breakthrough at Adept and a rune at Novice, and says so outright.
- **Twin Fang [D-Rank] → [C-Rank]**, gaining **Through the answer**: the follow-up resolves against a target that has already reacted, blocked or committed. Follow-up **×2.00**, Mana 4 → 5. Twin Fang stood at 446 this morning and stands at **557**.
- **Resonance Extraction [E-Rank] → [D-Rank]**, gaining **Cores and drops** — reads the harvestable structure of a beast core. **The first thing this skill has ever done outside a mine**, on the day he is carrying 49 beast cores into an artificer's workshop. Scope held at 5 by the ratchet, which computes 4 and takes the floor.

**Dimensional Projection finally moved its axis.** Novice → Practiced: **projectiles 1 → 2, range 5 m → 7 m, Mana 6 → 5.** The first point at which the skill does something Mana Bolt cannot, and exactly what the audit that ratified it predicted would be required.

Gold **171,861**; cash unchanged. **20 unspent stat points stand unallocated**, and the frost-fused plating of `EVT-000502` still stands unruled.

**2026-08-19 — Flash Step breaks through to B-Rank, and the offer had been suppressed (`EVT-000504`).**

Reaching Master at `EVT-000503` created a Section 7.5 breakthrough threshold, and **the barrier that recorded the mastery advance did not re-check the threshold that advance created.** It surfaced one exchange later, only because the player asked why the skill rendered *complete*. **This is the second suppressed breakthrough offer in this campaign** — Rupture and Exploit Pattern were both found sitting at Master with offers never presented at `EVT-000468`. The profile's own rule is that a mandatory threshold is re-checked from stored state at every declared boundary, *precisely because the delta that should dispatch it is the one that goes unwritten*, and that is exactly how it failed again.

**Taken: [C-Rank] Master → [B-Rank] Adept.** Gained **Through the ward** — the step crosses a closed ward or barrier-effect, held read of the far side still required — and retained Through the barrier at C-Rank. `mastery_level` 5 → 3, `rank_ascensions` 1 → 2, progress 0/3, six qualifying scenes back to Master. It still never crosses a Gate boundary, a Section 9.6 seal, or a Section 17 instance wall at any Rank.

**The ruling worth keeping is what was *not* built.** The Runtime proposed authoring two new counter kinds — `duration_floor` and `mana_floor` — to stop the whole-scene window and the 5-Mana cost regressing when mastery reset. **Both proposals were wrong, and reading two more paragraphs of the profile is what showed it.** Section 7.2 states that mastery's duration track terminates at whole scene and that **Rank saturates duration immediately at one Rank above native**; Flash Step is native D-Rank and has been above native since `EVT-000220`, so its window is Rank-derived and a mastery reset cannot touch it. And Section 7.5 names its ratchets as **the scope and magnitude ratchets** — cost is excluded deliberately, because breakthrough's entire price is mastery levels and cheaper casting is one of the things mastery buys. **Mana 5 → 6 is the design, not a defect.** No counter kind was created and none was needed.

No fictional time, no scene, no pool movement — Health 468/468 and Mana 170/170 unchanged, anchor held at 09:25 inside the cleared instance.
