**2026-08-17 — Profile 1.66: three annotations that stopped describing their own ladders (`EVT-000462`).**
Compatibility treatment over frozen 1.65, at the same 19:45 anchor as `EVT-000460` and `EVT-000461`. **Three cells in Section 7.3's magnitude-axis ladder described their own arithmetic and had stopped matching it.** Each grant cell carries two hand-written annotations - the **dominance ratio** the grant produces at each Rank step, and a word, *thin* or *dominant*, classifying it against Decision 090 point 5's 1.15× threshold. Neither is a rule; both are consequences of increments authored elsewhere in the same profile. **`F-014` predicted the class and the trigger turned out not to be the one it named:** not a retuned increment but the ladder's **extent**, **1.53 having authored A-Rank** across all three Section 7.3 tables and added a fourth reachable step the annotations never gained. **Dagger Mastery** stated three ratios where four steps exist, now `1.17× / 1.09× / 1.06× / 1.05×`. **Stone Skin** stated two where three exist, now `1.10× / 1.07× / 1.00×` - the last held flat by the Section 7.2 90% cap rather than a failed grant (B-Rank Master `30+50+20=100` and A-Rank Novice `30+75=105`, both clamped to 90). **Bulwark** stated no ratio and placed the cap a Rank late, now `1.06× / 1.00× / 1.00× / 1.00×` with the cap **first binding at D-Rank Master** (`60+25+20=105`) and holding every level from C-Rank up, so only its E→D step gains magnitude at all. **Nothing about the ladders moved** - every increment, native Rank, mastery step, cap and category is exactly what 1.65 held, and `tools/test_rank_dominance_law.ps1` verified the Rank Dominance Law before and after with no computed value changing. **Nothing retroactive:** no past ascension is re-adjudicated and none was resolved on a wrong number, the arithmetic having been correct throughout while only its description was stale; no offer reopens and no campaign owes a re-audit. What changes is that every stated ratio is **recomputed and compared on each run**, with Rupture and Mana Bolt excluded because they read effective Intelligence and no profile-side figure can own that. No pool, XP, Stat, skill, counter, gold, cash, item, or resolved outcome moved. `profile_version` 1.65 → **1.66**; see `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.65_to_1.66.md`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000462`.

**2026-08-17 — Profile 1.65: a cover story that was never limited by size (`EVT-000461`).**
Compatibility treatment over frozen 1.64, at the same 19:45 anchor as `EVT-000460`. Section 19.3 closed on *'Reawakening explains one jump; it cannot explain a curve'* — and ***one jump* reads as a bound on size**, which it was never meant to be. **It was misread exactly that way in play**, and nothing else in the section contradicted the misreading. **The section's own fiction settles it the other way:** reawakening is *'a correction of a card mismeasured at Awakening'* and *'the original card was wrong all along'* — and **a wrong measurement has no natural size**, so a wider gap makes the original appraiser's error more embarrassing rather than the correction less believable. **Ruled:** the classification is **not magnitude-limited** — an E-Rank card re-measuring at a B-Rank pool is exactly as admissible a claim as one re-measuring at D, and there is no size at which a regulator refuses it on size alone — and **what it cannot absorb is repetition**, a second correction on one card ceasing to be an error and becoming a pattern the instruments are not permitted to have. The constraint falls on the **number of claims, never the distance any one covers**; the closing line now reads *a jump of any size, never a series*. A wide gap still costs **attention**, which Section 19.1 already prices, and Sections 19.1, 19.2 and 19.4 are otherwise untouched. **Sharper than the question that raised it:** Alexander is a carded E-Rank at **140/140 maximum Mana**, which Section 19.2's table places in the **S-Rank band (120+)** — under the misreading the only sanctioned cover the world has was unusable at exactly his real magnitude, five bands above his card, and the whole Section 19 seam was closed to him. Nothing about his position moves: still barred from C-and-above contracts by his own card, no reawakening claimed, no re-assessment requested. **Folded-in repair rather than a separate Event:** `100_CHARACTER_SHEET.md`'s Field Command line read *'to one ally … before the ally's roll'* — the E-Rank Novice wording — while the same line recorded *'Currently 3 recipients, on the Adept rung'*; the prose never carried forward when the scope axis moved, stating the skill's floor and its live value in one sentence. Repaired to the rendering Silent Step and Resonance Extraction already use. `F-015`'s shape, its counters all correct. No pool, XP, Stat, skill, counter, gold, cash, item, or resolved outcome moved. `profile_version` 1.64 → **1.65**; see `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.64_to_1.65.md`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000461`.

**2026-08-17 — Profile 1.64: what *"capable"* means, and the ceiling it was hiding (`EVT-000460`).**
Compatibility treatment over frozen 1.63, at the freight-yard anchor immediately after the training closed. **The crew asked a question the profile could not answer.** With five B-Ranks and six C-Ranks standing in the yard, Section 9.4's legal minimum for a B-Rank Gate reads **`8 (B-Rank-capable, incl. ≥1 B)`** — and **nothing in the profile defined *capable***, the prose above the table saying only *combat-rated hunters of adequate rank* and never authoring adequacy. Read strictly the minimum wants eight carded B-Ranks and the crew is three short; read permissively it wants eight hunters capable of B-Rank work with at least one carded B, and the crew clears it. **The file chose neither, and the difference is the entire Rank ceiling of the campaign.** **The `≥1 B` clause is itself the evidence:** redundant if all eight must be carded B, meaningful only if the other seven need not be — the identical construction the A-Rank row uses and the C-Rank row omits. **Ruled: a hunter counts toward a Gate's legal minimum when their card Rank is at the Gate's Rank or one Rank below it, and nothing else.** A C-Rank counts toward a B-Rank Gate's eight; a D-Rank toward a C-Rank Gate's four; an E-Rank toward neither. **Capability is a card threshold, not a judgment** — a minimum resting on opinion would be unenforceable at the mouth and unprovable in a hearing, which is why the law counts cards and not competence, exactly as Section 19's re-assessment measures a Mana pool rather than asking anyone how good they are. The `≥1` clauses prevent an all-lower-tier party; **C-Rank carries none deliberately**, so four D-Ranks may lawfully enter a C-Rank Gate. **One Rank and not two**, Section 2 fixing Rank at Awakening and Section 4 making Rank the whole of capability. **What it means here:** the crew **clears a B-Rank Gate's legal minimum** — eleven combat-rated against a floor of eight — though not the 14-18 posting floor, and **Alexander stays barred from C-and-above contracts by his own E-Rank card** (Section 19.4), riding as support. **The crew is not muscle he lacks but the only lawful route to B-Rank content he has:** Section 12.5 stocks keys only at or below his System Rank C, so a B-Rank key is unbuyable until level 30, and a B-Rank Gate is the only place one can drop. **Nothing retroactive** — no past entry is re-adjudicated and Wade Bishop's four-against-eight stands exactly as `EVT-000384` records it. No pool, XP, Stat, skill, counter, gold, cash, or item moved. `profile_version` 1.63 → **1.64**; see `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.63_to_1.64.md`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000460`.

Nothing confirms danger, nothing confirms he is nothing, and the absence is consistent with a careful operator rather than evidence of anything. **The door is open and unopened** — no approach, no call, no watch, and his name has not been mentioned to Owen, Fenn, or Nakamura. **A ledger correction found in the same check, running in Alexander's favor for once:** `120_INVENTORY_AND_OWNERSHIP.md` recorded Wade Bishop's **$221,540** as *'three days out on a stated 1-2 business day window'* while **counting calendar days across a weekend**. 2026-08-14 is a **Friday**; the 15th and 16th clear nothing. **Monday 2026-08-17 is the first business day of that window** — the payout is due today or tomorrow and is **late on neither**, and the Saturday-filed Cicero Scar cut sits the same way. **2026-08-17 — The silent partnership, and a knife out of nowhere (`EVT-000447`, `OBJ-29`).**
Twenty minutes across a counter that changed a thirteen-day relationship's kind. **Alexander named the problem rather than making an ask:** the arrangement was not working — Walt cannot want help with Marnie and also wait for Alexander to arrive holding something valuable first, and three days of sitting on Prykowski's name was the proof. He offered a replacement instead of an ultimatum. **The instrument: not a loan, not charity, no paper signed.** Alexander funds scale; **Walt runs the floor.** Terms both directions — Walt goes bigger and **outbids other grey-market dealers**, building toward a **monopoly on mana-touched goods specifically**; Alexander takes a **profit share** and **standing first refusal** on anything interesting crossing the counter; **partners, no undercuts, no secrets**, the third aimed squarely at the three withheld days and understood as such; and **Walt's own added term, offered rather than extracted** — anything carrying real money or real risk, Alexander hears **before** it happens. **Walt priced the real cost before agreeing, and that is the substantive thing he did:** going bigger means being **seen**, he is careful precisely because nobody important has reason to look twice at an old man's pawn shop, and mana-touched material is the exact category that made a thirty-year supplier hang up on him in fear. He asked for structure that keeps them both off a radar — **agreed as a requirement and never designed**, a genuine open item. **Capital: $40,000 cash**, his own costed figure for a real result inside a week (storage, two vouched people, stock bought outright rather than brokered), delivered the same visit. **Cash $42,574.00 → $2,574.00.** Asked whether crystals would move faster he said no and explained why — liquidating material through the same channels he is trying to expand is *using the ladder you're building to reach the ladder* — so **cash starts it and material becomes stock once it runs.** **The demonstration is a knowing Section 19 exposure:** before Walt could reach for the money, Alexander opened an empty hand and **withdrew the Quickknife [C-Rank] into the air above the counter, caught it falling, and set it down beside the cash** — deliberate, for trust and a measured amount of pressure. **Walt did not flinch**, said it was not needed for trust, and admitted it **answered a question he had been too polite to ask for weeks**; the fourth time he has noticed something and declined to pursue it, and the first that was unambiguous rather than sleight. He pocketed the cash without counting it. He now holds first-hand knowledge that Alexander stores and retrieves objects from nowhere, and **no channel whatsoever to the System by name, to Flux Sight, or to anything read off his own counter.** **`REL-000069` recharacterized `informal-fence` → `business-partnership`; `OBJ-29` opens with a first demonstrable result due 2026-08-24** — though Walt's first act after being paid was to widen his own deadline to *inside two weeks*, which the objective records rather than adopts. **The thing under it that neither of them named:** Walt's reason for wanting cores is now disclosed and personal, and his operation runs on Alexander's money — genuine leverage, of exactly the kind Alexander holds over Wade Bishop, over a man who has done nothing to earn being held. No die, no Mana, no combat. Registry allocation: `EVT-000447`.

**2026-08-17 — Walt Adamik's counter: a core given, a granddaughter named, a name handed over (`EVT-000446`).**
No combat, no die, no Mana. **Marnie: nothing from either direction** — no word from her, no new lead of Walt's own, no second push at his silent supplier; told Fenn had produced nothing yet, he was unsurprised and defended her (*good doesn't mean fast on something this quiet*). **Two items read on sight**, Flux Sight's B-rung item appraisal exercised twice at **Tier 1 — no roll, no Mana** (Section 14.5.4), so the counter moves and nothing else does: a **Perimeter Ping [D-Rank]** (silent Gate-instability alert within ~200 m, no combat function, **$1,400, not purchased**) and a **Mana Reservoir [C-Rank]** — a genuine **unbound** passive Mana battery, asked at $2,800 and **bought at $2,500** after Walt came down once and then honestly declined to move further, with his standing buy-back at cost. **Its capacity and draw rate are unauthored and no figure is asserted** (Section 14.2). **Cash $45,074.00 → $42,574.00.** **A C-Rank Beast Core given rather than sold** — stock **9 → 8** — the material Walt named as his real want at `EVT-000157` and had never been offered since. Asked what it was worth *to him*, his first two answers were poor (a restatement of terms already granted, then a discount on Cicero's Quiet, declined as a loadout downgrade). **Pressed a third time, he stopped selling and disclosed:** his granddaughter **Nadia Adamik (`ENT-000207`)**, **Gate-touched — not awakened** — roughly three years ago; nothing licensed has helped, BGM does not cover a non-combat injury, and he has been told quietly that the right artificer working **high-Rank cores specifically** could build something that **helps, not cures** — a distinction he repeated. **Marnie was going to be that artificer.** That is the whole of the want-list he has deflected since `EVT-000157`, and the reason her silence was never business to him. **Alexander refused the disclosure as payment** — a personal confidence is not consideration — and Walt agreed, named it as getting in his own head, and produced the actual trade: **Sal Prykowski (`ENT-000206`)**, the supplier who went silent on CONSULT at `EVT-000352`, name and rough location on a scrap of paper, **withheld for three days**. **The confrontation is the scene's real content.** Put to him without softening — *you told me Marnie is the one who could help your granddaughter, and when I offered to find her you protected the man who probably knows why she's gone* — **Walt did not defend it.** He named it as fear rather than judgment, admitted he had told himself he was protecting a source while actually hoping the danger would resolve before he had to hand it on, conceded it made no sense against his own stake, and offered no version in which he came out well: *scared people protect the fear first and the thing they actually care about second.* **Alexander made no commitment** regarding Nadia, the cores, or Prykowski. Registry allocations: `EVT-000446`, `ENT-000206`, `ENT-000207`.

**2026-08-17 — The twenty-fourth daily, and Owen up first (`EVT-000445`).**
Daily quest issues 06:00 in the 1.63 frame, **reward line reading Ability Points +4** — the first daily in the campaign to issue at the Ascetic rate, the title having gone on the previous evening rather than mid-day. **A Character First for Owen, small on purpose:** he was **awake before Alexander and had made coffee**, which has never happened — the established shape of their mornings is Alexander waking him and Owen resisting it on principle. He refused to make anything of it (*'I got tired of waiting for you to do it… Turns out I can just get up'*) and undercut it with *don't get used to it*; recorded under Decision 076 as an observable break from an established pattern, his own doing. He was kissed awake anyway on the second pass and gave up the protest without pretending otherwise; the rest is not narrated, as this relationship's intimacy always has been. **Regimen run late and clean** — full calisthenics plus ten kilometres along the lake, Owen keeping pace throughout, unremarkable now and impossible three weeks ago. **Complete ~09:30. Streak 9 → 10**, not a multiple of seven, so a standard box. **Three pending entries, none claimed:** Ability Points **+4** × 1, Status Recovery × 1 (15 → 16), Daily Random Box × 1. No XP (Section 8.1), no Mana (the objectives are physical work, not skill use), Health 420/420 throughout. The day then splits by agreement — Owen downtown to pick a lunch place, Alexander to the warehouse district; Owen also caught himself out on having built Walt up entirely from descriptions of a man he has never met, and conceded it at once. **2026-08-16 — The walk home, and a title swapped before the clock (`EVT-000444`).**
No combat, no hazard, no die, no Mana. The second Crypt's exit deposits Alexander and Owen on Rogers Park sidewalk in early evening and they walk home; **phone checked at 17:19** — nothing from Wade, Nakamura or Fenn, the $221,540 still reading *processing*. **Monday planned jointly:** Owen is off until Wednesday, so Walt's, lunch and **his boat** against the 18:00 crew training, with his own practical additions (keep Walt under an hour; get fed). **The mechanically consequential part is the title loadout, and it started as a defect.** `100_CHARACTER_SHEET.md`'s `title` field still read **Ascetic + Prospector** — the `EVT-000357` first loadout — having never been rewritten when the live pair became **Cartographer + Untouchable** before the first Crypt (`EVT-000428` span). **`180_CURRENT_STATE.md` carried the correct pair for a full session while the sheet rendered the superseded one**: `F-015`'s exact shape, a stored value with nothing mechanical positioned to disagree with it, and the same class as `EVT-000439`'s four stale Stat Passive rows. **Repaired to the live pair, then Untouchable swapped out for Ascetic** on the player's declared instruction. **The timing is the point:** Ascetic grants `+4` Ability Points instead of `+3` (Section 16.2) and Section 3.9 stores that value in the pending entry **at issue**, so equipping before the 06:00 boundary rather than after makes the next morning's grant unambiguously +4 — and is why the `ascetic_claim_timing_evt_000357` seam, which cost a point last time, never arises. Slots remain 2 of 2; a third opens at the level-30 assessment. No Stat, pool, XP, gold, cash, or item moved. Registry allocation: `EVT-000444`.

**2026-08-16 — Profile 1.63: one System, one window (`EVT-000443`).**
Compatibility treatment over frozen 1.62, at the same anchor as `EVT-000441`/`EVT-000442`. Player question, and the right one: *is the Flux Sight template the same as the quest templates and the other System messages?* **It was not.** A **summoned panel** was a framed window — `╔══ ◈  S Y S T E M ══…`, interior column 6, bracketed Ranks, a fixed label vocabulary, a status gutter. A **fired notification** was plain text wearing a `[SYSTEM]` prefix and sharing none of it. Nothing in the fiction justified the split: the same System, the same Bearer, the same interface, looking like two things depending only on whether he had asked. **The prefix was load-bearing in the wrong direction** — cheap enough to attach to any shape at all, which is how **four mutually incompatible message shapes** accumulated beneath one label: an inline tuple (the read, level-up, mastery), labelled rows (the three quest kinds), an unlabelled record list (the five Section 18 class panels), and nested indented sub-blocks (the Title Assessment). **`EVT-000442`'s own Section 14.5.3 had declared two of them a contract** and closed with *'neither shape is available to the other'* — describing half its subject matter and stated as though it covered all of it. That is the defect this edge repairs; the visual split is what made it visible. **1.63 retires the prefix and renders every System utterance in the Section 15.1 frame.** What remains is **one render and two forms**, divided by what the System is *doing*: **the window** is what it **presents** — a read, an event, an offer, an assessment, a panel — framed always, its top rule carrying the event name where a panel carries `S Y S T E M`, and its first content row carrying the subject; **the ledger line** is what it **counts** while an action resolves — a pool or XP moving, on the Bearer or on a target he acted upon — unframed but **indented to interior column 6**, the window's own content column. **A window is an occasion and may cost three rows; a ledger line fires several times an exchange**, and framing each would put forty rules through a single dungeon fight. **The frame marks what the System chose to say; the indent marks what it is merely keeping.** A read is accordingly **one window however many subjects it covers**, and the fight after it is ledger lines. **Two long-standing defects fell out of conforming every site** rather than restating the contract: the Daily quest rendered **`Objectives:`/`Rewards:`** where Urgent and Hidden rendered **`Objective:`/`Reward:`** — one field under two names on the same surface since 1.0, now one, and a label inside a window is never punctuated with a colon; and the **Section 18 class panels rendered Rank as a bare letter** (`Gravecut · D · Field`) against Section 15.1's own *'a bare letter is never rendered'*, now bracketed everywhere. **Section 15.1's frame rule is corrected against its own templates** — it stated that no rule is closed on the right while every template in 15.2–15.4 closed its inner section rules on `╢`; the templates are what a Runtime copies, so only an inner rule closes. **Nothing stored moves, nothing is retroactive, and no promoted Event is re-rendered** — a notification's shape has never been an input to any resolution. `profile_version` 1.62 → **1.63**; see `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.62_to_1.63.md`.

**2026-08-16 — Profile 1.62: the Flux Sight read is bounded to news (`EVT-000442`).**
Compatibility treatment over frozen 1.61, at the same anchor as `EVT-000441`. **1.61 authored the read for a Bearer meeting a monster and then applied that cadence to everyone.** Player question the same day — *would it fire when I meet an awakened NPC too?* — and it does: the **C rung reads any perceived awakened**, and Alexander's Flux Sight stands at B. But 1.61's cadence was *full read at first perception in a scene*, extended from Section 14.6's designation-color rule **without accounting for a familiar NPC standing in the Bearer's own kitchen.** Owen Callahan lives with him, so every domestic scene would have opened with a combat-style stat block on his partner, restating a number that had not moved in days. **Three rules follow.** *(1)* **New Section 14.5.6, *first meeting then on change*:** the full read fires the first time he ever perceives someone, wherever that happens; afterwards it renders only when a value has **materially moved** since his last read of that person — Health or Mana away from where he last saw it, a changed Rank, a condition the pools carry — and an unchanged subject renders nothing across any number of scenes. **The passive is not narrowed and Section 4.4 is untouched**: Flux Sight is always in effect and he always knows; what is bounded is when the System *says so*, the same principle already governing the color (*'not repeated each exchange'*) and a target's pools in 14.5.5. **It is the more useful rule, not merely the quieter one** — a line restating an unchanged number carries no information, and a reader who learns to skip it is exactly the reader a real change slips past; under this rule a rendered line always carries news, and an injured friend coming through the door is the case it fires on. *(2)* **A populated room renders one room line, then individuals on engagement** — a guild hall or a bar emits a single line of the room's shape and counts by Rank with each Rank's color, then a full individual read on anyone he engages, is approached by, singles out, or fights; the room line carries no pools, a count of strangers having none to state. That is 1.61's grouping rule applied to people rather than monsters. *(3)* **Section 14.5.3 states the bracketed head grammar** rather than leaving it to pattern-matching: every bracketed message shares `[SYSTEM] <EVENT NAME IN CAPS> — <subject>`, and what differs below the head differs **by a rule** — a **fixed tuple renders inline on the head row** because the fields are always the same fields in the same order, a **variable field set renders as labelled rows** because the reader cannot know which are present. A read is one row; a quest is labelled rows; neither shape is available to the other. **Nothing is granted, no NPC record gains a field**, and *has he read this person before* is answered from the campaign's existing record of who he has met. Prospective from adoption — no backfill is owed, and a first read after adoption is correct behavior rather than a defect. `profile_version` 1.61 → **1.62**; see `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.61_to_1.62.md`.

**2026-08-16 — Profile 1.61: Flux Sight gets the authorization to fire and the template to fire into (`EVT-000441`).**
Compatibility treatment over frozen 1.60, raised by **`F-017` item 4** — the passive withheld across a whole combat session as though it required an active check. **The Runtime was following the rule that existed.** Section 14.3's trigger tiers are the entire authorization list for what the System says unprompted and Tier 3 forbids firing *'with no declared precondition at all'*; Tier 1 named the **designation color**, Tier 2 the **B-rung item appraisal**, and the **D and C rungs appeared in no tier whatsoever** — no authorization to fire, and a standing prohibition against firing anyway. **A second hole sat beside it:** Section 4.4 authored *what* Flux Sight reads and **nothing authored how it renders**, the only System output in the profile without an exact template, so it was re-invented at each use — three shapes in one campaign, the rule citation leaking into the fiction twice, and **Mana dropped in two of three** against a rung authoring Rank, Health and Mana as one read. **Settled:** every rung is **Tier 1, fires on sight, no roll and no Mana**, the item appraisal promoted to join them; **the read and the designation color are one line**, the color computed from the Rank the read states, firing alone only where Flux Sight does not cover a creature whose Rank is resolved, and a Gate taking no color at all; **Section 14.5 becomes the contract** — one grammar and two weights, bare for the Bearer's own high-frequency numbers and `[SYSTEM]` for anything carrying a subject; **the XP line names its award source**, which is what `F-017`'s third item lacked; a target's pools re-render in any exchange the Bearer's action moved them; pool labels are `HP`/`MP`/`XP` on every surface, the 2026-07-30 no-abbreviation ruling **scoped** to the skills table's cost column rather than overturned. **Why this entry is dated after the profile it adopts.** 1.61 was authored and committed world-side while the campaign side was never completed — no adoption Event, no changelog entry, no registry row, and Current State reading 1.60 against a character sheet already reading 1.61 — and **repository validation was green throughout**, because `profile_version` is a stored string with nothing to disagree with it and a missing Event is not a value any gate compares. **`F-015` in its structural form, the fourth time this session.** `profile_version` 1.60 → **1.61**; see `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.60_to_1.61.md`.

**2026-08-16 — Profile 1.60: education is capability, not a skill (`EVT-000440`).**
Owner ruling **rejecting `applied_analytical_reconstruction`** under Section 7.1's rejection route and retiring **First Principles**, the result 1.58 pre-authored for it. **This closes a finding this session opened four Events ago, and closes it against itself.** Player objection, and it is fatal rather than corrective: *the Ranking now actually fights his own knowledge he learned at the university.* **The native effect duplicated a modifier Alexander already receives.** Both evidence scenes resolved on the engine's standing **established-capability** step — `EVT-000372` records it in its own text as *'+1 step for the directly relevant established capability'*, and `EVT-000189` shows the identical shape elsewhere (*'net +1 (established capability at reading people, no specific skill applies)'*). That step fires for competence a character actually holds and needs **no skill whatsoever**. Alexander is a university-trained mathematician and physicist; sightlines, load paths, sweep cones and structural extent are what that training **is**. **So the skill would have granted nothing** — Section 4.3 caps the net step at +1, Section 7.2 forbids a second modifier from the same finding, and on the exact two scenes that formed the candidate it would have changed **no roll, no margin, no resolved outcome.** That is the **Rank Dominance Law failing at acquisition rather than ascension**: the skill could do nothing its own absence could not. **Three mastery axes were tried and all three failed for the same root reason**, which is how the defect was finally isolated instead of patched: **beneficiaries** (1.58's choice) priced a solitary analytical method as a group capability; **concurrent held derivations** rebuilt **Multitask's** retired defect exactly, a capacity ladder no play ever reaches, removed at 1.49 on the finding that *'a Rank step that raises an unreached cap grants nothing a Bearer can notice'*; and **scale**, starting at *one wall*, asserted a floor **beneath** what he had already demonstrated with no skill at all — at `EVT-000372` he mapped four cameras' cones across a whole building and found a four-foot gap. Ratification would have been a **downgrade dressed as progression**. There was no capability to grow because none was being granted. **`EVT-000405` was right for a reason it did not quite articulate**: it declined to open a candidate with *'No new repeatable method appeared this span'*, and `EVT-000436` called that a miss — read again, **no new method appeared because the method was never new.** The audit's own finding is withdrawn rather than defended. **The durable rule:** a System skill is a capability the world does not otherwise grant; where a Bearer's background already resolves an action, the established-capability step is the correct and complete pricing of it, and Section 7.1's third test — *distinct capability*, excluding generic competence — is exactly what keeps it out of formation. **Education carries no Rank, no Mana, no mastery track and no System Rank ceiling, and applies whenever directly relevant — strictly better than any E-Rank passive.** Alexander loses nothing; he keeps the thing that was always doing the work. **What changed:** candidate → `rejected` with reason and `resolution_event`, **evidence preserved unchanged**; First Principles removed from Sections 7.2/7.3/7.4; the trigger manifest's `pre_authored_result_keys` returns to `dimensional_weapon_control` alone; Section 7.2 keeps the *reasoning* under a rejected-candidate heading so the next reader does not reopen the same candidate on the same evidence. **Nothing unwound** — First Principles existed only as profile text, never on any ledger, so no counter, mastery, Mana, Rank or scope value moves. **Dimensional Projection is untouched and remains ratified and live.** Registry allocation: `EVT-000440`. Updated live targets: `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`, `worlds/gatefall/206_WORLD_RULE_PROFILE.md`, `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.59_to_1.60.md`, `worlds/gatefall/migrations/INDEX.md`, `tools/test_progression_policy.ps1`.

**2026-08-16 — Four Stat Passives were reading yesterday's stats (`EVT-000439`).**
Player-directed audit prompted by `tools/test_gatefall_quest_contract.ps1` failing on Conduit. **The test throws on its first failing row, so the finding is four rows rather than one.** Section 4.4 renders a Stat Passive with its governing **base** Stat, and base Stats moved twice since these lines were last written: `EVT-000426` allocated **+8 Intelligence and +5 Agility**, and `EVT-000431`'s Level 23 grant added **+1 to all five**. **Corrected: Conduit Intelligence 59 → 68** (missed both allocations), **Pre-empt Agility 55 → 61** (missed both), **Overpower Strength 62 → 63** and **Shrug Off Vitality 101 → 102** (each missed the Level 23 grant). **Flux Sight Perception 62 was already correct**, stated rather than passed over, because the finding is that four rows drifted and one did not. **No Rank moves and no capability changes today:** Section 4.4 reads the highest threshold met (30/36/44/54/66/80 → E/D/C/B/A/S) then clamps by System Rank + 1, System Rank is **C**, so the ceiling is **B** and all five were already there and stay there. Every held-rung clause was independently correct — **[A-Rank]** for Conduit, Overpower, Pre-empt and Flux Sight, **[S-Rank]** for Shrug Off, whose Vitality 102 clears the 80 threshold exactly as 101 did. **Conduit's +20 points at B is unchanged, so no Section 5.2 recovery rate moves and no settled span is re-derived.** **Why no gate caught it, which is the part worth keeping:** Section 4.4 makes a Stat Passive's Rank **canonical derived state, never a separately stored value**, so there is no stored Rank for a stale Stat to contradict; and the figure sits in a `skills_known` prose line rather than in `tracked_counters`, so no `counter_deltas` arithmetic reaches it either. Both halves of the usual defence are structurally absent at once — **an unclaimed delta cannot disagree with a stored value, and a derived value cannot disagree with its own input.** `F-015` in its purest form, and the reason a contract test asserting the *derivation* rather than a pinned literal is the only thing that could have found it. **What it would have cost:** three of the four would have landed correctly anyway at level 30 — Overpower, Pre-empt and Shrug Off derive the same rung at both figures — but **Conduit is a real one.** Intelligence 68 clears the A-Rank threshold of 66 and 59 does not, so the stale row would have taken **B-Rank (+20 points) where the true Stat earns A-Rank (+25)** the instant the System Rank ceiling lifted, a capability already bought and banked under Section 4.4's held-intact rule and silently underpaid. Read-back repair on the `EVT-000420` precedent; no fiction, no die, no XP, level, Stat, pool, gold, cash, item, quest, title, counter, or resolved outcome. Registry allocation: `EVT-000439`. Updated live targets: `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`.

**2026-08-16 — Profile 1.59: the half of the pocket dimension nobody wrote down (`EVT-000438`).**
Player observation on reading `EVT-000436`'s ratified skill back: ***'the launched weapon stays where it lands' does not survive a Bearer who can store anything.*** Correct, and the defect is larger than the sentence. **The pocket dimension's two directions were never symmetric, and only one was written.** Section 12.9 bounds **withdrawal** explicitly — *to his own hand*, with remote destinations denied by name — while **storage carried no stated bound at all**; its whole text was *he may send an item to the inventory … from anywhere*. Section 7.2 nonetheless asserted the pre-skill inventory was **hand-delivery only in both directions**, on the strength of a sentence Section 12.9 never wrote, and the play record had already contradicted it: **`EVT-000326` swept three crates *and a workbench* into the pocket dimension on one roll.** **The omission was load-bearing, which is why this is a required migration rather than a wording fix.** It was the only justification for Dimensional Projection's recovery cost, and therefore for whether the technique's **D rung granted anything at all** — *Recall* promised to end *the ordinary recovery cost of using the technique*, but if storage already worked at range Alexander could do exactly that at native E-Rank for free. Section 7.2 defines a category as **a kind of thing the Rank beneath cannot do at any value of the governing Stat**; the old wording would have failed the Rank Dominance Law the instant anyone read the two sections together — the same defect class 1.57 recorded against Mana Bolt's ladder, reached through an *unstated* rule rather than a redundant one. **Settled: storage reaches what he perceives; withdrawal still reaches only his hand.** Three limits authored with it — **nothing a person is holding, wearing, or carrying**, nothing behind a barrier he cannot perceive through, and a contested attempt resolves on the die. **Nothing here disarms anyone**, and remote deployment and launch still do not exist without Dimensional Projection. **The cost lands where the tension is: remote storage is free outside an exchange and spends the exchange's one inventory change inside one**, under Section 12.9's existing *Equip and swap* budget — the currency Section 7.2 has assigned this skill since 1.51. Hand-delivery both ways stays free and unlimited; only the remote reach is rationed. **What Alexander holds now:** a projected weapon is **never lost and never an errand**, but at native Rank he may **launch or recover in an exchange, never both** — a beat of tempo, paid like a weapon swap — and **`Recall` (D) survives as a genuine grant**, returning it at the action's end without spending that change. **Nothing is granted and nothing moves:** Dimensional Projection keeps [E-Rank], Novice, 5 m, 6 Mana, Agility to hit; its line is re-rendered over a restriction that did not previously exist in writing; the *Recall* rung is reworded and remains unreached until D-Rank; no other skill is touched. **Nothing retroactive** — `EVT-000326`'s sweep is retrospectively legitimate rather than newly permitted, and Dimensional Projection has never been used in play, so no projection exists to re-cost. Registry allocation: `EVT-000438`. Updated live targets: `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`, `worlds/gatefall/206_WORLD_RULE_PROFILE.md`, `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.58_to_1.59.md`, `worlds/gatefall/migrations/INDEX.md`.

**2026-08-16 — Profile 1.58: First Principles, authored before it exists (`EVT-000437`).**
Owner ruling taken immediately after `EVT-000436` opened the candidate it concerns. Compatibility treatment over frozen 1.57; no fictional time, no scene, no die, nothing granted, `profile_version` the only stored value touched. **What `EVT-000436` left standing was a trap rather than a wait.** It opened `applied_analytical_reconstruction` at 2/3 and correctly declined to ratify — threshold three, no authored result — but Section 7.1's **mandatory adjudication gate** says a candidate with no authored result reaching 3/3 stops play outright: no roll, no NPC action, no advance of in-world time, no next scene until the owner has authored and named the complete skill. The third scene would land mid-session and the technique would be authored under exactly the pressure that produces a bad one. **The ruling is the one 1.55 already made for Dimensional Projection** — that edge authored a Rank ladder for a skill nobody had demonstrated, over the objection that an unformed skill should not carry rungs, because **the ladder is what makes the skill safe to form**; Twin Fang had been made ascension-eligible with no Rank axis and sat one ascension from destroying 37.5% of its own damage. The same argument applies a step earlier, to the effect. **First Principles [E-Rank]**, passive, 0 Mana: **+1 modifier step on any action reconstructing unobserved structure, coverage, or reach from observed geometry and physical law** — sightlines and sensor cones, load paths and structural extent, trajectory, span, reachability. **It derives; it does not perceive**, which is what keeps it distinct from two skills Alexander already holds: it never reveals what is *present* (Keen Sense, which costs Mana and reads a subject) and establishes **no Pattern**, so it grants no standing exploit and occupies no Exploit Pattern slot. Its modifier does not stack with another from the same measurements, and **a derivation resting on wrong measurements is wrong** — the skill improves the reasoning, never the evidence. **Its mastery axis is authored rather than inherited, which is `F-014`'s lesson applied on time:** a permanent passive at the Section 4.3 step cap has no multiplier, no reduction, no duration to lengthen and Mana 0 — every closed-form axis inapplicable at once, the exact state in which Broken Rhythm climbed to a live mastery level granting nothing, twelve qualifying scenes for no change. Mastery grants **1/2/3/4/5 beneficiaries**; it carries a `scope_floor`; its Rank ladder joins the Section 7.3 **scope-axis** table (**Inference from absence** D, **Mechanism** C, **From secondhand measure** B, **Predictive** A) and it takes a row in Section 7.4's per-skill mastery table, because that section says a mastery-tracked skill without one is not finished. **Nothing is granted:** the candidate stays `tracking` at 2/3, ratification still requires a third qualifying scene under an unchanged threshold, no `skills_known` entry is written, no counter is initialized, neither `EVT-000372` nor `EVT-000398` becomes a use, and Keen Sense and Exploit Pattern are untouched. What changes is only what happens when a third scene lands — automatic ratification in its own settlement, since the key is now a declared `pre_authored_result_key`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000437`. Updated live targets: `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`, `worlds/gatefall/206_WORLD_RULE_PROFILE.md`, `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.57_to_1.58.md`, `worlds/gatefall/migrations/INDEX.md`.

Player-instructed retroactive skill-formation audit across every promoted Event of the campaign, run after Checkpoint 0075. No fictional time, no scene, no die, and no past exchange re-resolved; the repairs go **forward** on the `EVT-000389`/`EVT-000390`/`EVT-000406` precedent, and the scan itself is recorded as deliberately exceeding Section 7.1's *'a bounded session reconciliation, never a scan of older chronicles or checkpoints'* bound. **Finding A — `dimensional_weapon_control` had been at 3/3 since 2026-08-08 and nobody looked.** It sat `tracking` at 2/3 from the `EVT-000131` historical settlement (`EVT-000069`'s private summon-and-grip drill, `EVT-000070`'s Ashfield pocket-swap feint). Its third scene is the **sealed penalty-zone survival instance** — four hours at real death, cleared solo in ~25 minutes — where two kills opened on a **materialized-dagger strike**: `EVT-000195` (Rupture on the first common, 🎲 52 → effective 100 legendary, one-shot, no alarm) and `EVT-000197` (second elite ambushed on a throat seam, 🎲 58 → effective 100 critical, 230 against a 200 pool). **One continuous danger contributes once under test 5**, which was all the candidate needed. **Why it read `none`:** both Events are filed `kind: promotion`, which is not a settlement kind under this profile's trigger manifest, so no `dangerous-scene-settlement` ever evaluated that instance's notes — the `EVT-000327`/`EVT-000332` misfiling class arriving on the formation gate instead of the skill-credit one. Mastery 5/7/10/15/20 m, Mana 6/5/5/4/4, `magnitude_floor` from ratification. **All counters initialize at zero** — Section 7.1's pre-ratification evidence rule buys acquisition only. **Live from this Event forward**; Section 12.9's pre-skill hand-delivery-only boundary held for every prior exchange and none is re-resolved. **Finding B — a candidate that should have existed since 2026-08-14 and never did.** Twice Alexander resolved a problem by applying his **trained mathematics-and-physics background as the declared method, explicitly instead of a System skill**: `EVT-000372` (plotting four Loomis Street cameras' sweep cones by hand, 🎲 69 → effective 89, finding a four-foot blind spot the cold-engine van was already parked on, reframing the site from *professional security* to *staffed to cover its own blind spot*) and `EVT-000398` (a structural read of the Cicero Scar's unreclaimed core blocks, 🎲 88, establishing the sublevel needs an inner-cordon posting). **Neither settlement opened it** — `EVT-000376` never mentions the first, and `EVT-000405` asserted *'No new repeatable method appeared this span that isn't already a ratified skill'* over the second. **2026-08-16 — Keen Sense unfrozen: a Rank ceiling had been read as a mastery ceiling (`EVT-000419`).**
Owner ruling from a player-instructed progression audit run immediately after Checkpoint 0073. Five skills had been tracked correctly this session; **Keen Sense had not.** Its rendered line read *'progress 2/3 toward Master (capped until level 30)'*, its `mastery_progress` counter had sat at **2** while seven qualifying scenes accrued, and the stated justification was *'at the Section 7.5 ceiling.'* **That ceiling is on Rank alone** — Section 7.5's text is singular about it — and **Section 7.4 places no ceiling on mastery at all**, while Section 7.5 names the resulting state rather than forbidding it: *a skill already at Master and already at the ceiling simply holds.* **`EVT-000396` had not frozen it either; its own words were that the later scenes built *'a fresh track instead of banking against a Master ceiling.'*** The parenthetical was layered on afterwards, took the Rank ceiling as its authority, and converted a live track into a dead one — **no ruling ever made that decision, a sentence did.** Settled: progress 2 + 1 reaches 3, **`mastery_level` 4 → 5 (Master)**, `mastery_progress` **2 → 0** (complete at Master), and the remaining six scenes bank as lifetime counts exactly as Section 7.4's post-`F-012` rule requires. **What Master grants, stated rather than assumed: scope 7 → 8 subjects** (mastery's 5 plus Section 7.2's +3 for three Ranks above native E), `scope_floor` ratcheting 7 → 8, and **Mana unchanged at 1** (`2 × 0.9⁴ = 1.31`). A real grant, unlike `EVT-000418`'s Exploit Pattern advance where the ratchet was already carrying the formula's value. **Section 7.5's standing check ran immediately: Keen Sense is now at Master *and* at its Rank ceiling, so the breakthrough is withheld rather than offered** — recorded not silent per Section 7.2, becoming ascension-ready the instant System Rank reaches B at level 30 with no further scene required. **This is `F-012`'s shape a third time**: a counter pinned by a plausible-sounding reason that no gate could contradict, because a delta never claimed can never disagree with itself. The player found it by asking, same as `F-012`. Recorded against `F-015`. No XP, level, Stat, pool, item, quest, or resolved outcome changes. Registry allocation: `EVT-000419`. Updated live targets: `100`, `160`, `170`, `180`, `system/ID_REGISTRY.md`.

**2026-08-16 — the day Owen cleared a Gate, and the board finally settled (`EVT-000413`-`EVT-000418`).**

- **`GB-04` breaks, four days late (`EVT-000413`).** The Red Line Corridor E-Rank Coalition posting still read `posted` against a **midnight 2026-08-12 deadline** while the clock ran to 08-16. Section 9.10 owes no roll and no discretion at a deadline — `posted` at deadline **breaks** — and it never reached floor. Released 🎲 3d6 6+1+1 = **8 common**, 🎲 1d2 **1 elite**, 1 boss, all E-Rank; BGM emergency contract issued, guild strike team dispatched (Section 9.8's fixed Chicago order). **Alexander was not there and has not been told** — Decision 084 makes a board settlement silent to the Bearer, producing no notification and no panel line. **Second E-Rank break in that same corridor inside eleven days** after `GB-01`, which is a measurement of how thin the freelance pool is at the bottom of the ladder. `tracked_postings` **1 → 0**; the board is empty for the first time. **`F-002` has recurred on its own unaddressed cause** — that flag's campaign lapse was actioned in 2026-07-31, but the design question it raised (whether Section 9.10 needs a **declared audit boundary**, as Decisions 082 and 083 gave commitments and supply) was left open, and nothing fires on the clock.
- **The twenty-third daily, issued and completed (`EVT-000414`).** Woken by being kissed awake; Owen's fully-asleep deadpan about an unlisted, unranked, never-cleared 'kissing monster' is on the record. Full regimen and the lakefront run by ~07:15. **Streak 8 → 9** — not a multiple of seven, so a standard box. **Rewards read Ability Points +4** rather than +3 under Ascetic. **A Runtime correction:** the morning was first narrated running to 08:00 with a manufactured rush to reach Dale; the player rejected the invented urgency and it compresses honestly with a comfortable buffer.
- **Dale Pruitt's B-Rank harvest — the largest single harvest share of the campaign (`EVT-000415`).** Confirmed B-Rank, BGM-signed, **nine combat-rated against a legal minimum of eight** — Dale made the staffing a point of pride, a deliberate contrast with Wade's four-against-eight forged roster that nobody said aloud. **Marisela Kwan** (`ENT-000204`, new) ran point with her own independent eight; **Flux Sight** read her B-Rank, 600/600, 300/300 on introduction. **Reston** (`ENT-000205`, new) was the second cutter. Interior **Crypt** — named on the assignment, since Section 10 gives a confirmed Gate a surveyed archetype; **a Runtime correction**, it was first narrated as unknown. Population 🎲 9 common, 🎲 1 elite, 1 boss. **Three veins, three Keen Sense reads (🎲 52→92, 82→100, 62→100) and three Resonance Extraction Patterns (🎲 16→56, 36→76, 16→56), all landed: 64 B-Rank crystals mined solo.** **19 declared, 45 kept** — B-Rank stash 35 → **80**. **$60,677.42 filed** (19/31 of a 20% share on a ≈$495,000 clear). Alexander never fought and drew no weapon; the boss fight happened thirty meters away and he tracked it by sound while mining. **A second Runtime correction:** Dale was briefly written commenting on Alexander's combat reputation and on a running joke about undeclared crystals — he has never seen Alexander fight, and the two Frozen Gallery suspicion moments were deflected and never confirmed. Both retracted before entering canon.
- **The Longsword given away, and an offer declined (`EVT-000416`).** Owen's reaction to $60k split cleanly — genuine astonishment at the figure, then the read he always runs (not hurt, grinning, so it went fine) and a request for the account anyway. **Offered harvest work to cut his hospital hours, he declined with three reasons** and named the real one: *'I stay because it's the job where I don't have to be first through a door.'* Then said what he does want — more time where Alexander is, and the version of that is the crew — without pressing it. **The Longsword [C-Rank] handed over outright**, no longer Alexander's property. He objected practically (different stance, different reach, the morning before he was meant to learn something), called it beautiful, and **then refused Alexander's 'nothing in a D-Rank dungeon can kill you'** by quoting Alexander's own first-week words back: death in a Gate is final and the trade does not pretend otherwise. *'I don't need it to be nothing. I need you to say it's manageable and mean it.'* **This is `REL-000066`'s `EVT-000241` term running in reverse for the first time.** Separately, **a recording gap surfaced rather than a contradiction** — the two had established that the Quickknife does not suit Owen, and it was never written into his record; backfilled here, same class as `F-005`-`F-011`.

**2026-08-14 ~18:10 - Profile 1.51: the System window loses its vertical edges (`EVT-000378`).**
Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.50 -> 1.51, a **compatibility treatment** on player instruction. **No stored value is read, written, or re-derived** — `touched_state_paths` is empty and means it. §15.1's frame was a closed box with a `║` at both margins of every interior row; 1.51 keeps the horizontal rules and removes both edges, so a panel opens on `╔══…`, divides on `╟──…`, closes on `╚══…`, and an interior row is its content alone. **The right edge was the expensive half** — it only holds if every row is padded to an exact width, and these panels are dense with glyphs a Runtime can miscount (the mastery stars, the meter blocks, the `◈` in every title bar, the multiplication signs, the bracketed Ranks). One miscount rendered a wall that visibly failed to close, which reads to the Bearer as a **broken window** rather than as a window with a ragged margin. The left edge then repeated that per-row risk for no gain, since the rules already mark where a panel starts, ends, and divides — which is the whole of what a frame is for.

**Padding goes with it.** Rows are laid out on the columns each template sets and are never padded rightward, there being no margin to pad to; a short row simply ends. **The no-truncation rule is unchanged** — an over-long value still wraps onto an indented continuation row beneath its own label — and the rules still run to a nominal 78 cells so panels stack evenly. The gutter is unchanged in effect, its five cells now counted from the start of the row rather than from an edge character that no longer exists. **The change is necessarily global:** §15.4 states that `/system all` composes the §15.2 and §15.3 panels *unchanged* and authors no layout of its own, so the frame cannot differ between them — asking for it on one asks for it on all. **Nothing a Bearer can read moves:** every value reachable under 1.50 is reachable under 1.51, in the same section, on the same row, in the same order. **And nothing is rewritten backwards** — rendering is not stored state, so the first `/system` call after adoption simply draws the new frame; panels already written into this chronicle, the changelog, or an export stand exactly as they are, being the record of what the Bearer saw at the time. Migration record: `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.50_to_1.51.md`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000378`.

**2026-08-14 ~14:30 - Profile 1.50: the five stored inventory kinds get definitions, and the field gets their shape (`EVT-000367`).**
Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.49 -> 1.50, a **required-migration** advance. **§15.3.2 named five groups and defined none of them.** `/system gear` renders the dimensional inventory as **Gear · Consumables · Keys · Materials · Special** and prints each group's live line count — a number the profile states without ever saying which holdings produce it. That is §20.2's defect in its purest form: a stated figure resting on an unstated rule, decided fresh at every render by whoever happens to be rendering. **This is the second half of the defect `EVT-000366` opened.** The field it renders from was prose a renderer had to interpret twice — once to ask whether an entry named an item at all, and again to ask which group it belonged to. `EVT-000366` answered the first question by normalizing to one holding per entry; a real possession had already gone missing on it.

**The kinds are defined with a deterministic precedence order** — **Keys, Consumables, Special, Gear, Materials**, first match wins, exactly one group per holding. The order is what makes the boundaries decidable rather than tasteful. A key is a consumable by nature and is still a **Key**, because §17 makes instant-dungeon keys their own economy. An unidentified core is **Special**, not Materials, because its Rank is precisely what is not yet known — and it moves to Materials on the identification that resolves it, a state change with an Event behind it rather than a re-reading of unchanged facts. A stored weapon is **Gear** while the crystals out of the same Gate are **Materials**. **Special absorbs the unresolved** — anything whose worth is not mechanical, and any item whose function, Rank, or provenance is unresolved — which is what gives every ambiguous holding a defined home instead of a coin flip. An empty kind is retained rather than dropped, since §15.3.2 requires a group with no live holdings to render `none` and a kind absent from state cannot render anything.

**On Alexander: `system_state.inventory` becomes one list per kind**, and his twenty-eight holdings regroup as **keys 3 · consumables 7 · special 4 · gear 10 · materials 4**. Every text, Rank line, provenance and quantity carries over verbatim, the total is twenty-eight before and after, and **no holding, quantity, or owned object changed**; `equipment` is untouched. One assignment is worth recording because it is the boundary case the order exists for: the **half-finished leather piece** is **Special**, not Materials — literally unworked stock, but it carries the same maker's stamp as the S-Rank dagger and the keyed wallet, which makes it evidence in the Marnie thread. **A repository check now enforces the shape** — five named kinds, none missing, none invented, no repeated kind, and no holding sitting loose under the field — with three mutation legs in `tools/test_validate_repository.ps1` proving each half fires. **The 8-vs-3 Lesser Healing Potion discrepancy survives this reshape too, still flagged and still unresolved.** Migration record: `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.49_to_1.50.md`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000367`.

**2026-08-14 ~14:30 - `shop_holdings` becomes `inventory`, and stops hiding things (`EVT-000366`).**
Ledger repair, prompted by a `/system gear` render that omitted the **Gate Direction Finder [E-Rank]** — an item the sheet was carrying correctly all along. **No rule changed and no profile version moved**; 1.49 stays active. **The field was not a list of items:** 35 entries, only 28 of which named something owned. Five were session narrative (the `EVT-000327` extraction summary carries dice rolls, an XP total and goods bought-and-consumed, and names no owned object at all) and two were disposal records — so a renderer had to judge, per prose paragraph, whether it named a possession, and a miss dropped a real item with nothing to catch it. `equipment` never had this failure, being nine named slots. **The name was wrong too:** §12.9 calls this the *dimensional inventory*, where every item he owns lives unless worn, while `shop_holdings` described the origin of a minority of it — the finder was $800 cash to Elias Ward, the dock salvage was swept out of a loading bay. **Repaired:** renamed `system_state.inventory`, normalized to **one holding per entry (28)**, five narrative entries dropped as duplicates of promoted Events, two disposal entries moved to `120_INVENTORY_AND_OWNERSHIP.md` under a new **Disposed — No Longer Owned** heading where §15.1 already says they belong. All 28 keep their text, Ranks, provenance and quantities verbatim; `equipment` untouched. **The 8-vs-3 Lesser Healing Potion discrepancy is carried forward, still flagged** — normalizing a field's shape is not licence to pick a number. Startup dispatch (3 references), the ownership ledger's pointer and two tool assertions repointed; immutable checkpoints keep `shop_holdings` and are untouched.

**2026-08-14 ~14:30 - Profile 1.49: Multitask retired, Conduit takes the Intelligence slot (`EVT-000364`-`EVT-000365`).**
Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.48 -> 1.49, a **required-migration** advance. **Multitask's only grant was a ceiling nobody reached.** The play record convicts it: capacity 4 against two attached Hidden pointers, **no Urgent quest accepted in ninety in-fiction days**, and a use counter reading **0** while Flux Sight read 8, Overpower 4, Pre-empt 2 and Shrug Off 1. The binding constraint on concurrent quests is **supply** (§8.4.2's crisis criteria, §8.4.3's concealed-discovery and proximity rules), never the cap — so raising it was a Rank step granting nothing, §7.2's Rank Dominance Law failing on its own terms, and 1.48 had just extended that dead ladder to 5/6/7 rather than noticing.

**Capacity moves to System Rank** (§8.4): 2/3/4/5/6/7 at E/D/C/B/A/S, owned by no skill and raised by no allocation, item or title; `non_daily_quests` drops `base_capacity`, `multitask_bonus` and `analyst_bonus`. **Conduit** takes the Intelligence slot as **one category escalating across all six rungs** — +5/+10/+15/+20/+25/+30 percentage points on both §5.2 recovery rates — so the stat compounds with itself: Intelligence sets maximum Mana, and recovery is a percentage of that maximum. Composition order is fixed (base, Conduit, environmental halving, then any title multiplier, so a Frozen Gallery halves the improved rate and **Wellspring**'s doubling lands last), and **no rung reaches inside a fight**: recovery settles from elapsed time, an exchange is not an hour, and §5.3's exhaustion and the drained-boss tactic it supports are untouched. Nothing alters a skill's Mana cost, which stays mastery's axis (§7.2).

**Two 1.48 rungs corrected by the same standard** — a rung that changes subject is as wrong as one that grants nothing. **Overpower A** was *Anchoring* (immovability, i.e. defence) and becomes **three Ranks above, structures one Rank above the skill**, continuing E's one and D's two. **Pre-empt A** was *Interposition* (taking a hit for another, i.e. protection) and becomes **shared first blade**. Both prospective: no Bearer has ever stood at an A rung.

**On Alexander:** Multitask and its zero-valued counter removed; **Conduit authored at B-Rank on the same Intelligence 58** — no Stat moved, a different skill on the same rung — giving **30%/hr active, 45%/hr resting**. Across this morning's two-hour clear window that would have recovered ~60% of pool instead of 20%. **Quest capacity 5 → 4** from System Rank C: a reduction that cannot bite, since 4 stood for the whole campaign until hours ago, two slots are held, and nothing is evicted in either direction. **No Mana recomputed** — 118/120 stands exactly as `EVT-000361` left it, no elapsed span re-settled. Migration record: `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.48_to_1.49.md`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000364`-`EVT-000365`.

**2026-08-14 ~14:30 - Profile 1.48 adopted, and four Stat Passives rise C → B at once (`EVT-000362`-`EVT-000363`).**
Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.47 -> 1.48, a **required-migration** advance. **Section 4.4 had half a ladder.** When the Stat-50 skills were retired into their partners, **Overpower**, **Pre-empt**, **Shrug Off** and **Multitask** were authored at E, D and C and stopped — against a threshold table running 30 / 36 / 44 / 54 / 66 / 80. Flux Sight alone reached S. So a Bearer crossing 54, 66 or 80 banked the value against a rung that did not exist; the **authoring clamp** held the Rank down and the skill's row reported the hold at every checkpoint. 1.48 authors all twelve missing rungs. Each is a **category** under Section 7.2's Rank Dominance Law and its Stat Passive exemption — a kind of thing the Rank beneath cannot do at any Stat value — and none adds a modifier step, Mana cost, multiplier or scope count. **Death is untouched at every rung** (Section 20.4): Shrug Off at S removes the penalties harm imposes and never makes a killing blow survivable. Multitask's ladder continues as the capacity number it already was, **5 / 6 / 7** at B / A / S, and Section 8.4 follows it. The clamp is now spent and the **System Rank + 1 ceiling stands alone**; the held-rung render is completed to name *which* clamp binds and the *highest* threshold the Stat actually reached, and to forbid a row advertising a next threshold as a bare number when neither clamp would let it pay out.

**On Alexander, everything landed in one settlement and no Stat moved to earn it** — every point had been allocated already, banked against rungs that did not exist, and Section 4.4's promise is that held value applies the instant the limit lifts. **Overpower C → B** (Strength 61) gains *redirection*. **Pre-empt C → B** (Agility 54) gains *first blade* — he resolves before any opponent up to B-Rank in an exchange he opens, and it is the one of the four bought deliberately: those were the last 2 of the session's 56 points, spent purely for the 54 threshold. **Shrug Off C → B** (Vitality 100) gains *total suppression*, and its hold changes hands from `rung unauthored` to `System Rank ceiling` — a full S-Rank of Vitality now waits on level 40 and 50 rather than on authoring. **Multitask C → B** (Intelligence 58) gains capacity **5**, the only rung of the four that writes a stored number: `multitask_bonus` 3 → 4, `capacity_total` 4 → 5, no attached quest disturbed, slots 2/5 used. **Flux Sight is unchanged at B** — authored to S already, and Perception 60 sits under the A threshold of 66, which the ceiling would hold anyway. **Nothing retroactive**: no exchange re-resolved, no injury reclassified, no surprise round replayed; `successful_uses` untouched on all four, since a Rank change is not a use. Migration record: `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.47_to_1.48.md`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000362`-`EVT-000363`.

**2026-08-14 ~12:15 - Profile 1.47 adopted, and both missed Title Assessments run (`EVT-000348`-`EVT-000349`).**
Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.46 -> 1.47, a **required-migration** advance, and the first profile change in four versions that a character can notice. **Section 16 had law and no trigger.** Titles were authored at 1.0 to be granted "the instant the feat resolves" — a sentence that reads like a rule and never was one, because nothing in the profile ever caused a feat to be tested against the catalog. No schedule, no evaluating step in any settlement, no field to write a title into, no Event that could produce one: forty-six versions of live law, a `/system titles` panel, a Console row and a Tier-1 message block, all permanently empty. **1.47 gives it a trigger.** The **Title Assessment** (Section 16.1) now runs inside the level-up that settles **level 10** and every level-up settling a multiple of ten thereafter — every rung of the Section 6.6 System Rank ladder above the E a Bearer starts at, continuing past 50 on the decade — reading the Bearer's whole recorded career since onset and granting every title the record has earned. Nothing fires between assessments; no unearned criterion is ever published (Section 14.2); an assessment that grants nothing still renders. The catalog grows five entries to **twenty-one across three grades** — twelve **Common**, six **Rare**, three **Singular** — each entry decidable from resolved Events without a ruling, and each **named for what its feat made the Bearer rather than for the feat**: *Lone Clear → Loner*, *Untouched → Untouchable*, plus **Underdog**, **Diehard**, **Kingmaker**, **Prospector**, **Penitent**, **Ascetic**, **Giantslayer** at Common; **Cartographer**, **Deathless**, **Executioner**, **Warden**, **Vanguard**, **Wellspring** at Rare; and **Godslayer**, **Unbroken**, **The Unmeasured** at Singular, whose passives grant capabilities the profile grants nowhere else. **A title now grants two things**, where 1.0 gave only the second: **allocatable stat points the instant it is earned — +2 Common, +5 Rare, +10 Singular** — the "title reward that states a specific point bonus" Sections 3.2 and 4.2 have named as a stat-point source since 1.0 and no title ever stated — permanent and never contingent on equipping; and one **passive, active only while equipped**. **The loadout also grows:** one equipped slot per assessment passed, capped at five against a catalog of twenty-one, where 1.0 allowed exactly one forever and left everything else inert. `system_state.title` now holds a list. Section 3.2 gains the assessment as a further decade-level settlement; Section 14.3's Tier-1 list trades the untriggerable `TITLE EARNED` line for the `TITLE ASSESSMENT` block; `/system titles` gains an `ASSESSMENT` section and renders one row per slot, filled or empty.

**The catch-up, on Alexander — two rungs, judged separately.** He passed level 10 at `EVT-000150` (2026-08-05) and level 20 at `EVT-000341` (this morning), both under profiles with no assessment to fire, so the migration runs both at adoption in level order. Each rung is tested against the record **as it stood when that level was reached**, so the level-10 assessment is real rather than a formality; only the level-20 one absorbs everything resolved since (Rules Section 13.2 forbids un-resolving what was promoted in between). **The split is seven and three.**

- **Level 10, 2026-08-05 — seven, one of them Rare.** **Kingmaker** (`EVT-000059`, the onset scene itself: he read the Red Line Corridor boss's post-landing lock, called it, Tanya baited the leap and Elias and Tanya killed it, with boss XP and the clear milestone settling to him under Section 3.8), **Underdog** (`EVT-000070`, the Ashfield Gate resolved true C-Rank while he was System Rank E — two Ranks up, red — and he intercepted and killed a C-Rank common alone), **Diehard** (`EVT-000070`, the same fight: Health 40 -> 0 on a Rank-honest retaliation, survived by claiming a daily grant mid-exchange in front of Owen), **Loner** (`EVT-000097`, one of seven solo clears already standing at that rung), **Untouchable** (`EVT-000097`, which closes on `No injury; never hit`), **Centurion** (`EVT-000149`, the hundredth kill falling inside the Hive clear on solo instant-dungeon kills alone), and — **Rare** — **Cartographer** (`EVT-000120`, a Gate of every archetype on the Section 10 d8 cleared and credited, all eight interiors inside eleven days, the Beast Den at the Cicero Gate closing the set two days before the rung).
- **Level 20, 2026-08-14 — three, all Common.** **Prospector** (`EVT-000342`, 552 crystals extracted on cited events against a 500 floor — roughly 270 at the earlier rung), **Penitent** (`EVT-000198`, the penalty zone he triggered on purpose and cleared solo in twenty-five minutes; Level 10 -> 11 was crossed inside it), **Ascetic** (`EVT-000338`, twenty-one dailies against a floor of twenty — twelve at the earlier rung).

**Eleven unheld and unnamed to him** — Red Survivor, Break-Breaker and Giantslayer at Common; all five remaining Rare; all three Singular. The nearest is the Singular **The Unmeasured**: his E-Rank card stands two Ranks under System Rank C where the criterion asks three, so if he is still carded E when System Rank B lands at level 30, it grants itself. **The ten titles carry their grades' points — nine Common at +2 and one Rare at +5: unspent 30 → 47 → 53**, banked unallocated, so no Stat, Health/Mana maximum, or Stat Passive Rank moves on account of them. **Two equipped slots are open and both stand empty against ten earned titles**: the assessments grant titles, points and slots but fill nothing, `system_state.title` stays `null`, and no title passive is active until Alexander chooses through `/system titles` — he can now run two at once. **Beyond `titles`, the widened `title` field and those 23 points, nothing moved**: no pool, Stat, maximum, XP total, level, System Rank, counter, quest, deadline, streak, gold, cash, item, or resolved outcome. Next assessment: level 30, which opens a third slot. Migration record: `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.46_to_1.47.md`. Registry allocation: `EVT-000348`-`EVT-000349`.

**2026-07-28 - Profile 1.5 Mender-Treatment Migration (`EVT-000078`).**

- **Owner ruling.** During the boss fight, at an out-of-character pause, the owner ruled Profile 1.4's mender rule inconsistent with what a mender is in this world: under 1.4 a C-Rank healing-touch mender could not set a broken bone, only halve its healing clock, which contradicts both the fiction and the crew economics that make a mender worth a share of the split. Frozen Profile **1.5** was authored and adopted.
- **Rule change (Sections 6.3, 6.4):** a mender's resolved treatment touch now **clears an injury's severity outright**, rank-gated — E clears Minor, D clears Moderate, C clears Severe, B and above clear Critical. Clearing consumes one of the mender's limited per-clear touches; one touch either restores Health or clears one injury, never both; a mender treating above their tier still supplies the professional-care halving and may step severity down one tier by ruling. Section 6.4's Health/severity independence is preserved and clarified: the mender's touch is the one authored effect acting on the severity axis, while potions, Status Recovery, and level-ups act only on Health.
- **Preserved:** every Stat, level, XP total, pool value, item, equipment slot, skill, title, currency, ownership, and provenance. Alexander's Severe rib injury is preserved at its recorded severity and becomes clearable going forward rather than retroactively erased. Owen's unspent per-clear touches carry over.
- **Ledger updates:** `worlds/gatefall/206_WORLD_RULE_PROFILE.md` (version header, 1.4→1.5 migration block, Sections 6.3 and 6.4), `worlds/gatefall/README.md` (version references), `090` (profile binding 1.5), `100` (profile version, migration event, condition), `160` (`EVT-000078`), `170` (this entry), `180` (profile binding, condition, scene anchor, promotion status), registry (`EVT-000078`).

**2026-07-28 - Session 2 continued: The Ashfield Boss Engagement (`EVT-000079`).**

- **The plan.** Alexander proposed finishing the Gate and got the crew's agreement — Owen first, then Julian, Renata, and finally Priya, who ceded the tactical call outright but demanded a real plan and a contingency for her going down. Each member raised a genuine objection (Renata's two incompatible jobs, Julian's closing lane, Owen's exposure, Priya's wound); Alexander revised: unseen approach on Renata's read, Renata freed to read exclusively, Julian holding until the go, he and Priya rotating the boss's attention between them, Owen held back and instructed not to come for him if both were down. He drew a **lesser healing potion from his dimensional inventory and gave it to Priya** (framed as a purchase), restoring her Health without touching her wound's severity — Owen registered the fourth unexplained thing and said nothing.
- **The approach:** d100 50, net +2 (Renata's guidance, Ashfield haze), effective 90, strong success — the crew reached the den chamber unseen, boss circling unaware, rendered **red/pulsing** (two-plus Ranks above System Rank: E-Rank).
- **The engagement:** Julian's opening shot turned the boss into Alexander's flank; Alexander's ambush strike (d100 43, net +2, effective 83, strong success) dealt 43.5, then his weak-point strike to pull it off Priya (d100 50, net +3, effective 100, **critical**) dealt 87 and fixed its attention entirely on him.
- **Two natural fumbles.** Alexander planted the E-Rank Quickknife in the wound, instantly recalled the C-Rank Quickknife through the dimensional inventory, and struck — **natural 1**, back foot through churned ash, on one knee inside its reach. The boss's retaliation (d100 41, net +2, effective 61, success) dealt `62.5 × 1 × (1 − 0.087) = 57`: **Health 68 → 12/68**, meeting both injury tests under Section 6.3 and inflicting a **Severe left-rib injury**. Alexander claimed his pending **Status Recovery** (Health 12 → 68/68; pending rewards now AP +3 and three Random Boxes) — Owen's third witnessed impossible recovery. His next strike, on a body that could not plant, was a **second natural fumble (5)**, carrying him into the open.
- **Evasion:** d100 88, net −1 (Severe injury, open ground, boss badly wounded), effective 68, success — cleared the charge; the boss overshot and Priya struck it on the pass.
- **State:** boss badly wounded (five wounds, one limb dragging, Alexander's E-Rank Quickknife embedded in its shoulder), still active. Alexander at 68/68 with the untreated Severe injury and 5 unspent points. Combat ongoing.
