Two A-Rank directed assignments generated and accepted: Ashworth's confirmed op (named) and a second, unconfirmed op dispatched cold (offered). 45th daily completed, streak 33, Ability Points +4 (Ascetic) claimed, Daily Random Box: System gold +650,000.

**The far side, twice.** First visit: Ilith's Unremarked Movement reaches 3/3 and **ratifies as a genuinely novel technique** (no table entry matched it — Route 5's own privilege). Read the Want, Unwatched, Seamseal, Stillwater, Grip and Footing, and Second Wind all advance. Three new candidates opened on request: Reading the Room (Ilith), Doorward (Sevrin), Appraise (Tolo). Two attempts fail cleanly (Hold Together, second Diagram Sense) — no evidence recorded, correctly. Second visit, between five solo S-Rank instant dungeons: **Read the Want, Seamseal, and Stillwater all ratify**, Doorward advances, Unwatched and Reading the Room both fail their attempts. **Four ratified far-side techniques this checkpoint — Unremarked Movement, Read the Want, Seamseal, Stillwater** — the first real yield from `OBJ-55`'s five Standing threads.

**Two solo A-Rank Gate clears.** Ashworth's confirmed op clears clean, 9,900 XP, +$12,250,000 filed same-day. The offered op reveals as a **Red Gate** on entry — sealed until the boss dies, found out the hard way. Cleared clean anyway, 10,300 XP; its S-Rank-valued loot (Section 11.4's anomaly bump) filed to auction rather than sold same-day, **≈$138,400,000 estimated, pending**, alongside a same-day $4,000,000 flat-plus-severity payout. Combined XP crosses **Level 163→164.**

**Five more solo S-Rank instant dungeons**, bought outright as training ground, cross **Level 164→171** in one cascade — 114,000 XP, seven thresholds, +7 all base Stats, +35 unspent points. Unspent points close the span at **53**, unallocated. A late-caught arithmetic slip in the crystal sell-down (73 beast-drop crystals initially miscounted as 72) was corrected before promotion rather than carried forward.

**Chicago to Prague to Bucharest, and the Denver facility.** Rift-step to the existing Prague anchor, then an ordinary commercial flight rather than a blind step — Alexander now stages through Bucharest alongside Sorcha rather than Prague alone, ahead of tomorrow's Jiu Valley site visit. Dinner surfaces `OBJ-49`'s real cost ($34,000,000, 4-6 months for a clean build) and Sorcha's own Jiu Valley history, disclosed ahead of the site visit rather than on the perimeter. Alexander judges the timeline too long; Sorcha counters with a better option than the interim lease she'd already offered — buying and retrofitting a decommissioned BGM-surplus facility outside Denver. **Purchased outright, $12,000,000 total** (purchase + retrofit), usable immediately during the 6-8 week retrofit, existing on-site monitoring retained pending an air-gap check. `OBJ-51` closes, superseded. `OBJ-49` reshaped — designed, purchased, retrofitting. New Place entity minted: `ENT-000264`.

## Checkpoint 0141 — Jiu Valley, Blue Island, and Level 172

**Span:** `EVT-000902`-`EVT-000908` · **2026-09-10 06:00 -> 14:00 -05:00.**

**The 06:00 tick and the day's obligations.** Tier VII, first tick of the week — a new B-Rank awakened noticed elsewhere in Chicago, ambient news only. An S-Rank Gate offered via BGM National, accepted. 46th daily completed, streak 34. Elias Kane's call from the prior session finally completed — the Prague S-Rank network meeting moved inside a week, sparring taken up immediately rather than deferred.

**The Jiu Valley Exclusion.** Sorcha walked Alexander to the observation platform and disclosed her own second-year rotation — three trainees lost on a rescue attempt she was pulled from days before it happened. A twenty-eighth anchor set there, uncontested. Flux Sight and Keen Sense read the depression's central signature as unmeasurable from range; closing on foot under three stacked concealment methods (Silent Step, Unwatched, Unremarked Movement) held perfectly. A close read (corrected mid-scene from an initially ungrounded figure to something genuinely scaled against the setting's own power references) put the target at 45,000 Health, ~7,200 Mana — matched, not vastly exceeding, the strongest reference points in the world. **Three Rupture strikes, the third exploiting a tactical read of its ground-contact tracking, killed it** — the first confirmed kill against what outlasted six S-Ranks and thirty-one thousand deaths. Unwatched ratifies on the exchange, its third genuinely dangerous scene. 1,000 kill XP; no Gate-clear milestone (the Gate itself broke in 2020, nothing left to close); no loot banked — Gate-origin material almost certainly sits there, extraction from a restricted international zone left as an open world fact.

**Blue Island.** The accepted S-Rank Gate, cleared dagger-and-Far-Side-skills-only with Kane concealed alongside — a deliberate handicap that cost real Health for the first time all session. The boss restrained rather than killed, Overpower's uncapped grant doing real work against real resistance. **Kane's Standfast tested three ways**: broken outright by full-force Overpower (harder than raw stats alone predicted), pushed to redirect damage back through the field (fails on a structural rather than magnitude basis — Kane's own honest diagnosis), held clean against an ordinary manual strike. Boss released and finished by Kane's own hand — his first personal kill in years, core kept by agreement. Loot: 301 S-Rank crystals + 1 core filed to auction (no posted S-Rank price); flat fee + severity settled same-day, $5,500,000. Combined with the Jiu Valley kill, **LEVEL 171->172**. `REL-000107` deepens substantially — genuine flirtation, reciprocated; Alexander's offer to help Kane develop past Standfast's defensive-only design.

**The far side, a third visit.** Doorward ratifies (Sevrin's third qualifying scene) — six ratified techniques total now. Diagram Sense finally moves off its long stall. Four new candidates opened on request across all four teachers: Slip, The Bridge Stance, Read the Learner, Trace.

### Promotion barrier (`EVT-000908`)

`gatefall.skill_credit`: partial, credited at its originating scenes (Rupture/Flux Sight/Keen Sense/Exploit Pattern/Silent Step/Flash Step at Jiu Valley; Twin Fang at Blue Island) — this barrier owes nothing further. `gatefall.skill_formation`: two ratifications this span (Unwatched, Doorward), seven candidate advances, four new candidates opened — all recorded at their scene Events. **One continuity correction caught and fixed before promotion**: Unwatched had been narrated ratifying a second time; the character ledger reflects one ratification only. XP/Level 2,042/17,100 → **6,142/17,200, Level 171→172**. Unspent points 53 → **62**. Effective Stats STR 2930 / AGI 2675 / VIT 2845 / PER 2620 / INT 3022. Max Health 11,376→**11,380**, closing full after real damage taken and recovered. Max Mana 6,042→**6,044**, closing ~3,537, recovering. Cash $107,615,490.22 → **$113,115,490.22**; gold unchanged at 255,018,381. Pending payouts 1 → **2, ≈$259,800,000 estimated combined**. Beast Core [S-Rank] 147 → **148**. No canonical contradiction found.

**Repository allocation.** `EVT-000902`-`EVT-000908`; no new entity or relationship minted this span (`REL-000107` state-updated only; the twenty-eighth anchor is structural, not an identifier). ## Checkpoint 0144 — Suite 411's Captives, and the Far Door a Third Time

**Span:** `EVT-000914`-`EVT-000916` · **2026-09-11 11:20 -> ~15:35 -05:00.**

**Suite 411: seizure, interrogation, return.** Ingrid Solberg and Daniel Pruett seized clean from Suite 411 under Pre-empt and S-Rank Overpower — no contest, no roll owed — and travelling-stepped to the Denver facility. Interrogated concealed and voice-disguised, Keen Sense live the whole exchange: COMPLETE marks a maker proven independent of CONSULT, and D decides what happens next, with nobody marked COMPLETE ever heard from again; CONSULT itself is a controller with no filed name; Aldric Renn (`ENT-000268`, minted), Ingrid's own contact, met at Suite 3C, West Fulton Market. A genuinely novel combination — Overpower, Mend, Silent Step's `Unremembered` — was granted and attempted as a first qualifying scene toward a real candidate technique; a separately proposed Mana Bolt-based "knockout" was correctly flagged and abandoned before execution as outright lethal against an unawakened target. Both subjects returned unharmed to Suite 411, carrying no usable account of their captor but the entirety of what was said.

**The far door, a third time.** Opened at the Denver facility (2,500 Mana). Four teachers in one continuous expedition: **Tolo** (Diagram Sense ratifies; Appraise and Trace advance; Backread and What Is Left opened), **Sevrin** (The Bridge Stance advances; Closing Word, The Last Span, Bridgehold, and Counterseam opened), **Aumry** (Grip and Footing and Second Wind both ratify; Read the Learner advances; Nobody Falls Alone and Ten Thousand Repetitions opened), **Ilith** (Reading the Room and Slip advance; Second Skin opened) — nine candidates opened, three ratified, six advanced, the densest single skill-formation span of the campaign. The far-side drain (10%/hour, no recovery) ran the whole visit; a Greater Mana Potion on return restored the pool to afford closing the door. `far_side_notice` climbed from 27 to roughly 33 across the opening and the time it stood open; the unresolved third-threshold trigger (Section 4.3) remains flagged rather than invented around.

**Two corrections caught and fixed in play, both resolved honestly rather than argued from memory.** A claim that Silent Step erases a witness's memory was checked directly against `skills_known` rather than dismissed — the `Unremembered` category is real, ratified S-Rank canon, narrowly scoped to witnesses of Alexander himself. Separately, the Jiu Valley platform-access timeline was corrected: Alexander's own name belongs on BGM's review log beside Sorcha's, from the same officially cleared site visit — not Sorcha's alone, as first narrated (`OBJ-58` opened).

### Promotion barrier (`EVT-000916`)

`gatefall.skill_credit`: none — no genuinely dangerous scene this span. `gatefall.skill_formation`: full batch settled at `EVT-000916` — three ratifications (Diagram Sense, Grip and Footing, Second Wind), six candidate advances, nine new candidates opened. Mana closes at **3,539/6,044** (full trace at `EVT-000916`); Health unchanged, full, throughout. Unspent points unchanged at 66. Cash and gold unchanged; pending payouts unchanged at 1, ≈$138,400,000. Greater Mana Potion 45 → **44**. No canonical contradiction found.

**Repository allocation.** `EVT-000914`-`EVT-000916`; one entity minted (`ENT-000268`, Aldric Renn). No relationship minted — Renn holds no channel to Alexander yet. ## Checkpoint 0145 — The Pilsen Gate, a House on the Lake, and Two New Ambitions

**Span:** `EVT-000917`-`EVT-000923` · **2026-09-11 20:00 -> 2026-09-12 09:45 -05:00.**

**Dario Wexler, confirmed.** A text exchange settled the West Coast contact's meeting to Wednesday 2026-09-16, ~20:00, The Sidecut — `REL-000113` moves from loosely-scheduled to live/confirmed.

**A sharp argument with Owen, and a real pause.** Dinner turned when the `OBJ-12` succession disclosure was mischaracterized under pressure; Owen named it plainly and refused to be baited, then left the table hurt after a line about not being owed things "on a silver platter" landed on his oldest wound. Alexander left the apartment for the Rockwell bay and the Denver facility, carrying the bay's own furniture via the dimensional inventory (a stale entity-record field corrected in place). Slept there. **The next morning, by text, Alexander proposed a pause** — moving out until the relationship's status is decided, space given both directions, love affirmed on both sides. Owen corrected the "you left" framing once, agreed to the space, and went quiet as promised. Unresolved, open, left for the player to pick up.

**The 2026-09-12 06:00 world tick.** Escalation credit 7→8 (`GB-27` broke, deadline already passed). `GB-35` taken by the trade; `GB-34`/`GB-36` remain posted. One A-Rank Chicago gate (within-reach, archetype-twist-doubled) routed **NAMED** via Section 9.11 — BGM National decided Alexander was the answer before Renata Okonkwo-Vance dialed.

**The Pilsen Gate, cleared solo.** 48th daily completed first (streak 36, +4 AP, Daily Random Box → System gold +650,000). The NAMED assignment accepted cold by phone, before the location was even sent — and, once Alexander pointed out every prior National dispatch under the standing agreement has run solo, Renata dropped her own reflexive offer of a strike team and confirmed the same terms as always. Flooded Mine archetype, doubled twist; 16 A-Rank commons and 1 elite cleared without a genuine contest (the Rank gap leaves nothing uncertain to roll), the boss ended on one legendary Rupture strike. **Loot, corrected in place after a player-caught miss on the mineable deposit, both core drops, and the boss-drop roll**: 99 A-Rank crystals (17 beast-drop + 82 mined, Prospector included) and 2 A-Rank cores sold to USD via Renata directly, not the System shop — $8,200,000 total with the standing flat fee and severity. A-Rank Greatarm boss drop banked, off-build. XP +10,300, no level crossed. `OBJ-59` closed.

**The standing Red Gate auction clears.** The oldest pending payout on the books (open since `EVT-000896`) finally settles — 336 S-Rank crystals + 4 S-Rank cores, estimated $138,400,000, real auction result +9% over estimate on genuine bidding heat: **$150,856,000.** Pending payouts: 1 → 0.

**Two calls, two new ambitions.** Still in the Pilsen lot: Kesha Morrison on what founding a fifth Chicago guild — real structural leverage against Ironline's established corruption — would actually require (`OBJ-60` opened, she owes a callback); Renata Okonkwo-Vance on whether there's a real path to institutional standing with BGM National past incident-response (`OBJ-61` opened, she's taking it upstairs).

**A house on the lake.** With Owen's thread left open, Alexander went house-hunting on the North Shore through Delia Okonjo (outside her usual industrial lane) and bought the Winnetka lakefront estate outright — negotiated $17,500,000 down to $17,100,000, closed same-day. Five bedrooms, a bluff-edge pool, a boathouse sauna, direct Lake Michigan frontage. Not yet set as an anchor.

**Asset bookkeeping, no new Event.** Player-supplied portrait images placed for Ilith, Aumry, Orenne, Tolo, Sevrin, and updated Alexander/Owen portraits in the campaign ledger; Tereza Blažková, Reid Calloway, Lukas Reinhardt, Dominic Crane, and Elias Kane in the world-layer notable-figures file (a new `worlds/gatefall/assets/portraits/` folder, the first asset folder introduced there). Merged against an upstream repository pull that independently revised five of the same far-side figures' `appearance` fields the same session — real conflicts, resolved by keeping the revised canon text and re-adding the portrait fields alongside it. **Orenne's portrait is flagged stale** — generated against the pre-revision "pale" description, before the player's own deliberate change to a heavily muscled Black man; needs regenerating.
